### [1] [LeViT-UNet++: Towards Balanced Fine-Grained Details and Context for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22482)
*Guoping Xu,Jinling Zhu,Liuyue Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LeViT-UNet++高效编解码分割网络，通过Haar小波多尺度下采样模块与深度可分离卷积解码器，兼顾局部细节与全局上下文，在四个医学影像基准上实现精度-效率良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床场景（手术导航、放疗、实时引导介入）需快速准确的分割。CNN与Transformer虽进步显著，但在有限算力下难以兼顾精度；降低复杂度往往损失细粒度结构细节与全局上下文建模，导致不利的精度-效率权衡。

Method: 1) 提出LeViT-UNet++编解码架构；2) Haar Wavelet Multi-Scale Downsampling (HWMD)模块，将特征分解为低频与高频分量以提取多尺度结构信息，缓解下采样细节丢失；3) 基于深度可分离卷积的解码器，将Transformer块的全局多尺度表示与局部空间特征融合，实现渐进式特征细化并降低计算开销。

Result: 在Synapse、ACDC、LA2018、MSD Heart四个基准的2D与3D设置下评估，精度具竞争力，参数紧凑、推理快速。以Synapse为例：DSC 76.57%，仅8.025M参数，241.72 FPS。

Conclusion: 验证了小波多尺度特征提取与轻量局部-全局特征融合相结合在高效医学分割中的有效性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Efficient medical image segmentation is essential for clinical applications requiring fast and accurate delineation, such as surgical navigation, radiation therapy, and real-time image-guided intervention. Despite substantial advances in convolutional neural networks and Transformers, attaining high accuracy under limited computational resources remains difficult, because reducing model complexity often sacrifices fine-grained structural details and global context modeling, yielding an unfavorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. To address this, we propose LeViT-UNet++, an efficient encoder-decoder architecture that better balances local detail preservation and global context modeling. Specifically, we introduce a Haar Wavelet Multi-Scale Downsampling (HWMD) module that decomposes feature representations into low- and high-frequency components to extract multi-scale structural information, thereby mitigating detail loss during downsampling. We further develop a depthwise convolution-based decoder that integrates multi-scale global representations from Transformer blocks with local spatial features, enabling progressive feature refinement while reducing computational overhead. Evaluated on four benchmarks (Synapse, ACDC, LA2018, and MSD Heart) under both 2D and 3D settings, LeViT-UNet++ achieves competitive accuracy with a compact parameter footprint and fast inference. On Synapse, for instance, it attains a DSC of 76.57% with only 8.025M parameters and 241.72 FPS, confirming a favorable accuracy-efficiency balance. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining wavelet-based multi-scale feature extraction with lightweight local-global feature fusion for efficient medical segmentation. The source code is available at https://github.com/apple1986/levit_unet_pp.

</details>


### [2] [Depth-Aware Activation Steering via Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21655)
*Shilong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将可学习的跨层通路（AttnRes深度权重）与Jacobian-lens概念读出结合，提出一种轻量、免训练的深度感知激活引导流程，并在两个小模型上验证其内部对齐与行为改变均优于单层注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法通常在单一层注入向量，缺乏深度感知传播机制与内部验证手段，因而效果有限且难以诊断。

Method: 融合两项近期思想：AttnRes（提供可学习的跨层通路）与Jacobian-lens（J空间概念读出），构建深度感知引导流水线；按AttnRes式深度权重分配人格向量，在Qwen3-0.6B与Qwen3.5-0.8B上评估内部概念余弦/排名、行为偏移、符号特异性、随机方向对照、LoRA擦除实验与下游注意力JS散度；并以引导目标（200步、冻结主干）训练深度权重。

Result: 深度加权分布注入在深层概念余弦与排名（p<0.0001）及行为偏移上均强于单层注入；反向向量翻转全部六项指标，随机方向降低对齐（p<0.0001），短时LoRA微调可抹除印记；注入仅改变注入点下游注意力（上游JS=0.00000）。发现：注入层余弦提升123%来自算术效应，下游5–11层100%为动力学效应；强度窗口0.3–0.7单调，之后生成崩塌（617→306→25字符）；关键词规则指标未通过LLM评判验证（κ≤0.09），两项基于规则的结论被撤回；训练深度权重使留出提示的最终对齐提升10倍；跨人格向量保留96–99%效果。

Conclusion: 提出一种低开销、实用的深度感知激活引导方法，并配套严谨的评估协议（含指标效度检验）；特异性仅相对于随机方向成立，而非语义方向。代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Activation steering is a simple, training-free way to modulate LLM behavior, yet existing methods typically inject vectors at a single layer and lack mechanisms for depth-aware propagation or internal verification. We combine two recent ideas - Attention Residuals (AttnRes) providing learnable cross-layer pathways, and Jacobian-lens (J-space) concept readout - into a lightweight depth-aware steering pipeline. On two small open models (Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3.5-0.8B), distributing a persona vector according to AttnRes-style depth weights yields stronger internal alignment (deeper-layer concept cosine and rank, p<0.0001) and stronger behavioral shifts than single-layer injection. The effect is sign-specific: reverse vectors invert all six metrics, random directions degrade alignment (p<0.0001), and a short LoRA fine-tune erases the imprint. Injection rewires attention strictly downstream of the injection point (upstream JS = 0.00000). Three practical findings follow. First, a layer-wise geometric decomposition of cosine readouts: at the injection layer 123% of the increase is arithmetic, while 5-11 layers downstream 100% is dynamical. Second, an intensity window: effects are monotone over 0.3-0.7, then generation collapses (617 to 306 to 25 chars). Third, metric discipline: keyword-rule metrics fail LLM-judge validation (kappa <= 0.09), and two rule-based conclusions were retracted. Training the depth weights on a steering objective (200 steps, frozen backbone) raises final alignment 10x on held-out prompts. Cross-persona vectors retain 96-99% of the effect: specificity holds against random, not semantic, directions. We contribute a practical, low-overhead depth-aware steering method with a careful evaluation protocol. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [3] [Edge-Adaptive Hiera-Seg Model for Building-Integrated Photovoltaic Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21680)
*Na Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出边缘自适应HieraSeg模型，通过多朝向视觉-辐照度融合实现BIPV短期功率预测，并可解释预测证据来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIPV发电受立面朝向、屋顶几何、邻近建筑、植被、云移动、组件温度、逆变器状态与边缘设备可靠性等多因素耦合影响，传统预测方法难以兼顾。

Method: 将RGB立面图像、可选热红外线索、辐照度、组件温度、朝向、屋顶掩膜、阴影标签、天空状态、历史功率和边缘设备状态对齐为同步预测窗口；HieraSeg分层提取立面、屋顶、天空、阴影与背景区域，边缘自适应模块在丢帧、时延漂移和局部遮挡变化下调整特征权重；分割分支识别被遮挡组件区域并解耦各证据，再由预测头估计未来输出。

Result: 在模拟多建筑BIPV数据集上开展未见建筑与未见朝向测试，输出预测功率、置信区间、主导证据、遮挡贡献、朝向贡献及能量管理复核优先级。

Conclusion: 该框架可在通信时延与算力受限的边缘控制器上实现本地可追溯预测，将视觉遮挡、辐照动态与功率历史关联，服务于建筑能源管理、微电网调度与BIPV运维规划。

Abstract: Building integrated photovoltaic power forecasting is challenging because facade orientation, rooftop geometry, nearby buildings, vegetation, cloud movement, module temperature, inverter status and edge device reliability jointly affect power generation. This paper proposes an edge-adaptive HieraSeg model for short term BIPV power forecasting with multi-orientation visual-irradiance fusion. RGB facade images, optional thermal cues, irradiance measurements, module temperature, facade orientation, rooftop masks, shading labels, sky condition, historical power and edge device state are aligned into synchronous forecasting windows. HieraSeg extracts hierarchical regions from facade, rooftop, sky, shadow and background images, while the edge-adaptive module changes feature weights under missing frames, latency shifts and local shading changes. The segmentation branch identifies occluded module areas and separates facade, rooftop, sky and background evidence before the forecasting head estimates future BIPV output. Experiments are designed on a simulated multibuilding BIPV dataset with unseen building and unseen orientation tests. The model reports predicted power, confidence interval, dominant evidence, occlusion contribution, orientation contribution and energy-management review priority. This output is intended to show whether the forecast is driven by irradiance change, visible shading, orientation response, stale edge information or recent power history. The framework supports local forecasting at edge controllers with limited communication delay and computing resources, linking visual occlusion, irradiance dynamics and power history to traceable forecasting results for building energy management, microgrid scheduling and BIPV operation planning.

</details>


### [4] [EgoLink: Egocentric Language-Vision Interactive Network Knowledge Challenge Summary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21690)
*Renhe Sun,Yueying Feng,Bohan Yu,Tong Niu,Yunqi Liu,Zitong Wang,Haolin He,Kexin Shi,Hualei Wang,Qiuqiang Kong,Chang Yao,Yuge Huang,Weiqiang Wang,Jingyuan Chen,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoLink Challenge (ACM MM 2026) 提出了一个连接第一人称感知、社会推理与工具化交互动作的自我中心多模态智能基准，含"自我中心视频社会推理"与"交互式智能体服务任务"两个赛道，最佳系统分别取得 0.9263 准确率与 0.844 联合成功率/0.916 微准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦可见物体与活动识别，无法考察模型对情绪与社会线索的解读、视觉落地指代的消解，以及将用户请求转化为可验证的工具调用动作的能力。作者希望在同一框架下检验感知—推理—决策在离线问答与改变世界状态的交互中是否保持耦合。

Method: 构建双赛道基准：Track 1 为自我中心视频中的社会推理（多选题），Track 2 为通过对话、工具使用与闭环执行完成服务任务的交互式智能体。Track 1 采用多阶段数据流水线：MCQ 生成、思维链生成、自动质量分级、视频贡献度过滤。评估上结合客观答案打分与过程/结果两类评估（最终正确性 + 执行质量），并汇总任务设计、指标、规则、排行榜与代表性方案。

Result: 最佳 Track 1 系统达到 0.9263 准确率；最佳 Track 2 系统达到 0.844（联合成功率）与 0.916（微准确率）。参赛报告显示优胜系统共享同一原则：通过候选验证、选项鲁棒推理、场景感知规划、工具规范化以及回答/行动前的状态感知纠错，使中间证据显式化。

Conclusion: 过程验证与证据落地的推理是可靠自我中心多模态智能的关键；将答案正确性与执行质量联合评估的基准设计，能够更全面地刻画模型能力，并为后续研究提供可复用的数据构建与评测范式。

Abstract: The EgoLink Challenge at ACM Multimedia 2026 benchmarks egocentric multimodal intelligence linking first-person perception, social reasoning, and interactive action. Unlike benchmarks centered on visible objects and activities, EgoLink evaluates whether models can interpret emotional and social cues, resolve visually grounded references, and convert user requests into verified tool-mediated actions. Its two tracks cover social reasoning in egocentric video and interactive agents that complete service tasks through dialogue, tool use, and closed-loop execution. Together, they test whether perception, reasoning, and decision-making remain coupled under both offline question answering and state-changing interaction. We highlight a multi-stage Track 1 data pipeline for multiple-choice question (MCQ) generation, chain-of-thought generation, automatic quality grading, and video-contribution filtering. We summarize the task design, evaluation metrics, rules, leaderboards, and representative solutions. The benchmark combines objective answer scoring with process- and result-based evaluation, enabling analysis of both final correctness and execution quality. The best Track 1 system achieved 0.9263 accuracy, while the best Track 2 system achieved 0.844 joint success and 0.916 micro accuracy. Participant reports reveal a shared principle: strong systems make intermediate evidence explicit through candidate verification, option-robust reasoning, scenario-aware planning, tool canonicalization, and state-aware correction before answering or acting. These findings highlight the importance of process verification and evidence-grounded reasoning for reliable egocentric multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [5] [Video recordings and play behaviour annotations of group-housed dairy calves on two Dutch farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21706)
*Haiyu Yang,Heidi Lesscher,Enhong Liu,Miel Hostens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文公开了荷兰两家奶牛场的群养犊牛视频数据集，含651小时录像、104小时逐行为编码及110万帧解码帧与逐帧标注，用于犊牛行为与福利研究及自动行为识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玩耍行为被视为奶牛犊牛正向福利的指标，但公开可用的、带个体行为标注的群养犊牛视频十分稀缺，制约了相关行为学与福利研究以及自动化行为识别方法的发展与评估。

Method: 在两个农场（科研教学场2024、2025年，商业场2024年）的群养犊牛圈舍开展四次拍摄，每次约一周，共获1173个视频文件、651小时；由训练有素的观察者用Noldus The Observer XT按18项玩耍及其他行为的行为谱对8个观察日进行连续编码，共104小时，其中90小时覆盖圈内全部犊牛；并发布7个标注场次的110万解码帧、基于画面内相机时钟OCR读取的逐帧时间戳、逐犊牛分割轨迹和逐帧玩耍标签。

Result: 数据集包含651小时原始视频、104小时连续行为编码（90小时为全圈犊牛完整编码）、110万解码帧及分割轨迹与逐帧玩耍标签，形成多层级标注资源。

Conclusion: 该数据集可支撑犊牛行为与福利研究，并为自动化行为识别算法的开发与评测提供基准数据。

Abstract: Play is used as an indicator of positive welfare in dairy calves, but openly available video of group-housed calves with individual behaviour annotations is scarce. We describe video from four camera deployments of about one week each in group pens of dairy calves on two farms in the Netherlands: a research and teaching dairy farm (2024 and 2025) and a commercial dairy farm (2024). The recordings comprise 1,173 video files spanning 651 h. For eight observation days, trained observers coded calf behaviour continuously in Noldus The Observer XT with an 18-item ethogram of play and other behaviours, covering 104 h of video, 90 h of which have every calf in the pen coded. The release also contains 1.1 million decoded frames from seven annotated sessions, a per-frame timestamp read by optical character recognition from the camera clock burned into each frame, per-calf segmentation tracks and per-calf frame-level play labels. The data support studies of calf behaviour and welfare and the development and evaluation of automated behaviour recognition.

</details>


### [6] [Every Camera Grows Up: Camera-Height Regression in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21782)
*Sheng Lu,Guangpin Tao,Tong Lu,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示交互式视频世界模型存在"相机高度回归"失效：运动生成过程中相机会漂移到人眼高度（1.1–1.5 m），而非保持初始标定高度。作者提出EyeHeight基准（6种渲染高度+4种真实标定机位、无尺度多视角高度估计），发现六个开源模型在0.15 m起始下仅0–20%片段能保持在1.3×初始高度内，并追溯至以人为中心的视频先验。缓解手段（无引导采样+高度均衡微调）可部分恢复保持性，但损失约5 MUSIQ分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型被用作具身智能体的模拟器：给定起始图像与动作轨迹，生成智能体所见视角。起始图像固定了相机高度（如地面机器人0.15 m、俯视相机2.2 m），忠实模拟器必须在相机运动时保持该高度。然而现有模型是否具备这一性质尚未被系统检验。

Method: 构建基准EyeHeight：6种渲染高度（0.15–2.2 m）与4种真实标定机位，配合无尺度多视角高度估计；对6个近期开源权重模型执行180°转身行走轨迹测试保持性。用单应性跟踪地面运动，区分"表面几何一致但重建视点偏高"的现象；做消融分析：文本高度提示、稠密地面视差图、前半个去噪步的classifier-free文本引导；并采用无引导采样+高度均衡微调进行修复尝试。

Result: 六个模型均在180°转身行走中丢失初始高度：0.15 m初始化时仅0–20%片段落在初始高度的1.3×范围内，推断视点收敛到1.1–1.5 m的人眼高度，而真值渲染仅偏差数个百分点。回归随旋转角度增大，且在起始帧仍在上下文时即已出现；SANA-WM中360°转身回到初始视角时大部分可逆。Matrix-Game中单应性跟踪的地面运动在旋转时与恒定高度一致，但新外绘的房间几何给出更高的重建视点。文本高度线索与稠密地面视差图均无法阻止回归，前半个去噪步的CFG增强反而加剧。无引导采样+高度均衡微调可提升保持性，但损失5 MUSIQ分（该损失同样发生在早期步骤可被修复）。仅视点漂移即可改变视觉导航策略预测的路径。

Conclusion: 相机高度回归是当前视频世界模型的系统性缺陷，根源在于几乎不含地面级视角的以人为中心视频先验，而非缺乏显式高度条件。仅靠文本提示或几何监督无效；需从采样引导与数据分布层面（高度均衡微调）干预，且需注意画质代价。该缺陷会直接导致具身导航策略的路径预测偏移。

Abstract: Interactive video world models serve as simulators for embodied agents: from a starting image and an action trajectory, they generate the views the agent would see. The starting image fixes how high the camera is, for example 0.15 m for a floor robot or 2.2 m for an overhead camera, and a faithful simulator must keep this height as the camera moves. We show that six recent open-weight models fail to do so: as motion replaces the starting view with generated content, they move the camera toward human eye level, a failure we call camera-height regression. Our benchmark, EyeHeight, pairs six rendered elevations (0.15 m to 2.2 m) and four calibrated real-world rig heights with scale-free multi-view height estimation. All six models lose their initial height during a $180^\circ$ turn-and-walk rollout: initialized at 0.15 m, only 0-20% of clips end within $1.3\times$ of the starting height as inferred viewpoints converge to human heights of 1.1 m to 1.5 m, whereas ground-truth renders remain within a few percent. In Matrix-Game models, homography-tracked floor motion stays consistent with constant elevation during rotation even as newly outpainted room geometry yields a higher reconstructed viewpoint. Regression grows with rotation angle, begins while the start frame remains in context, and largely reverses in SANA-WM when a $360^\circ$ turn restores the initial view. We trace this pull to human-centric video priors in which floor-level views are nearly absent: in SANA-WM, textual height cues and dense floor-disparity maps do not prevent it, whereas classifier-free text guidance during the first half of denoising strengthens it. Combining unguided sampling with height-balanced fine-tuning recovers more retention but costs 5 MUSIQ points, a loss fixed during those same early steps. Viewpoint drift alone shifts the paths predicted by visual navigation policies.

</details>


### [7] [Data-Driven Variable-Length Compression of 3D Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21802)
*Yvette Y. Lin,Sai Karthikey Pentapati,Sarvesh Sundaram,Guandao Yang,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种学习式变长三维形状压缩方法，利用训练集统计结构，并通过快速前馈推理为每个形状自适应分配压缩表示长度，在网格与SDF上均改善率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维形状压缩方法存在两点局限：一是无法利用数据集层面的统计结构，二是对所有形状使用固定压缩率，忽略了不同形状复杂度的差异。二者共同导致压缩效率不足。

Method: 构建学习式变长压缩框架，作用于通用潜在表示；借助训练数据集跨样本结构建模，并用仅需快速前馈推理的机制，按单个形状复杂度自适应确定压缩表示大小，而非固定压缩率。

Result: 在网格（meshes）与符号距离场（SDF）两类表示上同时提升了率失真（rate-distortion）性能；压缩表示尺寸随形状复杂度自适应变化，且推理为快速前馈。

Conclusion: 数据集级结构利用与逐形状变长压缩可以兼得，且不牺牲推理速度；方法具有跨表示通用性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Compressing 3D shapes is important for the efficiency of many applications, yet existing methods either cannot leverage dataset-level statistics or use a fixed compression ratio for all shapes. We propose a learned variable-length compression method that exploits structure across a training dataset while adapting compressed representation size to individual shape complexity using only fast feed-forward inference. Our method operates on general latent representations and improves rate-distortion for both meshes and signed distance fields. Code is available at https://github.com/voilalab/varlen-3D.

</details>


### [8] [Sketch, Don't Paint: Allocating Test-Time Compute in Generative Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21812)
*Sheng Lu,Guangpin Tao,Tong Lu,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究多模态推理中"视觉思维"（像素级渲染）的测试时计算分配问题：何时该渲染、每个视觉思维需要多少去噪步数，并提出草图投票（sketch-and-vote）策略降低去噪开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交错式思维链模型通过合成像素（如在思维链中画迷宫路径、逐帧生成视频解）进行推理，但每个视觉思维都沿用来自照片级生成任务的完整扩散调度，迭代去噪主导了推理成本，因此需要研究测试时计算在"何时渲染"和"渲染多少步"两个维度上的最优分配。

Method: 通过只改变思维渲染方式的受控干预实验，识别出三类思维模式（因果性、装饰性、有害性）；再基于每个模型-基准对仅十个标注样本，在"直接作答/纯文本/视觉"三个层级间进行选择；在渲染有价值的场景提出 sketch-and-vote：用无标签选定的 8 步草图从独立噪声采样，直到某答案获得三票。

Result: 8 步草图准确率接近 50 步完整渲染，且其计划一致性与两次独立完整渲染之间的一致性相当；视频模型失败多因停滞未达目标而选择弃答；在 14 个模型-基准对上，直接作答在 9 对上最优，层级自适应选择将平均准确率从 60.7%（总是渲染）提升至 65.1%；sketch-and-vote 在图像推理器与两个视频推理器上以更少去噪步数超越完整渲染，VSP 空间规划提升 8.6 点，迷宫任务分别提升 9.6 与 22.1 点。

Conclusion: 视觉思维并非总是必要且应视模型与任务自适应分配测试时计算；早期低步数草图已足以承载计划信息，结合投票机制可在显著降低去噪成本的同时提升准确率，"总是画图"是次优策略。

Abstract: Multimodal models increasingly reason by synthesizing pixels, sketching maze paths within interleaved chains of thought or rolling out video solutions frame by frame. Each visual thought runs the full diffusion schedule inherited from photorealistic synthesis, so iterative denoising dominates inference cost. We study how to allocate test-time compute along two axes: when to render visual thoughts rather than thinking in text or answering directly, and how many denoising steps each thought requires. Controlled interventions that vary only how the thought is rendered uncover three regimes. Causal thoughts carry the plan and become legible early: an 8-step sketch nearly matches a 50-step render in accuracy, commits to the same plan as often as two independent full renders agree, and in video models mostly fails by stalling short of the goal, so the rollout abstains. Decorative thoughts can be omitted without loss, whereas harmful thoughts lower accuracy. Because the regime depends on both model and task, always drawing is suboptimal. Across 14 model-benchmark pairs of three interleaved reasoners, answering directly, without any thinking, wins 9 pairs, and choosing among direct, text-only, and visual tiers from ten labeled examples per pair raises mean accuracy from 60.7% (always drawing) to 65.1%. Where drawing pays, we propose sketch-and-vote, which samples 8-step sketches (a step count chosen without labels) from independent noises until one answer reaches three votes. With this one setting, sketch-and-vote beats full rendering with fewer denoising steps on an image reasoner and two video reasoners, by 8.6 points on VSP spatial planning and by 9.6 and 22.1 points on mazes.

</details>


### [9] [Rethinking Perceptual Similarity for Remote Sensing: Multi-Layer Attention Scoring and Human Preference Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21816)
*Eli Underwood,Marc Bosch,Neil Joshi,Jeffrey Wen,Myron Brown*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出遥感感知距离RSPD：基于RemoteCLIP分层对齐注意力响应的成对感知度量，在多种遥感生成任务的盲测基准上优于全球嵌入余弦距离并可比最强通用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像的感知相似度指标难以迁移到遥感图像：遥感中的严重地理空间与语义错误在视觉上仍可能显得合理，却对下游任务决定性错误，需要与人类感知对齐的专用度量。

Method: 1) 受控扰动实验，逐步加大常见合成扰动强度；2) 分析RemoteCLIP中各层对齐注意力分数(AAS)响应，发现不同遥感伪影在不同深度被最优捕获；3) 据此构建RSPD——对七个独立标定的层距离取平均的成对度量，不使用人类偏好标签调参；4) 构建盲测、参考条件2AFC基准（5个300三元组子集，覆盖合成伪影、机载/星载超分、SAR-to-光学转换、时序对照），并构建300三元组、标签确定的有序失配基准。

Result: 在相同RemoteCLIP检查点下，RSPD显著优于基于最终全局嵌入的简单余弦距离，并在真实遥感生成任务上与最强通用基线相当。

Conclusion: 分层聚合的遥感专用感知度量可有效对齐人类判断，为遥感生成图像质量评估提供可行方案；但相比通用最强基线尚无明显优势，仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Perceptual similarity metrics for natural images transfer poorly to remote sensing (RS), where severe geospatial and semantic failures can remain visually plausible while still being decisively wrong for downstream use. To address this, we develop a novel human-aligned perceptual quality metric for generated RS imagery and evaluate it across several generation tasks. We first conduct a controlled perturbation study where we progressively increase the severity of common synthetic perturbations. Using the layer-wise Aligned Attention Score (AAS) responses in RemoteCLIP, we find that different RS artifacts are best captured at different depths. Based on these findings, we introduce Remote Sensing Perceptual Distance (RSPD), a pairwise metric that averages seven independently calibrated layer distances without tuning on human-preference labels. To evaluate our metric on realistic RS generation tasks, we construct a blind, reference-conditioned two-alternative forced-choice (2AFC) benchmark with five 300-triplet subsets spanning synthetic artifacts, airborne and spaceborne super-resolution, SAR-to-optical translation, and timeseries controls. We also construct a 300-triplet ordered misregistration benchmark with deterministic labels. Using the same RemoteCLIP checkpoint, RSPD substantially outperforms simple cosine distance with the final global embedding and is competitive with the strongest generic baselines across the realistic RS generation tasks.

</details>


### [10] [Can a World Model Predict a Deepfake Video? A Consistency Margin Prior for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21846)
*Pradip Gupta,Shubham Mandloi,Ravi Kumar Satzoda,Sujeeth Bharadwaj,Yassine Benajiba,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于V-JEPA 2.1预测残差的Deepfake检测方法，仅训练8.25M参数即可在跨数据集上获得强泛化能力（FF++训练，Celeb-DFv2 AUC 90.5），并证明一致性边际的方向影响跨域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Deepfake检测器在同源数据集上表现良好，但跨数据集测试时性能显著下降，原因是过度依赖生成器特定伪影。作者探究：预训练视频预测模型能否提供跨数据集更通用的信号。

Method: 冻结V-JEPA 2.1编码器，用rank-16 LoRA适配预测器；将预测器输出与冻结编码器目标的差异定义为预测残差，并假设被篡改片段残差更大，作为一致性边际先验；用轻量Latent Consistency Head (LCH)将残差幅度与方向分离进行训练与分类；保持其他设置不变，对比标准与反向边际目标以检验方向对泛化的影响，并在FF++验证集上对齐两者性能。

Result: 反向边际设置在平均跨数据集泛化上差距更大，说明一致性边际方向影响迁移；完整模型仅新增8.25M可训练参数（占总334M的2.47%）；仅在FF++训练时在Celeb-DFv2上AUC达90.5，并在多个跨数据集基准上表现良好；消融显示分离幅度与方向、减少数据集特定变化及低秩投影均提升跨域泛化。

Conclusion: 自然视频预测残差可作为跨数据集鲁棒的Deepfake检测信号；一致性边际的方向对跨域迁移具有实质影响，且参数高效的LoRA适配配合残差幅度/方向分离设计可实现强泛化性能。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors often perform well on the dataset they are trained on, but their performance drops when tested on videos from different datasets. In this paper, we study whether a predictive video model can provide a signal that generalizes better across datasets. Instead of relying only on generator-specific artifacts, as many existing detectors do, we use a model trained to predict natural video and measure the difference between what it predicts and what it observes in a video. Using V-JEPA 2.1, we keep the encoder frozen and adapt the predictor using rank-16 LoRA. We define the difference between the predictor output and the frozen encoder target as the prediction residual. We hypothesize that manipulated clips produce, on average, larger prediction residuals than authentic clips, and use this as our consistency-margin prior. We process these residuals using a lightweight Latent Consistency Head (LCH), which separates residual magnitude from direction for training and classification. To test whether the direction of the consistency margin affects cross-dataset generalization, we compare standard and reversed margin objectives while keeping the remaining training setup fixed. We match the two settings on FF++ validation performance before evaluating them on unseen data. The reversed-margin setting exhibits a larger cross dataset generalization gap on average, providing evidence that the direction of the consistency margin affects cross-dataset transfer. Our complete model adds only 8.25M trainable parameters, which is 2.47% of the total 334M parameters. Trained only on FF++, it achieves an AUC of 90.5 on Celeb-DFv2 and performs well across several other cross-dataset benchmarks. Our ablations show that separating residual magnitude from direction, reducing dataset-specific variation, and using a low-rank projection improve cross-dataset generalization in our experiments.

</details>


### [11] [AR-WAM: efficient streaming world-action modeling for autonomous driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21851)
*Baiting Luo,Shatadal Mishra,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Nejib Ammar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AR-WAM 是一个高效的流式世界-动作模型，通过自回归时序建模与单次前向的未来条件化，兼顾未来预测与实时响应，在 NAVSIM-v1 上以单前视相机取得 91.5/94.0 PDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需在快速响应新观测的同时预判交通演化。视频基础模型具备预测能力，但重复编码历史与迭代式视频生成导致推理成本高，难以用于实际驾驶。

Method: 提出 AR-WAM：(1) 采用因果视频骨干，实现增量更新视觉上下文并复用历史计算；(2) 不用迭代生成，而以单次前向传播从近期观测得到未来特征，用于条件化轨迹生成；(3) 在监督训练后用强化学习直接优化驾驶奖励以改进策略。

Result: 仅用单个前视相机，NAVSIM-v1 上监督训练达 91.5 PDMS，强化学习后达 94.0 PDMS，优于对比的强端到端世界-动作模型；在连续驾驶的动态设定中保留视觉历史，优于强世界-动作基线。

Conclusion: 单次前向的未来特征条件化可在不做迭代视频生成的前提下提升动态交通下的泛化性，结合因果流式结构与强化学习，可实现高效且高性能的世界-动作驾驶模型。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires anticipating how traffic will evolve while responding quickly to new observations. Video foundation models offer the first ability, but repeated history encoding and iterative video generation make them costly to use for driving. We present AR-WAM, an efficient streaming world-action model that combines autoregressive temporal modeling with single-pass future conditioning. Because its video backbone is causal, AR-WAM updates its visual context incrementally, reusing past computation and conditioning future prediction on observed motion. We further hypothesize that future features computed from recent observations can improve generalization in dynamic traffic without iterative video generation. AR-WAM therefore obtains these features in one forward pass and uses them to condition trajectory generation, and reinforcement learning then improves the policy through direct optimization of driving rewards. With a single front camera, AR-WAM achieves 91.5 PDMS after supervised training and 94.0 after reinforcement learning on NAVSIM-v1, outperforming the compared strong end-to-end world-action models. In a dynamic setting of continuous driving, supervised AR-WAM keeps its visual history across decisions and outperforms strong world-action baselines.

</details>


### [12] [FaceRiff: Data-Efficient Unified Text-to-3D Face Asset Synthesis via Geometric Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21867)
*Elly Akhoundi,Mathieu Lamarre,Thibault Goyallon,Patrick Anderson,Étienne Danvoye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceRiff：基于SDXL与LoRA的高效文本驱动统一3D人脸资产生成框架，在共享UV空间直接输出网格、反照率与孔隙级位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、几何一致（UV对齐）且带物理基位移的人脸数据集稀缺；现有3D人脸生成方法速度慢、单模态或依赖大规模专用数据；大型图像扩散模型虽含强人脸先验，但无法原生输出光照无关纹理、孔隙级位移与UV对齐几何。

Method: 在预训练SDXL骨干上为各任务挂载专用LoRA适配器，以UV法线图为几何条件：逐实例法线引导反照率与位移合成，数据集平均法线为网格生成提供规范空间锚点；仅用约350身份、2,049次扫描，单GPU训练，每任务更新不足3%参数，无需逐实例优化。

Result: 相较FaceG2E与DreamFace，在保持提示对齐与固定拓扑UV一致性的同时具备更高单提示多样性；可生成光照无关反照率（支持PBR重打光）及前人缺失的孔隙级位移；45名业界专家盲测显示其真实感显著优于DreamFace，接近真实扫描。

Conclusion: 预训练扩散先验可在有限数据下被有效适配，实现统一的3D人脸资产合成。

Abstract: High-resolution facial datasets with consistent UV-mapped geometry, albedo, and physically based displacement are scarce, yet such assets are essential for photorealistic digital-human rendering. Existing 3D face generation methods are often slow, single-modality, or require large specialized datasets. Meanwhile, large image diffusion models encode strong facial priors but do not natively produce lighting-independent textures, pore-scale displacement, or UV-aligned geometry. We present FaceRiff, a unified, data- and parameter-efficient framework for generating complete 3D face assets (mesh, albedo, and displacement) directly from text in a shared UV space. FaceRiff adapts a pretrained SDXL backbone with task-specific LoRA adapters and uses UV normal maps as geometric conditioning: per-instance normals guide albedo and displacement synthesis, while a dataset-mean normal provides a canonical spatial anchor for mesh generation. Using only 2,049 scans from approximately 350 identities and training on a single GPU, FaceRiff updates less than 3% of the backbone parameters per task and produces render-ready 3D face assets without per-instance optimization. Compared with FaceG2E and DreamFace, FaceRiff produces greater per-prompt diversity while preserving prompt alignment and fixed-topology UV consistency, and additionally generates lighting-independent albedo suitable for PBR relighting and pore-scale displacement absent from prior text-driven baselines. A blind study with 45 industry professionals shows that FaceRiff achieves substantially higher perceived realism than DreamFace, approaching that of real captures. Overall, FaceRiff demonstrates that pretrained diffusion priors can be adapted to unified 3D face asset synthesis under limited data.

</details>


### [13] [Domain-Tuned Diffusion Super-Resolution for 4K Facial Asset Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21890)
*Elly Akhoundi,Mathieu Lamarre,Thibault Goyallon,Patrick Anderson,Étienne Danvoye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种领域定制的扩散上采样器，将面部资产贴图（反照率与置换）从1024分辨率4倍上采样至4096，通过SDXL+LoRA+Tiled ControlNet实现渲染级细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影视级面部资产需要4K反照率与置换贴图用于特写，但生成式流程通常只产出1K。直接套用照片超分效果差：面部UV贴图具有特定统计分布，而置换是标量高度场，其误差会直接扭曲几何。

Method: 从双线性上采样结果出发，对冻结的SDXL骨干按模态分别注入轻量LoRA模块进行领域适配；并用Tiled ControlNet在扩散先验恢复毛孔/皱纹级细节的同时保持输入结构。

Result: 在留出贴图上，相比双线性及现成GAN/扩散超分器，细节更锐利、更忠实；反照率LPIPS由0.21降至0.08，同时避免置换浮雕错位或过度。

Conclusion: 轻量级领域适配即可产出可直接用于渲染的4K面部资产贴图，无需定制超分架构。

Abstract: Physically based facial assets require 4K albedo and displacement maps for cinematic close-ups, yet generative pipelines commonly produce them at only 1K. Naively applying photographic super-resolution is a poor fit: facial UV maps have domain-specific statistics, and displacement is a scalar height field whose errors directly distort geometry. We present a domain-tuned diffusion upscaler for 4x (1024 to 4096) facial asset super-resolution. Starting from a bilinearly upsampled map, we adapt a frozen SDXL backbone per modality with lightweight LoRA modules, while a Tile ControlNet preserves input structure as the diffusion prior restores pore- and wrinkle-scale detail. On held-out maps, our method produces sharper, more faithful detail than bilinear and off-the-shelf GAN and diffusion super-resolvers, reducing albedo LPIPS from 0.21 to 0.08 while avoiding misplaced or excessive displacement relief. These results show that lightweight domain adaptation can produce render-ready 4K facial asset maps without a custom super-resolution architecture.

</details>


### [14] [Temporal Pseudo-RGB Encoding for Label-Free Detection of VEEV Infection in Human Brain Endothelial Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21892)
*Zhengjie Zhu,Stephanie Valdez Trefry,Hao Lu,Abbas Alili,Yongxin Guo,Aarthi Narayanan,Metin Nafi Gurcan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将三个时间偏移的明场帧堆叠为伪RGB图像，使ImageNet预训练CNN无需改结构即可建模感染时序；VEEV感染的HBMVEC上达91.6%准确率，优于单帧模型4.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标记病毒感染的检测对抗病毒筛选与病原监测至关重要，但多数深度学习方法仅分析单帧图像、不建模时间演进过程；作者希望在不对网络架构做修改的前提下，利用预训练CNN捕捉感染轨迹中的形态变化。

Method: 提出时间伪RGB编码：把t−6 h、t−3 h、t三帧明场图像分别作为R、G、B通道堆叠成单张图像，使形态变化转化为颜色信息，从而可用ImageNet预训练CNN训练。在感染VEEV TC-83的HBMVEC细胞上，用多任务网络一次前向同时预测感染/模拟状态与感染后时间；采用留一行交叉验证，三次实验共36孔全部折外评分，并与单帧模型、循环模型、3D卷积模型及ViT-B/16骨干对比。

Result: 伪RGB编码准确率91.6%，单帧模型87.4%（配对差+4.2个百分点，95% CI [2.5, 6.1]；符号翻转检验p=3×10⁻⁵；孔级推断，条件于三次实验）。在40–48 h晚期终点（悬浮细胞与碎片使常规明场图像难以判读）优势扩大至+23.9个百分点（95% CI [19.2, 28.9]）。固定行划分下感染后时间回归MAE=1.31 h（R²=0.975）。在测试的时间模型中平均准确率最高，成本远低于循环与3D卷积方案。

Conclusion: 伪RGB时序编码是一种低成本、免架构改动的高效时序建模手段，显著优于单帧分析且晚期优势更大；但其相对单帧模型的优势是唯一被统计分辨的差异，ViT-B/16骨干未见相应增益，因此结论限于卷积骨干与该病毒—细胞体系。

Abstract: Label-free detection of viral infection is central to antiviral screening and pathogen surveillance. Most deep-learning methods analyze individual frames and do not model temporal progression. We evaluate temporal pseudo-RGB encoding, which stacks three time-offset brightfield frames ($t{-}6$ h, $t{-}3$ h, $t$) as the red, green, and blue channels of a single image so that morphological change becomes color and an ImageNet-pretrained convolutional neural network (CNN) can be trained on infection trajectories without architectural modification. In human brain microvascular endothelial cells (HBMVEC) infected with Venezuelan equine encephalitis virus (VEEV) TC-83, a multi-task network predicts infected-versus-mock status and time post-infection in one forward pass. Across three experimental runs under leave-one-row-out cross-validation, with all 36 wells scored out of fold, the encoding reaches 91.6% accuracy against 87.4% for the single-frame model (paired difference $+4.2$ percentage points, 95% CI $[+2.5, +6.1]$; sign-flip $p = 3\times10^{-5}$; well-level inference, conditional on three runs). At the late-phase endpoint (40-48 h), where floating cells and debris can make conventional brightfield images difficult to interpret, the advantage widens to $+23.9$ points (95% CI $[+19.2, +28.9]$). Time post-infection is recovered on the fixed row split with MAE = 1.31 h ($R^2 = 0.975$). Among the temporal models tested the encoding has the highest mean accuracy, at a fraction of the cost of the recurrent and 3D convolutional alternatives, though only its margin over the single-frame model is resolved; the tested ViT-B/16 backbone showed no corresponding gain, so the finding is limited to convolutional backbones and this virus-cell system.

</details>


### [15] [REEL: Brain-Guided Video Generation and Selection by Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear Decoding from fMRI data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21907)
*Fabrizio Spera,Tommaso Boccato,Moein Khajehnejad,Nicola Toschi,Matteo Ferrante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出REEL（Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear decoding）框架：利用线性语义解码器、预训练视频生成器与三路神经编码流（岭回归读出、其中两路学习式池化），以贝叶斯启发的"生成候选—重排序"策略从fMRI信号重建视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态图像视觉脑解码进展显著，但视频重建仍困难，核心瓶颈在于快速变化的视频刺激与BOLD响应缓慢动态之间的时间失配。作者希望探索一种概念上更简单、且与复杂流程互补的策略。

Method: 在CC2017与BOLD Moments Dataset (BMD)上评估贝叶斯启发的proposal-and-rerank策略：线性语义解码器从fMRI预测语义嵌入，预训练视频生成器产生多个候选视频，再由三条神经编码流（岭回归读出，两流采用学习式池化）将候选映射回预测脑响应并进行重排序。

Result: 相较于仅生成单一候选，重排序使平均Video 50-way准确率在CC2017三位被试上由0.2359提升至0.2434，在BMD十位被试上由0.3653提升至0.3920。

Conclusion: 结果表明，重排序是一种可行且概念更简洁的视频脑解码改进途径，可作为日益复杂解码流程的互补方向。

Abstract: Visual brain decoding from functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data has made remarkable progress in recent years, driven by increasingly powerful artificial intelligence (AI) models and the growing availability of large-scale public datasets. However, despite the impressive advances achieved in static image decoding, the reconstruction of videos from fMRI signals remains challenging due to the temporal mismatch between rapidly changing video stimuli and the slow dynamics of the blood-oxygen-level-dependent (BOLD) response. We evaluate a Bayesian-inspired proposal-and-rerank strategy on CC2017 and the BOLD Moments Dataset (BMD), using a linear semantic decoder, a pretrained video generator, and three neural encoding streams with ridge readouts and learned pooling in two streams. We refer to this framework as REEL (Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear decoding). Compared with a single generated candidate, reranking increases mean Video 50-way accuracy from 0.2359 to 0.2434 across three CC2017 participants and from 0.3653 to 0.3920 across ten BMD participants. These findings suggest an alternative and conceptually simpler strategy for improving video brain decoding, highlighting the potential of our approach as a complementary direction to increasingly complex pipelines.

</details>


### [16] [Wrong Input, Wrong Threshold: Re-evaluating Adversarial Facial Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21923)
*Tunahan Parlayıcı,Erchan Aptoula,Yücel Saygın*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文揭示人脸隐私保护方法宣称的90%以上保护率源于评测协议的三重缺陷：输入前端不真实、度量饱和、阈值不匹配；主张改用配对余弦偏移来度量，并指出可微对齐与底库评分是可恢复的关键设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有面部隐私（反识别）方法普遍报告黑盒识别模型上超过90%的保护效果，但这一数字来自有缺陷的评测协议；作者希望证明这些高保护率是评测假象，并为该领域建立更可信的评测与训练范式。

Method: 指出三类协议缺陷并逐一量化：(1) 输入缺陷——2022年后方法将整张照片或检测框直接送入网络，而非部署系统的“检测—关键点对齐—裁剪”前端；(2) 度量缺陷——成功率之差会饱和，改为报告配对余弦偏移（两阈值代数相消）；(3) 阈值缺陷——沿用的四个常数在整图上使FAR偏离名义1%。此外分析失配成因为重采样不匹配，并通过可微对齐算子端到端优化验证。

Result: 经真实前端重评分，标准迁移攻击损失56%的躲避保护、预算分配攻击损失7%，且无需重新生成图像；成功率差随预算变化达8.2倍而底层效应仅1.34倍；配对余弦偏移在三类未见识别器上放大2.2倍与10.7倍，并在匹配预算下逆转L∞迁移排序（对齐方法+0.18，MI-FGSM −0.05）；可微对齐优化在未见识别器与商用系统上带来+24至+58点提升（其中一个仍有残差）；阈值在整图上误接受率达2.3–5.4%。

Conclusion: 现有面部隐私方法的保护率被显著高估，问题源于前端、度量与阈值三处协议缺陷而非扰动本身失效；恢复可微对齐与底库评分（第一代文献即采用）可大幅挽回损失。作者呼吁以配对余弦偏移等不受阈值校准影响的指标重评该领域。

Abstract: Published face-privacy methods report protection above 90% against black-box recognition models. That number comes from an evaluation protocol with two independent defects, reported in a measure that cannot size either one. The input is wrong: methods published since 2022 feed the network the whole photograph, or a detector's box, where deployed systems detect, align to a landmark template and crop. Re-scoring the same files through a realistic front end costs a standard transfer attack 56% of its dodging protection and a budget-allocating attack 7%, with no image regenerated. The measure is wrong: that loss is a difference of success rates, so it saturates, moving by 8.2x over a budget sweep while the effect beneath it moves by 1.34x. We therefore report the paired cosine shift, in which both thresholds cancel algebraically, so the input defect holds without committing to a calibration. Measured that way it is 2.2x and 10.7x larger on three unseen recognizers, and at matched budget it reverses the L-infinity transfer ranking: the one surveyed method that aligns at evaluation gains 0.18 where MI-FGSM loses 0.05. The cause is a resampling mismatch, not perturbation lost outside the crop; optimising through a differentiable alignment operator largely removes it, adding +24 to +58 points on unseen recognizers and a commercial system, with a residual on one of them. The thresholds are wrong: the four constants inherited since 2021 hold the false-accept rate near its nominal 1% on aligned crops but admit 2.3-5.4% on whole images, with an opposite-signed bias across dodging and impersonation, the two tasks reported side by side. Differentiable alignment and gallery scoring are not new: the first generation of this literature used both, and we show what dropping them cost.

</details>


### [17] [GridDriver: On-Board BEV-Based Language Planning and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21924)
*Katharina Winter,William Engel,Zain A. Alishammat,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GridDriver是一个高效的BEV条件化大语言模型框架，统一了轨迹预测、结构化驾驶指标、自然语言导航、驾驶解释与场景问答，并通过轻量时空BEV感知、空间结构化token及逐帧调度实现车载部署，在NAVSIM与nuScenes-QA上取得有竞争力表现，规划延迟32.7 ms、吞吐9.4 plans/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV条件化LLM可支持语言交互式驾驶，但车载部署要求在自回归语言生成的同时兼顾高效感知与连续规划，现有方案（如Q-Former压缩）存在几何信息丢失与效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出GridDriver框架：采用轻量级时空BEV感知模块与空间结构化token为共享LLM提供场景上下文；采用逐帧调度机制，将轨迹预测与异步语言生成交替执行；用单一共享checkpoint同时支持规划与问答任务；并构建ROS 2车载流水线，在自研采集数据与研究车辆上进行开环车载验证。

Result: NAVSIM上PDMS达87.9%，nuScenes-QA准确率57.6%（共享规划与问答checkpoint）；空间表征分析显示相较Q-Former压缩，几何信息保留与轨迹预测均改善；系统单独规划延迟32.7 ms，车载ROS 2流水线吞吐9.4 plans/s；并在真实采集数据上完成开环车载运行评估。

Conclusion: GridDriver在公开基准与真实场景中验证了BEV条件化LLM高效车载部署的可行性，并系统性地总结了该方法的优势与局限。

Abstract: Bird's-Eye-View (BEV)-conditioned large language models (LLMs) enable language-interactive driving, but onboard deployment requires efficient perception and continuous planning alongside autoregressive language generation. We present GridDriver, an efficient BEV-conditioned LLM framework combining trajectory prediction, structured driving indicators, natural-language navigation, driving explanations, and scene question answering. Lightweight spatio-temporal BEV perception and spatially structured tokens provide scene context to a shared LLM, while frame-wise scheduling interleaves trajectory prediction with asynchronous language generation. GridDriver achieves competitive performance on public benchmarks, reaching 87.9% PDMS on NAVSIM and 57.6% accuracy on nuScenes-QA with a shared planning and QA checkpoint. Spatial representation analyses demonstrate improved geometric information retention and trajectory prediction compared with Q-Former compression. The system achieves 32.7 ms standalone planning latency and 9.4 plans/s in an onboard ROS 2 pipeline. We further evaluate planning on our own data recordings and demonstrate open-loop onboard operation on a research vehicle. Across public benchmarks and real-world evaluations, we identify strengths and limitations of the BEV-based approach.

</details>


### [18] [Does the Model Use What It Cites? Counterfactual Tests of Grounded Reasoning Chains in Small Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21940)
*Jugal Gajjar,Kamalasankari Subramaniakuppusamy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"引用差"(cite gap)指标，通过遮蔽模型自生成推理链所引用的区域并与其同尺寸对照框比较，检验答案是否真的依赖被引用区域；在CLEVRER上引用差为0.089，NExT-QA上仅0.003，说明常规的"可验证"检查无法识别引用与答案依赖的脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对grounded reasoning chain的评估只检查每一步引用的帧跨度与框是否可验证（"模型看向哪里"），但"答案是否依赖被引用的区域"是另一个问题。作者希望在不依赖人工标注的前提下直接检验这种依赖关系，并揭示常规grounding检查的盲区。

Method: 提出干预式指标cite gap：将模型在自己推理链中引用的区域涂黑，统计正确答案翻转比例，并与在同一帧上涂黑同尺寸其他框作为对照，取配对差值。应用于2-3B视频-语言模型，分别使用精确CLEVRER链与伪标注NExT-QA链训练；额外做"在所有帧遮蔽被引用实体"的变体实验。

Result: 同一权重下cite gap在CLEVRER为0.089、NExT-QA为0.003；跨模型汇总，遮蔽被引用框使正确答案翻转数为对照的2.2倍（CLEVRER）与1.1倍（NExT-QA），在仅考虑依赖视频的答案时结论一致，且两数据集的框均通过grounding检查。按实体在所有帧遮蔽时翻转数为对照的1.9倍。

Conclusion: NExT-QA链被监督去引用在多数帧中持续可见实体的两帧，因而该结果符合监督预期，关键在于常规grounded chain检查无法察觉。引用可以既可验证又相关，但答案并不依赖它；检验grounded reasoning应移除链中所指称的对象，而不仅是指向的位置。

Abstract: A grounded reasoning chain cites a frame span and a box for every step, so a reader can check where the model looked. Whether the answer depends on the cited region is a different question. We test it by intervention: the regions a model cited in its own chain are blacked out, and the share of correct answers that flip is compared with blacking out boxes of the same size elsewhere on the same frames. The paired difference, the cite gap, needs no annotation. We apply it to 2-3B video-language models trained on exact CLEVRER chains and on pseudo-labelled NExT-QA chains. With the same weights, the cite gap is 0.089 on CLEVRER and 0.003 on NExT-QA. Pooled over models, masking the cited boxes flips 2.2 times as many correct answers as the control on CLEVRER and 1.1 times as many on NExT-QA, also among the answers that depend on the video at all, while the boxes pass a grounding check on both. Masking the cited entity on every frame, instead of the cited box, flips 1.9 times as many answers as its control. The NExT-QA chains were supervised to cite two frames of entities that stay visible on most frames, so this outcome is what the supervision predicts; the point is that the usual check on grounded chains cannot see it. A citation can be verifiable and relevant without the answer depending on it, and a test of grounded reasoning should remove what a chain names, not only where it points.

</details>


### [19] [Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Wheelchair Configuration Classification in Accessible Automated Vehicle Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21945)
*Samir Ahmed,Shaaz Saleem,Devin Dhami,Wesley Maia,Angel Martinez-Sanchez,Parthib Roy,Jannatul Ferdous Srabonee,Ahmed Sabbir Arif,Ross Greer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出轮椅配置图像数据集WCID（467张脸部模糊图像，192个采集组，仅用于评估），标注手动/电动二分类、轮椅子类型、占用情况、视角、场景、光照、遮挡、驱动方式与控制方式，并用手CNN骨干与冻结视觉语言探针评估跨数据集泛化与高斯模糊鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化车辆需兼顾包括轮椅使用者在内的弱势道路使用者；为提供个性化、支持独立出行的体验，车辆设计须适配多样化的轮椅配置。但轮椅高度个性化（车架几何、驱动方式、外观各异），乘员或附件还可能遮挡视觉线索；现有移动辅具数据集仅标注"是否存在轮椅"，未描述其具体配置。

Method: 构建WCID数据集：467张脸部模糊图像、192个采集组，划分为273张验证集与194张测试集，每张图像标注(a)手动/电动二分类、(b)轮椅子类型、(c)占用情况、视角、场景、光照、遮挡、驱动与控制。三个CNN骨干与一个冻结的视觉语言探针在外部分布数据集上训练，在WCID上评估跨数据集泛化性，并用高斯模糊测试鲁棒性。

Result: 卷积模型在清晰图像上表现良好，但在强模糊条件下性能显著下降，距实际部署仍有差距。

Conclusion: WCID填补了细粒度轮椅配置标注的空白，为评估跨数据集泛化与鲁棒性提供了基准；代码、划分、标签、训练权重与模糊图像均已开源；未来需提升退化条件下的鲁棒性方可走向部署。

Abstract: Making automated vehicles accessible requires consideration of vulnerable road users, including wheelchair users. To offer a more tailored experience that supports independence for this group, vehicle design needs to accommodate the wide range of wheelchair configurations. However, wheelchairs are individualized, varying in frame geometry, propulsion, and visual appearance. Moreover, occupants or accessories may obscure visual cues. Existing mobility-aid datasets only label the presence of a wheelchair, rather than describing its specific configuration. To fill this gap, this paper introduces the Wheelchair Configuration Image Dataset (WCID), comprising 467 face-blurred images across 192 capture groups solely for evaluation. Each image is annotated with (i) a two-class label of manual or powered, (ii) a chair subtype, (iii) occupancy, viewpoint, setting, lighting, occlusion, propulsion, and control, with a 273-image validation split and 194-image test split. Three convolutional neural network backbones and a frozen vision-language probe are trained on an external image dataset and evaluated on WCID to measure cross-dataset generalization, while also testing robustness using Gaussian blur. Convolutional models do well on clean images, but performance drops under strong blur, leaving a gap before deployment. Code, split assignments, labels, trained weights, and the face-blurred images is available at: https://github.com/Mi3-Lab/MAID

</details>


### [20] [SCAN: Site-Aware Collaborative Privacy-Preserving Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for Neurological Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21955)
*Nikhil J. Dhinagar,Rishab Pendam,Harmony S. Cen,Sophia I. Thomopoulos,John P. John,Mahir H. Khan,Sook-Lei Liew,Erik Kaestner,Carrie McDonald,Paul M. Thompson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCAN：站点感知协作适配框架，让新站点在不重训、不共享脑MRI扫描的前提下适配3D医学视觉-语言模型，并通过差分隐私保护的原型摘要实现跨站点增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球神经影像数据可增强诊断基础模型，但隐私法规阻碍脑MRI扫描的中心化汇聚。

Method: 各站点保留精确类原型用于本地预测，并共享经一次校准噪声扰动的范数有界求和与计数（隐私保护类摘要）；冻结VLM，无需重训。基于12国67站点7,641例扫描（AD、卒中、癫痫及对照）评估本地适配、跨站点收益及对既有站点的影响。

Result: 7个未见站点上冻结VLM零样本AUC：AD与卒中0.833–0.945；免训练本地原型将3个站点癫痫AUC由0.598/0.625/0.508提升至0.684/0.729/0.762，某站点AD由0.833升至0.863，接近站点本地训练分类器（平均AUC 0.757 vs 0.759）。ε=4差分隐私下新站点可贡献且既有站点AUC变化≤0.2%，但共享增益有限；成员推断攻击接近随机水平。

Conclusion: SCAN为医院提供无需交换扫描或重训即可适配与共享脑MRI VLM的全球框架，隐私保护下实现协作适配，不过跨站点共享的收益仍有限。

Abstract: Worldwide neuroimaging data could strengthen diagnostic foundation models, but privacy rules often prevent centralizing brain MRI scans. We present SCAN, a site-aware collaborative adaptation framework for Neurological Disease Classification that lets new sites adapt a 3D medical vision-language model (VLM) without retraining or sharing patient scans. Each site keeps exact class prototypes for local prediction and can share privacy-protected class summaries: norm-bounded sums and counts perturbed once with calibrated noise. Using 7,641 scans from 67 sites across 12 countries, covering Alzheimer's disease (AD), stroke, epilepsy and controls, we assess local adaptation, cross-site benefits, and effects on existing sites. At seven unseen sites, the frozen VLM's zero-shot AUCs were 0.833-0.945 for AD and stroke. Training-free local prototypes improved epilepsy AUC at three sites from 0.598/0.625/0.508 to 0.684/0.729/0.762 and AD AUC at one site from 0.833 to 0.863, nearly matching a classifier trained at each site (mean AUC 0.757 vs. 0.759). With differential privacy ($ε$ = 4), new sites contributed without materially reducing accuracy at existing sites (AUC change $\leq$0.2%), although gains from sharing were limited, and membership attacks performed near chance. SCAN offers hospitals a worldwide framework to adapt and share brain MRI VLMs without exchanging scans or retraining.

</details>


### [21] [How Many Persistent Features Does a Biometric Need? A Law Linking Equal Error Rate to Feature Count and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21973)
*Oleg Komogortsev,Mehedi Hasan Raju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文推导出生物特征识别中EER与特征数、持久性之间关系背后的解析规律：EER=Φ(−½√N·atanhρ)，并扩展到持久性可变情形下存在不可突破的EER下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期实证工作只是用直线把等错误率（EER）与特征持久性、所需特征数联系起来，缺乏理论依据。作者希望推导这些经验规律背后的解析定律。

Method: 设N个独立标准化高斯特征，测试-重测相关为ρ，用余弦相似度打分，经Fisher z变换把真实与冒犯分数化为两个正态分布，导出EER∝Φ(−½√N·atanhρ)及反解N=(2q/atanhρ)²；相关特征用有效特征数N_eff替代。进一步让持久性在比较间变化，引入Fisher尺度上的离散度τ，得到EER=Φ(−m/(√(τ²+v)+√v))，v=1/(N_eff−2)。

Result: 无需拟合参数，简单定律复现了已发表仿真与EER-ICC关系线，并预测GazeBase眼动嵌入的KCC-EER关系（R²=0.88，GazeBaseVR上0.63）；一般形式在101种条件（眼动、击键、ECG、人脸）上R²=0.98、中位误差0.6个百分点，简单定律为0.87和1.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 当持久性服从正态分布时，EER存在下限Φ(−m/τ)，增加特征数无法降低；乘积τ√(N_eff−2)给出设计规则：该值远低于1时增加或去相关特征完全有效，超过约3后作用甚微，此时只能靠更高或更均匀的持久性改善。

Abstract: A biometric designer controls the number of features and their temporal persistence. Earlier empirical work linked the equal error rate (EER) to persistence and to the required number of features by straight lines. We derive the law behind these regularities. For $N$ independent standardized Gaussian features with test-retest correlation $ρ$, scored by cosine similarity, Fisher's $z$-transform turns the genuine and impostor scores into two normals, giving $\mathrm{EER}=Φ(-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{N}\,\operatorname{atanh}ρ)$ and, inversely, $N=(2q/\operatorname{atanh}ρ)^2$ with $q=Φ^{-1}(1-\mathrm{EER})$; intercorrelated features enter through an effective count $N_{\mathrm{eff}}$. The central extension lets persistence vary across comparisons with spread $τ$ on Fisher's scale: $\mathrm{EER}=Φ(-m/(\sqrt{τ^2+v}+\sqrt{v}))$ with $v=1/(N_{\mathrm{eff}}-2)$, which for a normal spread has a floor $Φ(-m/τ)$ that no number of features can lower. With no fitted parameter the simple law reproduces published simulations and the EER-ICC line, and predicts the KCC-EER relation of eye-movement embeddings on GazeBase ($R^2=0.88$), though less well on GazeBaseVR ($0.63$). Read from the genuine and impostor scores, the general form matches the EER of eye movements, keystroke dynamics, the ECG and face embeddings over 101 conditions with $R^2=0.98$ and a median error of $0.6$ points, against $0.87$ and $1.9$ points for the simple law. The 61 eye-movement, keystroke and ECG conditions check consistency on the same scores; the 40 face conditions use held-out subjects, with the same accuracy. The product $τ\sqrt{N_{\mathrm{eff}}-2}$ gives a design rule: adding or decorrelating features pays fully while it is well below one and little once it exceeds about three, where only higher or more uniform persistence helps.

</details>


### [22] [Pathology Randomization for Synthetic Priors and Zero-Shot Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21975)
*Alexandra G. Roberts,Tonmoy Hossain,Mark Olchanyi,Stefan Brandstätter,Jonathan Williams Ramirez,Ana Lawry Aguila,Matthew Rosen,Annabel Sorby-Adams,W. Taylor Kimberly,Juan Eugenio Iglesias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathoDraw：以"病灶标签图→对比无关渲染"的合成MRI生成器，仅用合成病理训练单一3D UNet，实现零样本跨对比度、跨疾病的临床脑MRI异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院脑影像档案含大量真实病理、覆盖少数群体与罕见病，但缺乏标注；现有合成数据方法多限于健康解剖，临床任务仍需真实患者数据、对比度专用模型或疾病专用生成器，难以扩展到未整理的临床数据。

Method: 提出PathoDraw：先对健康解剖施加病灶的条件分布得到标签图，再经对比无关渲染生成多对比度MRI；单一网络从任意输入扫描中估计健康解剖并输出精确的带符号强度偏差。对比无关3D UNet直接学习病灶下的健康标签与带符号偏差，结合稳健组织统计与学到的特征识别病理。

Result: 在无任何真实病理训练的前提下，于公开卒中、肿瘤、白质高信号数据集及归档临床数据上实现零样本检测，覆盖T1w、FLAIR、T2w及多分辨率，遵循近期无监督异常检测基准协议；单模型超过或媲美用真实扫描训练的对比度专用检测器，并保持健康解剖完整。

Conclusion: 同一生成器还可训练超分辨率与含病理配准网络；模型跨采集与疾病稳健，可支持未整理的归档数据用于临床分析。

Abstract: Hospital brain imaging archives hold a diverse array of pathology at sample sizes beyond prospective studies, particularly for underrepresented populations and diseases. To unlock uncurated clinical data and scale analysis, methods are needed to detect abnormalities across contrasts and resolutions. Synthetic data has provided robust, contrast-agnostic models for a variety of human neuroimaging tasks in healthy anatomy, but pathology detection and other tasks in clinical settings still require real patient data, contrast-specific models, or disease-specific generators. We propose PathoDraw, a generative model of brain MRI with pathology. A conditional distribution of lesions given the healthy anatomy is drawn into the label map before contrast-agnostic rendering, and a single network estimates healthy anatomy with exact signed intensity deviation of any input scan. The contrast-agnostic 3D UNet learns the healthy labels under the lesion and the signed deviation directly and identifies pathology via robust tissue statistics and learned features, despite no training on real pathology. We show zero-shot detection on public stroke, tumor, and white matter hyperintensity datasets as well as archived clinical data, spanning $T_1$w, FLAIR and $T_2$w at various resolutions under a recent unsupervised anomaly detection benchmark protocol. Our single model exceeds or performs comparably to contrast-specific anomaly detectors trained on real scans and keeps healthy anatomy intact. The same generator trains networks for super-resolution and for registration in the presence of pathology. Our model, robust across acquisition and disease, enables the use of uncurated, archived data for clinical analysis. Our code is available at https://github.com/agr78/Pathology-Randomizer.

</details>


### [23] [DEFTS: Data-Efficient, Training-Free Few-Shot Learning for CLIP in the Text Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21993)
*Yijin Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DEFTS：一种仅在文本子空间内运行的数据高效、免训练的小样本分类方法，通过将估计的图像原型投影到文本嵌入张成的线性子空间并求解少量贝叶斯系数，缩小图文模态鸿沟，在12个基准上显著优于现有免训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP少样本方法多依赖图像嵌入、忽视文本嵌入的语义结构；原始文本嵌入直接作分类器虽次优，但其张成的线性子空间具有强判别性，值得加以利用。

Method: DEFTS：将估计的图像原型投影到文本子空间以缩小模态鸿沟；把原型表示为文本嵌入的线性组合，将高维估计问题降维为少数系数的求解；系数由贝叶斯后验均值计算，先验以原始文本嵌入坐标为自然中心。

Result: 在12个图像分类基准上的大量实验表明，DEFTS显著优于现有免训练小样本方法。

Conclusion: 文本子空间蕴含充分判别信息，将其作为少样本适配的紧凑参数化空间可在免训练、数据高效条件下有效提升CLIP少样本分类性能。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) achieves impressive zero-shot image classification performance by comparing image embeddings against class-specific text embeddings. While recent few-shot methods leverage labeled training samples to improve upon this zero-shot baseline, they often predominantly rely on image embeddings and underutilize the semantic structure of the text embeddings. In this paper, we introduce DEFTS, a data-efficient, training-free few-shot classification method strictly in the text subspace. We first demonstrate empirically that while raw text embeddings can be suboptimal as direct classifiers, the linear subspace they span remains highly discriminative. DEFTS therefore proposes to reduce the image-text modality gap by projecting estimated image prototypes directly into this subspace. Importantly, by expressing these prototypes as linear combinations of text embeddings, DEFTS reduces a high-dimensional estimation problem to the derivation of just a few coefficients. These coefficients are then computed via a Bayesian posterior mean, using a prior naturally centered on the original text embedding coordinates. Extensive experiments across 12 image classification benchmarks demonstrate that DEFTS significantly improves upon existing training-free methods.

</details>


### [24] [Deciding Without Pointing: A Case Study of Whether a Decision Model Localizes the Anomalies It Detects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21995)
*Hsiang-Hung Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以Clef-flash（后训练Qwen3.5-9B骨干）在工业检测基准上做案例研究，考察决策模型在“检测到异常”时是否同时“定位到异常”。结论是：零样本下检测性能不均衡，决策头的路由注意力几乎不具备定位能力，骨干注意力虽有指向缺陷但对问题措辞与文本上下文高度敏感，且定位能力远不及专用少样本定位器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型可以针对图像回答类型化问题并给出每个选项的概率，而无需生成文本，这类模型在工业检测中日益重要。但一个关键问题尚未被检验：当模型判定存在异常时，它究竟是否在图像中定位到了该异常？本文旨在系统性地回答这一“检测—定位一致性”问题。

Method: 采用案例研究法：以Clef-flash（后训练Qwen3.5-9B骨干）为对象，在MVTec AD与MVTec AD 2上进行零样本检测与定位评测。手段包括：（1）按类别计算图像级AUROC；（2）从决策头的路由注意力导出逐图像素AUROC图；（3）通过遮蔽图像token进行消融，检验检测是否依赖视觉证据；（4）分析多模态LLM骨干注意力相对中心先验与随机水平的表现；（5）改变问题措辞、移除文本上下文以测试鲁棒性；（6）用正常图像做线性探针检验骨干隐状态是否编码位置；（7）对比更大模型Clef（后训练Qwen3.8-27B骨干）；（8）尝试区域化提问、重训决策头、读取骨干等补救方案，并与专用少样本定位器对比。

Result: （1）零样本检测不均衡：MVTec AD上按类别图像AUROC为0.924，MVTec AD 2上仅0.668。（2）在MVTec AD上，决策头路由注意力的逐图像素AUROC均值仅0.391；将图像token从决策头中遮蔽，检测结果不变，说明决策头未使用视觉定位证据。（3）骨干注意力确实指向缺陷，但更换问题后其表现低于中心先验，移除问题中的文本上下文后甚至低于随机水平，部分原因是骨干注意力离开目标物体；而决策头的定位图几乎不变。（4）探针实验显示骨干隐状态在正常图像上携带位置信息，但在测试集图像上决策依赖于缺陷区域。（5）更大模型Clef的决策头同样给不出有用的定位图。（6）区域化提问、重训决策头、读取骨干隐状态均可部分恢复异常位置，但均不及专用少样本定位器。

Conclusion: 决策模型的“检测”与“定位”之间存在显著解耦：决策头给出高置信判断时并不依赖视觉定位证据，骨干注意力虽含定位信息却对问题表述与文本上下文高度敏感、脆弱不稳，规模增大也未改善。可通过区域化提问、重训决策头或读取骨干表征部分恢复定位能力，但均无法匹敌专用少样本定位器；这提示决策式多模态模型在需要可解释定位的工业检测场景中仍存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Decision models can answer a typed question about an image with a probability per option, without generating text. In our case study of Clef-flash (post-trained Qwen3.5-9B backbone) on industrial inspection benchmarks, we ask whether it localizes the anomalies it detects. We obtained uneven results with zero-shot detection: per-category image AUROC 0.924 on MVTec AD and 0.668 on MVTec AD 2. On the former, the decision head's routing attention produces 0.391 mean per-image pixel AUROC, and hiding the image tokens from it leaves detection unchanged. The multimodal LLM's backbone attention does point at the defect, however it falls below a center prior when we change question. It falls below chance if we remove textual context in the question, partly due to backbone attention leaving the object, while the head's map hardly changes. The backbone's states carry the location when probed with normal images, on the test-split images, however, the decision depends on the defect region. A larger model, Clef, that comes with a post-trained Qwen3.8-27B backbone, gives no useful map in the decision head either. We found that it's possible to recover anomaly location by asking question specifically on an image region, retraining the head or reading the backbone, but none matches dedicated few-shot localizers.

</details>


### [25] [Beyond Appearance: Where Forest Visual Place Recognition Actually Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21996)
*Blake Matheson,Ben McShanag,Isabella Moffat,Michael Milford,Peyman Moghadam,Dana Kulić,Somayeh Hussaini,Kavindie Katuwandeniya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在WildCross森林基准上以MegaLoc为外观基线，系统评估时序、语言、结构、几何四类上下文表示对VPR的增益，发现仅分数空间序列匹配带来稳定提升，其余上下文无一致改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林环境中重复植被、光照/季节/视角变化导致不同地点外观相似、同一地点外观差异大，纯外观描述子难以可靠识别，作者希望检验多种上下文表示能否改进外观基线，并明确各自失效的边界。

Method: 以MegaLoc为外观基线，在WildCross基准上对比：分数空间与描述子空间序列匹配、视觉-语言描述与重排序、场景图表示、以及基于深度（LiDAR）的树干图；测试场景涵盖同向重访、延长路线与反向穿越。

Result: 同向重访下两种序列匹配Recall@1相近（94.1% vs 91.5%）；100%精度下召回由单帧MegaLoc的50.9%升至分数空间71.6%，而描述子空间降至36.5%。其余上下文表示均未稳定超越外观基线。

Conclusion: 时序聚合（尤其分数空间）是当前最有效的上下文形式；语言描述缺乏地点特异性、重排序受限于初始候选列表、树干图即便有LiDAR深度仍表现弱，说明高区分度上下文表示在森林VPR中仍未解决。

Abstract: Visual place recognition (VPR) in forest environments remains challenging because repetitive vegetation, and changes in illumination, season, and viewpoint can make distinct places appear similar and revisited places appear substantially different. In this paper, we investigate whether temporal, linguistic, structural, and geometric context can improve on an appearance-only descriptor, and what limits each representation when it does not. Using MegaLoc as the appearance-based baseline on the WildCross benchmark, we evaluate score-space and descriptor-space sequence matching, vision-language captions and reranking, scene-graph representations, and depth-derived stem graphs across same-direction revisits, extended routes, and reverse traversals. On a same-direction revisit, both sequence matching strategies reach similar Recall@1 (94.1% vs. 91.5%), but recall at 100% precision rises from 50.9% (single-frame MegaLoc) to 71.6% in score space and falls to 36.5% in descriptor space. The remaining context representations provide no consistent improvement over the appearance-based baseline. Same-place captions show limited place-specific discrimination, reranking is constrained by the initial candidate shortlist, and stem-graph matching remains weak even with LiDAR depth.

</details>


### [26] [Where Should the Lamp Go? Ambient-Aware Lamp Placement for Portraits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22005)
*Yuyan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightMatch：一种基于人类偏好的逆向光照框架，先学习Bradley-Terry质量模型，再通过感知头从冻结自监督特征回归球谐光照，最后利用球谐线性性分离可控光源并优化其可达位置，用于"开拍前灯该放哪"的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光照估计与肖像重打光均在拍摄后编辑光照，无法在拍摄前指导灯具摆放位置，因此"灯应放在何处才能最好地照亮人脸"仍是开放问题。

Method: 1) 用成对人类判断学习Bradley-Terry质量模型；2) 感知头通过交叉注意力从冻结的自监督特征回归球谐光照；3) 放置求解器利用球谐线性性分离可控光与环境光，并优化其可达位置。

Result: 在留出环境上达到oracle改进的87.8%，相对忽略环境光的传统求解器提升28.4%，相对使用DPR估计器的同流程提升70.5%；在未见身份的真实OLAT人脸上达73.2%；真实拍摄中灯方向误差降低37.7%。

Conclusion: 偏好驱动的逆向光照可有效解耦环境光并优化可控光源位置，在合成与真实场景均显著优于基线，为拍摄前布光提供可量化指导。

Abstract: Portraits shape first impressions, and lighting is an important factor. Prior work, such as lighting estimation and portrait relighting, edits lighting after capture but does not guide where to place a light before the shot. This leaves an open question: where should a light go to best light a face? Therefore, we propose LightMatch, a preference-grounded inverse lighting framework that disentangles the controllable light from ambient illumination and optimizes its placement. We first learn a Bradley-Terry quality model from pairwise human judgments. A perception head then regresses spherical-harmonic lighting from frozen self-supervised features via cross-attention. Finally, a placement solver isolates the light via spherical-harmonic linearity and optimizes its reachable position. On held-out environments, LightMatch attains 87.8% of the oracle improvement, outperforming the conventional solver that ignores ambient light by a relative 28.4% and the same pipeline with the widely used DPR estimator by a relative 70.5%. It reaches 73.2% on real OLAT faces of unseen identities and, on our real captures, cuts lamp-direction error by 37.7%.

</details>


### [27] [BATMoS: Budget-Aware Guidance Allocation for Efficient Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22028)
*Zhixu Du,Kuo Yang,Jingwei Sun,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BATMoS，在扩散去噪轨迹上按幅度时间规则在“新鲜CFG”与“缓存引导”间路由，无需训练路由器或逐预算阈值搜索，用更少分支评估取得接近完整CFG的VBench总分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型的推理预算效果取决于计算在去噪轨迹上的分配位置；八种等预算配置的VBench总分相差4.80分，说明显式分配设计很重要。作者观察到：引导评估在扩散时间上价值不等，保留采样更新而选择性复用引导即可在更少去噪器评估下保持质量。

Method: BATMoS（Budget Aware Temporal Mixture of Strategies）：仅用单一预训练去噪器，在新计算的classifier-free guidance与缓存引导之间路由；采用基于幅度的时间规则，将引导复用分配给低幅度块，其余块保留新鲜引导；由单个整数控制计算预算，无需额外路由器训练或逐预算阈值搜索。

Result: 在CogVideoX-2B上，每配置4,720个视频、覆盖全部16个官方VBench维度：BATMoS在八种分配策略中总分最高，用少20%分支评估达到距完整CFG仅0.10分；与特征复用组合可将计算从80降至62次有效分支评估，总分仅下降0.11分。

Conclusion: 时间维度的引导分配是从固定推理预算中提取更多生成质量的实用机制。

Abstract: The effectiveness of a video diffusion model's inference budget depends on where computation is spent along the denoising trajectory. Across eight equal-budget inference configurations, VBench total scores span 4.80 points, highlighting the importance of explicit allocation design. Our key insight is that guidance evaluations are not equally valuable across diffusion time: retaining the sampling updates while selectively reusing guidance can preserve generation quality with fewer denoiser evaluations. We introduce BATMoS (Budget Aware Temporal Mixture of Strategies), which routes between fresh classifier-free guidance and cached guidance using a single pretrained denoiser. A magnitude-based temporal rule assigns guidance reuse to low-magnitude blocks and reserves fresh guidance for the remaining blocks, with a single integer controlling the computation budget. The resulting schedule requires no additional router training or per-budget threshold search. Evaluated on CogVideoX-2B with 4,720 videos per configuration across all sixteen official VBench dimensions, BATMoS achieves the highest total score among the eight allocation policies and comes within 0.10 points of full classifier-free guidance using 20\% fewer branch evaluations. BATMoS also composes with feature reuse, reducing computation from 80 to 62 effective branch evaluations with a 0.11-point decrease in total score. These findings make temporal guidance allocation a practical mechanism for extracting more generation quality from a fixed inference budget.

</details>


### [28] [Data-Efficient Algal Bloom Segmentation in Freshwater Bodies Using a Three-Stage Transfer Learning Pipeline with Attention-Enhanced Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22064)
*Albert Ansah Benson,Mingqiang Han,Rahul Harsha Cheppally,Treman Singh Kaloya,Menard Soni,Sudan Baral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Swin Transformer U-Net(SCSE解码注意力)迁移学习流水线，从ImageNet→水体分割(RIWA、LaRS)→藻华分割，并配合模型辅助标注将藻类语料从145扩至655张；语料扩展贡献最大(+11 mIoU)，测试mIoU 0.919、藻类IoU 0.902，但近重复审计提示训练/测试存在重叠、结果偏乐观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有害藻华威胁水质与公共健康，但淡水影像中藻华区域的自动分割受限于像素级标注稀缺。

Method: Swin Transformer U-Net + SCSE解码器注意力；分阶段迁移学习：ImageNet-1K → 水体分割(RIWA、LaRS) → 藻华；模型辅助标注协议扩充语料；41张验证集上单种子消融(检查点S2-A4及变体)；与9个基线在256张测试集上对比，并做近重复审计。

Result: 语料扩充为主导因素(+11 mIoU)；水体预训练仅在扩充语料上有效(+1.1)；SCSE +0.3；可学习位置编码 -0.6 故弃用；新增476张(188张无藻)使非水体场景假阳性面积由22.6%降至4.9%；S2-A13达mIoU 0.919、藻类IoU 0.902。近重复审计显示该测试划分存在训练/测试重叠，分数可能偏高。

Conclusion: 数据扩充与域内预训练是淡水藻华分割性能的主要驱动力，但当前评测划分存在数据泄漏风险，所得高分需在去泄漏划分上重新验证。

Abstract: Harmful algal blooms threaten water quality and public health, yet automated segmentation of bloom regions in freshwater imagery is limited by scarce pixel-level annotations. We present a transfer-learning pipeline that adapts a Swin Transformer U-Net with SCSE decoder attention from ImageNet-1K to water-body segmentation (RIWA, LaRS) and then to algal blooms, together with a model-assisted annotation protocol that expands the labelled algae corpus from 145 to 655 images. In a single-seed ablation on a 41-image validation split (checkpoint S2-A4 and its variants), corpus expansion is the dominant factor (+11 mIoU points); water-body pretraining helps only on the expanded corpus (+1.1 points over ImageNet initialization); SCSE attention adds 0.3 points; and a learnable positional encoding lowers mIoU by 0.6 points and is omitted. Adding 476 images, 188 of them algae-free, reduces false-positive area on non-water scenes from 22.6% to 4.9%. Against nine baselines on a 256-image test split, a separately trained checkpoint of the same architecture (S2-A13) reaches mIoU 0.919 and the highest algae IoU (0.902); a near-duplicate audit indicates train/test overlap in this split, so these scores are likely optimistic.

</details>


### [29] [Segmentation-Guided Direct Capacity Estimation of Rooftop Photovoltaics from Aerial Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22076)
*Guibin Chen,Emma Fan,Yuzhang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Direct Head：以 U-Net 分割与软掩码池化为引导，直接从 PV 区域特征与面积描述子回归非负容量，避免容量受"掩码面积×功率密度"乘积约束，在 7,590 个法国屋顶装置上显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网规划需要安装级屋顶光伏容量清单，但互联记录往往不完整或空间聚合。传统基于图像的方法先分割 PV 阵列、将掩码转为物理面积、再乘以估计功率密度，使容量预测被绑定在掩码面积上，误差会沿该乘积链累积。

Method: Direct Head 为分割引导架构：保留 U-Net 分割分支与软掩码池化，在 PV 区域特征和面积描述子上直接回归非负容量。实验设置三组独立运行，覆盖 7,590 个法国屋顶装置，采用原始划分与基于省份的留出划分两种评估协议，并与参数匹配的面积—密度神经网络、传统估计器、受控架构变体及辅助损失消融进行对比。

Result: 相对参数匹配的面积—密度神经模型，Direct Head 在两种协议下平均绝对误差分别降低 8.7% 与 12.5%，对称平均绝对百分比误差分别降低 14.0% 与 20.7%；案例与消融结果进一步显示分割提供有效空间证据，但容量无需由面积—密度乘积决定。

Conclusion: 分割与容量回归可以解耦：将分割作为空间证据输入而非面积换算的中间量，能提升安装级容量估计精度并增强跨空间留出的泛化性。

Abstract: Distribution planners need installation-level inventories of rooftop photovoltaic (PV) capacity, but interconnection records may be incomplete or spatially aggregated. Conventional image-based estimators segment PV arrays, convert the mask to physical area, and multiply by an estimated power density, coupling capacity prediction to mask-derived area. We propose Direct Head, a segmentation-guided architecture that retains U-Net segmentation and soft-mask pooling while directly regressing nonnegative capacity from PV-region features and area descriptors. We evaluate three independent runs on 7,590 French rooftop installations under the original split and a department-based holdout. Relative to a parameter-matched area-density neural model, Direct Head reduces mean absolute error by 8.7% and 12.5%, and symmetric mean absolute percentage error by 14.0% and 20.7%, respectively. Comparisons with conventional estimators, controlled architecture variants, auxiliary-loss ablations, and representative cases show that segmentation provides useful spatial evidence without requiring capacity to be determined by an area-density product.

</details>


### [30] [When AUC Meets Generative Reconstruction: A Unified Principle for Balanced Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22094)
*Boyu Han,Qianqian Xu,Shilong Bao,Zhiyong Yang,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将生成式重建重构为生成输出流形内的判别排序问题，并给出AUCGR与pAUCGR两个即插即用目标，缓解对比学习与重建目标组合时的梯度冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型需要既能提取细粒度视觉特征、又具语义解耦与判别性的视觉编码器。对比学习通过压制类内变化提升类间判别，生成式重建强调感知保真但缺乏显式比较监督；用复合损失简单聚合二者，会在共享隐空间中产生梯度干扰，导致优化冲突与不稳定。

Method: 统一表示学习范式：将生成式重建重写为生成输出流形上的判别排序问题，保留感知重建作为监督信号，同时对匹配/不匹配重建输出进行系统排序，把判别信息嵌入生成过程。具体提出AUCGR，通过保证匹配对得分高于不匹配对来优化ROC曲线下面积(AUC)；提出pAUCGR，聚焦可靠特征子空间内的不匹配比较以缓解监督偏差。目标函数即插即用，无需改动现有生成式重建框架结构。

Result: 理论分析建立了与AUC/pAUC优化的严格联系并给出泛化保证；大量实验表明方法同时提升判别准确性与细粒度感知表示质量。

Conclusion: 以排序形式表达判别约束可避免复合损失的竞争问题，使判别信号与生成重建自然融合，且具备理论支撑与架构无关的通用性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) require visual encoders that extract fine-grained visual features while constructing semantically disentangled and discriminative representations. Conventional methodologies encounter a fundamental trade-off: contrastive learning enhances inter-class discrimination by suppressing intra-class variability, whereas generative reconstruction emphasizes perceptual fidelity but lacks explicit comparative supervision. The naive aggregation of these objectives via composite loss functions often induces optimization conflicts and instability due to gradient interference within the shared latent space. To resolve this, we introduce a unified representation-learning paradigm that reformulates generative reconstruction as a discriminative ranking problem within the generative output manifold. Our approach maintains perceptual reconstruction as a supervisory signal and integrates a ranking-based discriminative constraint by systematically ordering matched and mismatched reconstruction outputs. This formulation embeds discriminative information seamlessly into the generative process, alleviating the risk of loss competition. Specifically, we propose AUC Generative Reconstruction (AUCGR), which optimizes the area under the ROC curve (AUC) by ensuring higher scores for matched pairs than for mismatched pairs, and Partial AUC Generative Reconstruction (pAUCGR), which addresses supervision bias by focusing on mismatched comparisons within reliable feature subspaces. The proposed objectives are designed to be plug-and-play, requiring no architectural modifications to existing generative reconstruction frameworks. Theoretical analysis establishes rigorous connections to AUC/pAUC optimization and provides generalization guarantees. Extensive experiments validate that our method advances both discriminative accuracy and fine-grained perceptual representation.

</details>


### [31] [CueSplat: Learning to Schedule Sparse Deformation for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22095)
*Qingyuan Tang,He Zhu,Shixuan Sun,Anbang Wu,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CueSplat 是一个后训练（post-training）加速框架，利用高斯轨迹的局部时间可预测性，冻结预训练 4DGS 主干，用轻量学习路由器按可靠性+渲染贡献挑选需精确形变更新的高斯，并以漂移信号触发周期性全形变帧重建预测历史，在边缘设备上实现最高 8.15× 加速且质量几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于形变的 4DGS 需在每帧对全部高斯评估形变网络，在边缘设备上计算开销巨大。作者观察到高斯轨迹通常具有局部可预测性，但预测可靠性随高斯个体和时间变化，因此存在按需选择精确更新的空间。

Method: 1) 冻结预训练主干、保留其高斯集合的后训练框架；2) 由近期历史外推预测高斯属性，并用轻量学习路由器在离线校准的场景特定预算内选择需精确形变更新的高斯，选择同时考虑预测可靠性与渲染贡献；3) 选中索引在邻近帧复用以降低路由开销，但被选高斯每帧仍获精确更新；4) 用漂移信号调度连续三帧全形变更新，重建预测历史以抑制累积误差。

Result: 在四种主流 4DGS 算法上评估，Jetson AGX Orin 与 Orin NX 上分别实现最高 6.74× 与 8.15× 加速，数据集平均 PSNR 最多仅下降 0.16 dB。

Conclusion: 通过将局部时间可预测性与预算受限的精确更新选择相结合，可在保持渲染质量的前提下显著降低 4DGS 在边缘设备上的推理成本，且框架具有跨算法的通用性。

Abstract: Deformation-based 4D Gaussian splatting (4DGS) enables dynamic scene rendering. However, evaluating deformation networks for all Gaussians at every frame is compute-intensive on edge devices. We find that Gaussian trajectories are often locally predictable, yet prediction reliability varies across Gaussians and time. Thus, we propose CueSplat, a post-training framework that exploits local temporal regularity while keeping the pretrained backbone frozen and retaining its Gaussian set. Specifically, CueSplat predicts Gaussian attributes from recent history and uses a lightweight learned router to select exact deformation updates within a scene-specific budget calibrated offline. Selection accounts for prediction reliability and rendering contribution. Selected indices are reused across nearby frames to reduce routing overhead, while selected Gaussians still receive exact updates each frame. To control accumulated prediction error, we use drift signals to schedule three consecutive full-deformation frames that rebuild the prediction history. Evaluated across four mainstream 4DGS algorithms, CueSplat achieves up to $6.74\times$ and $8.15\times$ speedups on Jetson AGX Orin and Orin NX, respectively, with a negligible quality drop (at most $0.16$ dB in dataset-average PSNR).

</details>


### [32] [Diagnosing and Correcting Skin-Tone Color Casts in Ultra-Lightweight Photo Retouching Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22106)
*Choulhyouc Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示了超轻量级修图网络在合成自监督训练下会产生“皮肤偏绿”色偏（非原位恢复皮肤色度且自身引入色相偏移），全帧PSNR/SSIM无法察觉。作者提出零参数的逐像素、皮肤掩膜、非对称绿过量损失，将该色偏从70张肖像中52张降到1张，且不损害域内指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 色偏通常被认为来自场景或相机，修图模型负责去除；但作者发现模型本身可能残留或引入色偏。特别地，合成自监督（对高质量图像加退化再学习恢复）这一无需配对数据的常用配方会静默地产生偏绿肤色，而全帧PSNR/SSIM几乎不变，现有图像级指标无法检测。

Method: 采用以真值为锚、不做白平衡的评估协议量化色偏；在代表性的6,825参数直方图条件网络（及CNN条件网络）上对比合成自监督与MIT-Adobe FiveK专家配对训练。提出逐像素、皮肤掩膜、非对称的绿过量损失（不增加参数），仅针对带符号的绿-品红轴进行约束。

Result: 色偏在70张肖像中的52张出现；全局均值绿色损失反而恶化（52→57）。所提损失将色偏降至1/70（分层肤色；FFHQ 70k图上68.1%→2.2%；留出CelebA-HQ 64.4%→0.8%）。FiveK域内PSNR/SSIM基本不变（+0.13 dB，+0.004），代价是全帧CIEDE2000小幅上升（8.77→9.69），以及近中性皮肤区域的品红过校正（skin Δa* −1.5→+4.4，CIEDE2000 5.6→6.4）。该损失在三种超轻量架构上均有效；全帧绿色指标与方法级皮肤CIEDE2000相关（Spearman ρ=0.89）。最终模型可导出为35 KB的TFLite。

Conclusion: 合成自监督可系统性诱发皮肤偏绿色偏而逃逸图像级指标；针对绿-品红带符号轴的皮肤掩膜非对称损失能在零额外参数下有效抑制色偏，但存在近中性肤色品红过校正的权衡。模型可部署为35 KB TFLite。

Abstract: Color casts are expected to come from the scene or camera, and a retouching model removes them. We report a case where the model leaves one: an ultra-lightweight retouching network renders human skin greener than the original because it under-restores the skin chroma that a degradation removes and, even on clean inputs, adds a small hue shift of its own. A common paired-data-free recipe, synthetic self-supervision (degrade high-quality images, learn to restore them), silently produces this cast while whole-frame PSNR/SSIM stay essentially unchanged. In a representative 6,825-parameter histogram-conditioned network, the cast appears in 52 of 70 portraits under a ground-truth-anchored, white-balance-free protocol. Both a histogram-conditioned and a CNN-conditioned network develop the cast under synthetic self-supervision, yet both are near-neutral in-domain when trained on MIT-Adobe FiveK expert pairs. Image-level objectives miss it: a global-mean green loss worsens the cast (52/70 to 57/70). With zero added parameters, our per-pixel, skin-masked, asymmetric green-excess loss reduces the cast to 1/70 across stratified skin tones (68.1% to 2.2% over the 70,000-image FFHQ corpus; 64.4% to 0.8% on held-out CelebA-HQ photos). In-domain FiveK PSNR/SSIM are unchanged (+0.13 dB, +0.004), at the cost of a small whole-frame CIEDE2000 increase (8.77 to 9.69) and a magenta over-correction where skin is nearly neutral (skin Delta a* -1.5 to +4.4 and CIEDE2000 5.6 to 6.4 on clean inputs): the loss targets the signed green-magenta axis, not total skin-color fidelity. The same loss suppresses the cast across three ultra-light architectures. A whole-frame green metric correlates with skin-region CIEDE2000 at the method level (Spearman rho = 0.89). The final network exports to a 35 KB TFLite model.

</details>


### [33] [RetiDual-Select: Reliability-Aware Specialist-Generalist Routing for Retinal OCT under Image-Quality Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22117)
*Zihao Han,Philippe De Wilde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RetiDual-Select 将静态度量融合（校准 PoE）、门控候选、验证集冻结的 harm guard 与独立复核评分分离，使自适应融合仅在图像质量造成可靠性机会时替换静态参考，否则回退或转人工复核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应融合虽可修复静态集成（static ensemble）的错误，但也可能推翻原本正确的决策，因此需要一种可控的自适应机制，在利用融合增益的同时限制风险。

Method: 以视网膜专用模型 RETFound 与医学视觉语言模型 MedSigLIP 的图像编码器构成双分支；以校准的 product-of-experts（PoE）融合为静态参考；小型 gate 为每张图像提出候选替代；由验证集冻结的 harm guard 决定候选能否替换参考；另一独立评分用于优先安排复核。评估覆盖 3 个数据集与 15 个预设合成扰动条件。

Result: 静态 PoE 在三个数据集上均取得最高或并列最高的干净准确率。在 2% 接受错误目标下，复核策略对 Kermany-4 接受 94.3% 图像、选择性准确率 99.89%；OCT-C8 接受 92.4%、选择性准确率 98.65%。两分支均正确率分别为 95.0% 与 91.8%，仅恰有一支正确的样本占 4.4% 与 6.2%。自适应增益集中在严重对比度降低条件，此时路由机会扩大约九倍；百分位重标定后可基本消除该效应，提示其源于动态范围压缩。事后仅验证集分析中，gate 跟随更可靠分支时增益更高；冻结 guard 将最差条件下 OCT-C8 的损失从 1.39 降至 0.36 个百分点。

Conclusion: 当图像质量造成可靠性机会且 gate 能跟随该机会时，自适应路由才有价值；否则系统保持 Static PoE 或转交复核。结论受限于所评估的合成扰动，尚不能证明对自然采集或扫描仪漂移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Adaptive fusion can repair a static ensemble's errors, but can also overturn correct decisions. RetiDual-Select combines retinal specialist RETFound with the image encoder of the medical vision-language model MedSigLIP, separating candidate fusion, routing safety and predictive review. Calibrated product-of-experts (PoE) fusion is the static reference, a small gate proposes an alternative for each image, a validation-frozen harm guard decides whether that candidate may replace the reference, and a separate score prioritizes review. Static PoE reached the highest or joint-highest clean accuracy on all three datasets. At a clean 2% accepted-error target the review policy accepted 94.3% of Kermany-4 images at 99.89% selective accuracy, and 92.4% of OCT-C8 at 98.65%. Both branches were correct on 95.0% and 91.8% of clean images; only 4.4% and 6.2% had exactly one correct branch. Across 15 prespecified synthetic conditions, adaptive gains were concentrated in severe contrast reduction, where this routing opportunity widened roughly ninefold; percentile rescaling largely removed the effect, linking it to compressed dynamic range. In post-hoc validation-only analyses, gain was higher when the gate followed the more reliable branch, and a frozen guard cut the worst-condition OCT-C8 loss from 1.39 to 0.36 percentage points. Adaptive routing was useful when image quality created a reliability opportunity and the gate followed it; otherwise the system retained Static PoE or deferred for review. The findings are bounded by the synthetic perturbations evaluated and do not establish robustness to natural acquisition or scanner shift.

</details>


### [34] [FrameSeg3D: Learned Local Frames for Segmenting Small Parts on High-Resolution 3D Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22124)
*Xinwen Zhang,Tongyan Wang,Huawei Lin*

Main category: cs.CV
