TL;DR: 提出FrameSeg3D：先从低分辨率全局扫描预测每个小部件的局部坐标系（中心+朝向），再沿其法向以扫描仪原生分辨率近距离分割，使一个分割网络可通用于指甲、牙齿等不同部件，并在手扫与Teeth3DS+上取得最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率3D扫描上的小部件（指甲、牙齿）分割面临三重困难：部件占扫描面积极小、其边界仅表现为高度与曲率的细微变化、且各自朝向不同。现有方法分两类且均有缺陷：整体处理类（点/网格网络需降采样以适配内存，小部件仅剩少量点；多视图法渲染整物体，部件仅占几像素且倾斜部件被从侧面观察）分辨率不足；先定位再近距离分割类方法依赖特定部件的先验知识（如牙弓形状），难以迁移到其他部件。

Method: 作者指出精度瓶颈在于"部件被如何观察"（观察方向与尺度）而非落在其上的点数。FrameSeg3D分两阶段：(1) 从低分辨率全局视图为每个部件预测局部坐标系，即中心与朝向；(2) 在围绕其朝向法向的视图中，以扫描仪原生分辨率对部件进行分割。由于每个部件都被居中、正对相机且归一化到固定尺度，单一分割网络即可服务不同种类部件，并能从少量标注掩码中学习。

Result: 在指甲与牙齿两类部件上测试：手部扫描指甲的平均Dice达0.970，超过现有最优方法；在Teeth3DS+测试折上单颗牙齿IoU为96.5，边界IoU为70.5，边界精度最优。

Conclusion: 通过"先定位局部坐标系、再沿法向近距离高分辨率分割"的框架，解耦了覆盖全扫描与保持分辨率之间的矛盾，并以任务无关的归一化视图实现跨部件泛化与小样本学习，验证了观察方式（方向与尺度）是决定小部件分割精度的关键因素。

Abstract: Segmenting small parts on high-resolution 3D scans, such as nails on hand scans and teeth on intraoral scans, poses three challenges: 1) each part covers a tiny fraction of the scan, 2) its boundary is only a subtle change of height and curvature, and 3) it faces its own direction. Existing methods fall into two groups, and both fall short. The first group processes the whole scan at once and sees each part too coarsely. Point and mesh networks must shrink the scan to fit in memory, so a small part keeps only a few of its points. Multi-view methods render images of the whole object, so each part fills only a few pixels and a tilted part is seen from the side. The second group first finds each part and then segments it up close. But these methods find the parts with knowledge about one kind of part, for example the shape of the dental arch for teeth. Such knowledge does not carry over to other kinds of parts. We find that accuracy is limited by how a part is seen, from which direction and at what scale, not by how many points land on it. We therefore present FrameSeg3D. It first predicts a local frame for every part, its center and the direction it faces, from a low-resolution view of the whole scan. It then segments the part in views around its frame normal at the scanner's resolution, so covering the whole scan no longer costs resolution. Because every part arrives centered, facing the camera, and at a fixed scale, one segmentation network can serve different kinds of parts and learn from few labeled masks. We test FrameSeg3D on two kinds of parts, nails and teeth. It outperforms state-of-the-art methods on hand scans (mean Dice 0.970 on nails) and on the Teeth3DS+ test fold (per-tooth IoU 96.5), with the most accurate tooth boundaries (boundary IoU 70.5).

</details>


### [35] [Text-Guided Modality Correction for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22126)
*Yu Quan,Dong Zhang,Ping Gong,Peng Huang,JinHui Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Text-Guided Modality Correction Network（TMCNet），将文本引导融入区域特征提取、跨模态对齐与多尺度解码，提升开放词汇目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇目标检测方法对目标区域的文本引导不足、跨模态对齐策略有限、层级互补特征利用不充分，限制了新类别和不同尺度目标的检测能力。

Method: TMCNet包含三部分：(1) 文本引导模态编码器，将文本语义注入query以引导区域特征聚合，降低表示语义歧义；(2) 跨模态对齐模块，通过视觉与文本特征交互强化区域表示与类别语义的对应；(3) 渐进式文本校正解码器，结合自顶向下路径与横向连接，逐级融合文本引导的深层语义特征与对应尺度浅层视觉特征。

Result: 在COCO-OVD和LVIS-OVD两个公开数据集上，仅以简单文本查询为引导即取得较强性能；扩展至开放词汇分割任务亦获得性能提升。

Conclusion: TMCNet通过多阶段文本引导有效增强区域-类别语义对应与跨层级特征融合，兼具跨任务适用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) is essential for detecting objects beyond the categories seen during training. Establishing vision-language associations is crucial for recognizing unseen categories, while integrating complementary visual features across levels supports detection at different scales. However, existing methods often provide insufficient textual guidance for object regions, rely on limited cross-modal alignment strategies, and underuse complementary features across levels, limiting their ability to detect unseen categories and objects of varying scales. To address these limitations, we propose a Text-Guided Modality Correction Network (TMCNet) that incorporates textual guidance into region feature extraction, cross-modal alignment, and multi-scale decoding to strengthen region-category semantic correspondence and promote complementary feature fusion across levels. First, a text-guided modality encoder incorporates textual semantics into queries to guide regional feature aggregation, reducing semantic ambiguity in object representations. Second, a cross-modal alignment module strengthens the correspondence between region representations and category semantics through interactions between visual and textual features. Finally, a progressive text correction decoder combines a top-down pathway with lateral connections to progressively fuse text-guided deep semantic features with shallow visual features at the corresponding scales, preserving both semantic information and spatial detail for objects of different sizes. Experiments on two public datasets, COCO-OVD and LVIS-OVD, demonstrate that TMCNet achieves strong performance in open-vocabulary object detection using simple text queries as guidance. Extending TMCNet to open-vocabulary segmentation also yields performance gains, demonstrating its cross-task applicability and extensibility.

</details>


### [36] [CEVA: Complementary Evidence Verification via Frame-Level Reorganization for Short-Video Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22141)
*Wenshen Yang,Junfeng Hao,Jing Li,Decheng Liu,Yangkunfei,Shuyu Jiang,Haizhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CEVA：首个在融合前按证据角色组织帧的短视频假新闻检测框架，通过DBCR分离显式对齐帧与互补上下文帧，在FakeSV/FakeTT上取得最佳综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频成为公众追踪事件的常见渠道，其快速、视觉说服力强的形式也助长假新闻传播。与图文帖不同，短视频新闻交织多模态与多场景，难以确定哪些帧能验证主张。现有检测器要么对采样帧同质池化，要么按文本-帧相似度选帧；二者都忽视了对齐帧可能只是重复误导性主张，而不占主导的上下文才可能反驳它。

Method: 提出CEVA框架。核心为双分支互补重组模块（DBCR），将帧区分为显式对齐帧与候选互补上下文帧两类证据角色，实现融合前的证据组织；随后在重组后的证据上执行带双向对比正则的跨模态融合。

Result: 在FakeSV与FakeTT上，CEVA在对比方法中取得最佳综合结果；在固定帧预算与骨干网络条件下，DBCR优于均匀、随机与基于相似度的选帧策略，并能提升两种结构不同的检测器。

Conclusion: 融合前证据组织是短视频假新闻检测中有效且可迁移的组件。

Abstract: Short-video platforms are now a common way for the public to follow unfolding events. Their rapid, visually persuasive format also makes fake news easier to spread. Unlike text-image posts, short-video news interleaves multiple modalities and changing scenes, making it difficult to identify which frames verify a claim. Existing detectors either pool sampled frames homogeneously or select them by text-frame similarity. Both overlook that aligned frames may repeat a misleading claim, whereas less dominant context may challenge it. We present CEVA (Complementary Evidence Verification Approach), to our knowledge the first short-video fake news detection framework to explicitly organize frames by their evidential roles before multimodal fusion. Its Dual-Branch Complementary Reorganization (DBCR) module separates explicit-alignment frames from candidate complementary-context frames, and cross-modal fusion with bidirectional contrastive regularization operates on the reorganized evidence. On FakeSV and FakeTT, CEVA achieves the best overall results among the compared methods. Under fixed frame budgets and backbones, DBCR outperforms uniform, random, and similarity-based selection and improves two structurally different detectors. Together, these results establish pre-fusion evidence organization as an effective and transferable component of short-video fake news detection.

</details>


### [37] [EgoHandWAM: Probing the Role of Hand Motion in Egocentric World Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22151)
*Kailin Wang,Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Zhiyou Heng,Zhaosong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究 EgoHandWAM，用手部运动预测第一视角未来视觉特征，发现轨迹预测有限、手部历史对视觉预测有边际贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究手部运动能在多大程度上预示第一人称相机的未来观测，即手部状态是否为可用的预测性信号。

Method: 基于冻结 DINOv2 编码器，在 751,065 个 EgoDex 窗口上训练，从当前图像与近期手部状态联合预测三维腕部/指尖轨迹及未来视觉特征；并进行消融（打乱历史、遮蔽、仿真 RoboTwin 任务替换实验）。

Result: 纯动作模型将两秒腕部终点误差由 60.26 mm 降至 56.48 mm，但仍低估长距离伸取；加入未来特征监督几乎不改变轨迹精度。视觉头在真实手部历史下平滑 L1 较复制当前特征降低 1.32%，打乱跨会话历史后增益消失；四任务探针中加入未来位移与有效性掩码使误差降 3.93%，仅掩码收益很小。仿真 RoboTwin 中用对齐人类样本替换辅助机器人批次在三个随机种子下无一致增益。

Conclusion: 手部运动与未来视觉之间存在真实但有限的预测信号，历史一致性是关键；手部预测作为辅助信号尚不足以稳定提升下游任务成功率。

Abstract: How much does hand motion tell us about what a first-person camera will see next? EgoHandWAM forecasts three-dimensional wrist and fingertip trajectories and a future visual feature from the current image and recent hand states. We train on 751,065 EgoDex windows using a frozen DINOv2 encoder. On held-out development sessions, the action-only model reduces two-second wrist endpoint error from 60.26 to 56.48~mm relative to a stationary forecast. It still underestimates long reaches. Adding future-feature supervision gives nearly identical trajectory accuracy. With the recorded hand history, the visual head reduces smooth L1 error by 1.32\% relative to copying the current feature. Shuffling histories across sessions erases this gain. In a separate four-task probe, adding recorded future displacements to observed context and future tracking-validity masks reduces visual-feature error by 3.93\%. Masks alone give little improvement. A trajectory predicted from the same observations adds little. On two simulated RoboTwin tasks, substituting aligned human examples for an auxiliary batch of robot examples gives no consistent increase in task success across three training seeds.

</details>


### [38] [Unsupervised Multi-modal Medical Image Registration via Invertible Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22152)
*Mengjie Guo,Jinming Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无监督可逆神经网络配准方法INNReg，通过图像翻译+配准两阶段实现多模态医学图像对齐，并用可逆网络与NMI屏障损失保证几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态医学图像配准对图像引导治疗至关重要，但不同模态间空间关系复杂且往往未知，直接配准困难。

Method: INNReg由图像到图像的翻译网络（将多模态图像转为单模态）和基于翻译结果的配准网络组成；为保持翻译后的几何一致性，引入可逆神经网络，并结合动态深度可分离卷积的局部注意力机制；同时设计基于归一化互信息（NMI）的新型屏障损失（barrier loss）约束配准网络。

Result: 在MRI T1/T2与MRI/CT两个公开多模态医学图像数据集上的实验表明INNReg性能优越。

Conclusion: INNReg以无监督翻译驱动方式有效提升多模态配准精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: In medical imaging, the alignment of multi-modal images plays a critical role in providing comprehensive information for image-guided therapies. Despite its importance, multi-modal image registration poses significant challenges due to the complex and often unknown spatial relationships between different image modalities. To address this, we introduce a novel unsupervised translation-based multi-modal registration method, termed Invertible Neural Network-based Registration (INNReg). INNReg consists of an image-to-image translation network that converts multi-modal images into mono-modal counterparts and a registration network that uses the translated mono-modal images to align the multi-modal images. Specifically, to ensure the preservation of geometric consistency after image translation, we introduce an Invertible Neural Network (INN) that leverages a dynamic depthwise convolution-based local attention mechanism. Additionally, we design a novel barrier loss function based on Normalized Mutual Information to impose constraints on the registration network, which enhances the registration accuracy. The superior performance of INNReg is demonstrated through experiments on two public multi-modal medical image datasets, including MRI T1/T2 and MRI/CT pairs. The code is available at https://github.com/MeggieGuo/INNReg

</details>


### [39] [Centroid Spectral Coupling: Implicit Alignment and Representation Diversity in Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22158)
*M. Hadi Sepanj,Benyamin Ghojogh,Saed Moradi,Paul Fieguth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出中心化谱耦合(CSC)单目标自监督学习损失，将视图对齐、表征多样性与抗坍缩隐式耦合于一个岭谱目标中，在多个基准上取得非对比方法中最佳线性探测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非对比自监督方法需用多个加权损失项分别实现视图对齐、表征多样性与防坍缩，引入权重调参负担且目标不统一。作者希望用单一目标同时隐含地满足这三项要求。

Method: CSC 对独立归一化的增强视图对构造质心，并对其无中心化二阶矩矩阵施加岭(ridge)谱目标；通过保留质心模长编码视图一致性，并鼓励谱方向上表征能量均衡。理论分析给出非负对齐项与谱不平衡项的精确分解、全局最优几何刻画、对齐与多样性的有限损失保证，并证明常量输出完全坍缩在归一化表征空间中为严格鞍点。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Imagenette 上，CSC 线性 Top-1 精度均为所评估非对比方法中最高；两个 CIFAR 数据集上 kNN Top-1 也最高。岭参数敏感性实验显示经验性对齐—多样性权衡：增大 κ 增强视图对齐但降低表征有效秩。

Conclusion: 统一谱目标无需显式对比负样本、也无需分别加权的对齐与多样性惩罚，即可获得有竞争力的视觉表征，验证了单项目标耦合三项性质的有效性。

Abstract: Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) aims to align augmented views while preserving representation diversity and preventing collapse. Existing non-contrastive methods often address these goals using multiple weighted loss terms. We introduce Centroid Spectral Coupling (CSC), a single-term SSL objective that implicitly couples view alignment, representation diversity, and collapse resistance. CSC constructs centroids from independently normalized augmented-view pairs and applies a ridge-based spectral objective to their uncentered second-moment matrix. Preserving centroid magnitudes encodes view agreement while encouraging balanced representation energy across spectral directions. We establish an exact decomposition into nonnegative alignment and spectral-imbalance components, characterize the geometry of global optima, and derive finite-loss guarantees for alignment and diversity. We also show that complete constant-output collapse is a strict saddle in normalized representation space. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Imagenette show that CSC achieves the highest linear Top-1 accuracy among the evaluated non-contrastive methods on all three benchmarks, and the highest kNN Top-1 accuracy on both CIFAR datasets. A ridge-parameter sensitivity study reveals an empirical alignment--diversity trade-off: increasing $κ$ strengthens view alignment but reduces representation effective rank. These results show that a unified spectral objective yields competitive visual representations without explicit contrastive negatives or separately weighted alignment and diversity penalties.

</details>


### [40] [HandRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Hand Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22164)
*Zhekai Wang,Luqing Luo,Wenhao Yao,Junjie Lu,Huanan Zhu,Feifei Gao,Yuhua Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HandRSI：递归自我改进框架，交替优化可执行推理harness与视频估计器（选择性蒸馏），在HOT3D/ARCTIC上大幅降低MPJPE-p与抖动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称3D手部姿态估计存在图像边缘漏检、遮挡手位置错误与抖动等问题；常见修复手段（手写推理精修与微调）相互依赖却被分开调参，效率低且易顾此失彼。

Method: 构建含视频估计器与可执行推理harness的手部姿态系统。每轮由编码智能体改进harness，仅在hold-out数据上使十项基准指标的复合值提升且无显著单项退化时接受；被接受的harness指导估计器做选择性蒸馏——无GT帧上监督手部存在性与时序一致性，GT帧监督姿态；随后在新估计器上重新验证harness，形成递归闭环。

Result: 以ACE-Ego-Hand为起点，五轮后在HOT3D上MPJPE-p降低35%、ARCTIC降低11%，抖动减半；HOT3D上比同GT微调的ACE-Ego-Hand低16%；统一协议下在两项基准的全部十项指标上优于九个复跑方法；经超体积选择得到20.7–41.1 FPS的Lite/Medium/Heavy系统。

Conclusion: 将推理精修与模型微调耦合为递归自我改进闭环可稳定提升精度与稳定性，并兼顾速度档位；方法、各轮阶段与完整协议及代码、评估器、harness、权重均公开。

Abstract: Egocentric 3D hand pose estimators still miss hands at the image border, misplace occluded hands and jitter. Such failures are usually fixed by hand-written inference refinements and by fine-tuning, two interdependent steps that are tuned separately. We present HandRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework for a hand pose system that consists of a video estimator and its executable inference harness. In each round, coding agents refine the harness, and a change is accepted only if it improves a composite of all ten benchmark metrics on held-out data without significantly regressing any of them. The accepted harness then guides the estimator through selective distillation, which supervises hand presence and temporal consistency on frames without ground truth, while ground truth supervises pose. Since the updated estimator may change which refinements remain useful, the harness is re-validated on it. Starting from the released ACE-Ego-Hand model, five rounds reduce MPJPE-p by 35% on HOT3D and 11% on ARCTIC and roughly halve jitter. On HOT3D, HandRSI's MPJPE-p is 16% lower than that of ACE-Ego-Hand fine-tuned on the same ground truth. Under a unified protocol, it outperforms nine re-run published methods on all ten metrics of both benchmarks. Hypervolume selection on the accuracy-speed front yields Lite, Medium and Heavy systems running at 20.7 to 41.1 FPS. This report gives the full method, every stage of every round and the complete protocol. Code, evaluator, harnesses and weights are public.

</details>


### [41] [CueClash: Frontier Vision-Language Models Judge 3-D Relations Close to the Picture Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22180)
*Soham Dan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CueClash通过将2-D线索与真实布局分离，发现主流VLM在3-D关系判断中高度依赖2-D捷径（+18~+37 pp），而给定坐标时准确率99~100%，失败环节在于从图像到布局的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 照片中2-D线索与地面布局通常一致，使VLM的3-D关系判断可能只是利用了图像2-D捷径而非真正推断空间布局，需要可控实验将两者解耦以量化这一shortcut gap。

Method: 构建CueClash：渲染场景中五类关系的2-D线索在恰好一半陈述上正确。定义shortcut gap=线索正确时准确率−线索错误时准确率。测试Claude Opus 5.5、GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.6 Flash（低推理努力）；另做给定地面坐标与关系定义的条件、像素测量+相机深度条件、俯视地图重建分析，并在CV-Bench照片上验证。

Result: shortcut gap为+18~+37 pp；剔除两类混杂关系后为+7~+29 pp。给定坐标与定义时准确率99~100%。像素测量+相机深度对可穿过开口的物体基本消除gap。俯视地图大多保留深度顺序但压缩深度；GPT-5.5地图近乎复制图片，Opus与Gemini分别纠正约0.6的压缩，Gemini另对所有深度施加额外均匀缩放。Opus主要在"fits"关系上跟随图片，其深度纠正程度与真实场景人类观察者相当。三模型在2-D线索误导的CV-Bench照片上同样掉点；两个开源模型中布局信息能到达语言模型，但其地图丢失了大量信息。

Conclusion: VLM的3-D关系判断瓶颈不在关系推理本身，而在从图片到布局的转换步骤；模型具备布局知识但中间地图表示有损且受2-D线索偏置，且该偏置可泛化到真实照片基准。

Abstract: A vision-language model (VLM) can judge 3-D relations from the picture or from the ground layout. In photographs the two usually agree. We separate them with CueClash, rendered scenes in which the 2-D cue for each of five relations is right on exactly half the statements. The shortcut gap is accuracy when the cue is right minus accuracy when it is wrong. For Claude Opus 5.5, GPT-5.5 and Gemini 3.6 Flash at low reasoning effort it is +18 to +37 percentage points. It is +7 to +29 without two relations whose cue-wrong items are confounded with borderline cases or occlusion. Given ground coordinates and each relation's definition, they answer 99 to 100% correctly. Pixel measurements with camera depths remove most of the gap for objects fitting through openings. The failure lies in the step from the picture to the layout. The maps that the models draw of the scene from above mostly keep the depth order but, like the picture, shorten depth. GPT-5.5's maps stay close to a copy of the picture. Opus 5.5's and Gemini's maps undo about 0.6 of this shortening. Gemini's then shrink all depths by a further uniform factor. Opus 5.5 follows the picture mainly on fits, and its maps correct depth about as much as human observers of real scenes do. The three models also lose accuracy on CV-Bench photographs where a reliable 2-D cue misleads. In two open models the layout information reaches the language model, but their maps lose much of it.

</details>


### [42] [SRED: Sparse Regional Evidence Distillation Across Magnifications in Whole-Slide Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22189)
*Zhiyuan Yang,Jiahao Cheng,Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出稀疏区域证据蒸馏（SRED），通过SAE事件字典与逻辑OR池化，将20x教师特征的“区域事件是否发生”作为目标蒸馏给5x学生编码器，无需切片级标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高倍（20x）WSI保留细节但需编码大量patch、计算昂贵；低倍（5x）计算省但丢细节。现有跨倍率蒸馏匹配的特征常包含低倍输入无法获取的细节信息，且部分方法依赖切片级标签，限制可扩展性。

Method: 用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从20x教师特征学习事件字典；对空间对齐的patch做逻辑OR池化，记录每个事件是否在对应5x区域内任一patch中被检测到，从而保留局部与共享证据；再以这些区域级目标微调预训练5x编码器（无切片级标签）。

Result: 相比原生5x特征，7个分类/分级队列上宏F1平均提升1.1个点，5个生存队列上C-index平均提升1.7个点；运行时间与原生5x相当，较20x平均加速11.58倍。可视化显示部分字典成分对应可识别的组织学模式。

Conclusion: SRED以“区域可达事件”为蒸馏目标，规避了低倍不可达细节的匹配问题，且无需切片级标签，在性能提升的同时保持低倍高效推理，并具备目标可解释性。

Abstract: In computational pathology, high-magnification whole-slide image analysis preserves tissue detail but requires encoding many patches, whereas lower magnification reduces computation by covering a higher field of view per patch while sacrificing detail. Existing cross-magnification distillation matches features that often encode detail unreachable by low-magnification input, sometimes requiring slide-level labels that limit scalability. To address those issues, we introduce Sparse Regional Evidence Distillation (SRED), which transfers regional event occurrences that encode reachable high-level interactions between details without slide-level labels. We use a sparse autoencoder (SAE) to learn an event dictionary from 20x teacher features. Logical OR pooling records whether each event is detected in any spatially aligned patch, retaining both localized and shared evidence within the corresponding 5x region. A pretrained 5x encoder is fine-tuned to predict these regional targets without slide-level labels. Visual analysis identifies dictionary components responsive to recognizable histological patterns, supporting target interpretation. Compared with native 5x features, SRED improves average macro-F1 by 1.1 points across seven classification and grading cohorts and average C-index by 1.7 points across five survival cohorts. Runtime matches native 5x, with an average 11.58x speedup over 20x.

</details>


### [43] [MIRAGE: Motion Identity Reduction via Autoregressive Generative Encoding for Privacy-Preserving Skeleton-based Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22200)
*Thomas Carr,Xujun Che,Depeng Xu,Shuhan Yuan,Aidong Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRAGE 是一种无需目标骨架、因果在线的人体骨架匿名化器：在原始坐标空间用注意力对历史帧计算逐帧残差，在 NTU RGB+D 60 上于自适应攻击者设定下把重识别率从 75.4% 降至 43.4%，同时保持 83.9% 的动作识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨架流被大量送入第三方分析，而骨架本身可凭体型比例与运动风格识别人。已有对抗匿名器仅逐帧处理、缺乏时序建模；运动重定向需目标骨架与完整序列，无法在线使用。因此需要一种目标无关、可因果流式运行的匿名化方法。

Method: MIRAGE 在原始坐标空间为每个到来帧添加降身份残差，残差由对过去帧的注意力计算得出。在 rotary-position 编码器—解码器中使用统一的因果（可选窗宽）掩码控制所有注意力，并借助 key/value 缓存实现逐帧推理；批处理与流式输出在全部 56,578 条 NTU 60 序列上最大差异仅 7.2e-6 m。评估覆盖被动攻击者（原始数据训练的身份模型）与在匿名输出上重训练的自适应攻击者，报告结果采用无界窗口（状态随流长度增长）。

Result: 在 NTU RGB+D 60、五个生成器种子下与 Moon 等人的逐帧残差匿名器同等训练条件对比：消费者重训练后，MIRAGE 动作识别 83.9%（原始 89.1%，Moon 等 88.7%）；重训练身份分类器在 40 个受试者上重识别率 43.4%（Moon 等 69.4%，原始 75.4%）；最强自适应攻击者仍达 49.3%（随机 2.5%）。对冻结的原始训练分类器，重识别率低于最强重定向基线（10.36% vs. 22.09%），但冻结动作识别降至 37.04%（对照 75.82%）。

Conclusion: MIRAGE 能降低身份泄露但并未真正实现匿名化：在自适应、需重训练的设定下取得较好的隐私—效用折中，且流式与批处理一致、可在线部署；但在全残差强度下，其输出不是现有冻结模型的直接替代输入。此外无界窗口导致状态随流增长，是部署时需权衡的限制。

Abstract: Skeleton motion identifies people through body proportions and movement style, and skeleton streams are increasingly passed to third-party analytics that run action classifiers trained on raw data. A prior adversarial anonymizer adds a per-frame residual in the raw coordinate space but sees one frame at a time; motion retargeting needs a target skeleton and complete sequences, which rules out online use. We present MIRAGE, a target-free, causal anonymizer that adds an identity-reducing residual to each incoming frame in the raw coordinate space, computed by attention over past frames. One causal, optionally windowed mask governs every attention in a rotary-position encoder-decoder, and key/value caches let the trained function run frame by frame; on all 56,578 NTU 60 sequences, batched and streaming outputs differ by at most 7.2e-6 m. Reported results use an unbounded window, so per-stream state grows with stream length. We evaluate against a passive adversary that applies raw-trained identity models and an adaptive adversary that trains on the anonymizer's output, treating the adaptive adversary as the headline, on NTU RGB+D 60 over five generator seeds, alongside the per-frame residual anonymizer of Moon et al. trained identically. When consumers retrain on the released data, MIRAGE keeps 83.9% action recognition (raw 89.1%; Moon et al. 88.7%) while a retrained identity classifier reaches 43.4% re-identification (RI) over 40 subjects, against 69.4% for Moon et al. and 75.4% on raw data. The strongest adaptive attacker still reaches 49.3% (chance 2.5%), so MIRAGE reduces identity leakage; it does not anonymize. Against raw-trained (frozen) classifiers, MIRAGE lowers RI below the strongest retargeting baseline (10.36% vs. 22.09%), but frozen action recognition falls to 37.04% (vs. 75.82%), so at full residual scale it is not a drop-in replacement for raw input to existing frozen models.

</details>


### [44] [ARG-Avatar: Animation-Rendering Guidance for Efficient Feedforward Avatar Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22207)
*Jianjin Xu,Soham Dasgupta,Fernando De la Torre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅68M参数的轻量前馈框架ARG-Avatar，利用3D动画与渲染提供的几何对应先验（FACRoPE注意力与ITR管线）替代大规模缩放，实现SOTA的重建质量与显著效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式可动画3D人体化身重建推理快、易用，但近期方法严重依赖扩大模型容量、训练数据和算力。作者质疑这种缩放的必要性，认为3D动画与渲染本身已提供强几何对应关系，而现有方法却在从数据中重复学习这些对应。

Method: 设计Foreground Avatar Coordinate RoPE注意力（FACRoPE）与Intermediate Token Rendering（ITR）管线，引导化身token关注其对应的图像patch，从而注入几何对应先验；整个框架仅有68M可训练参数。

Result: 在多数据集上，输入两张及以上图像时持续优于先前方法。相比LHM++，可训练参数少约9倍、训练序列少4.8倍、训练算力少14倍，推理快6倍，并在分布外NeuMan基准上PSNR提升2.7 dB。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用3D动画与渲染的几何对应先验，而非单纯堆叠容量与数据，可实现小而高效的前馈化身重建模型并达到SOTA，表明大规模缩放并非必要。

Abstract: Reconstructing animatable 3D human avatars from images is important for applications such as gaming and augmented reality. Feedforward methods make avatar reconstruction accessible by reducing inference time to a few seconds, but recent approaches rely heavily on scaling model capacity, training data, and computation. We ask whether such scaling is necessary. To this end, we introduce ARG-Avatar, an efficient feedforward framework with only 68M trainable parameters that achieves SOTA reconstruction quality. Our key insight is that 3D animation and rendering already provide strong geometric correspondences that existing methods largely attempt to learn from data. In light of this, we design the Foreground Avatar Coordinate RoPE attention (FACRoPE) and Intermediate Token Rendering (ITR) pipeline to guide avatar tokens to attend to their corresponding patches. Experiments across multiple datasets show that ARG-Avatar consistently outperforms prior methods when using two or more input images. Compared with LHM++, ARG-Avatar uses approximately 9$\times$ fewer trainable parameters, 4.8$\times$ fewer training sequences, and 14$\times$ less training compute, while running 6$\times$ faster and achieving a 2.7 dB PSNR improvement on the out-of-distribution NeuMan benchmark.

</details>


### [45] [Joint-ID: Transformer-based Joint Image Enhancement and Depth Estimation for Underwater Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22212)
*Geonmo Yang,Gilhwan Kang,Juhui Lee,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Joint-ID，一种基于Transformer的联合学习框架，从原始水下图像同时实现高质量图像增强与深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下成像受散射、吸收和湍流影响导致图像质量下降、可见度受限；增强与深度估计通常被分开处理，未利用二者互补性。

Method: 构建Transformer网络Joint-ID；设计多模态目标函数，针对无效深度、清晰度不足及基于颜色与局部纹理的图像退化；采用端到端训练流程，在共享分层特征空间中联合实现复原与深度估计；并构建含多种失真与场景深度的合成数据集用于多任务学习。

Result: 在合成数据集、标准数据集及具有多样光谱和强浑浊度的真实水下图像上验证，增强与深度估计均有效；并证明其可支持特征匹配与显著性检测，服务于视觉引导的水下机器人。

Conclusion: Joint-ID有望提升水下环境视觉感知能力，应用于海洋学与水下机器人等领域。

Abstract: Underwater imaging is a challenging task due to factors such as scattering, absorption, and turbulence, which degrade image quality and limit visibility. In this paper, we propose a novel approach for enhancing underwater images that leverages the benefits of joint learning for simultaneous image enhancement and depth estimation. We introduce Joint-ID, a transformer-based neural network that can obtain high-perceptual image quality and depth information from raw underwater images. Our approach formulates a multi-modal objective function that addresses invalid depth, lack of sharpness, and image degradation based on color and local texture. We design an end-to-end training pipeline that enables joint restoration and depth estimation in a shared hierarchical feature space. Additionally, we propose a synthetic dataset with various distortions and scene depths for multi-task learning. We evaluate Joint-ID on synthetic and standard datasets, as well as real underwater images with diverse spectra and harsh turbidity, demonstrating its effectiveness for underwater image enhancement and depth estimation. Furthermore, we demonstrate the ability of Joint-ID to perform feature matching and saliency detection for visually guided underwater robots. Our proposed method has the potential to improve the visual perception of underwater environments and benefit applications such as oceanography and underwater robotics. Supplements are available at: https://sites.google.com/view/joint-id/home

</details>


### [46] [ExplicitIR: Explicit Spatial Policy Fields for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22222)
*Dezhi Zhang,Shangrong Yang,Yudong Liang,Wenjian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ExplicitIR，将一体化图像修复的修复策略显式表示为逐像素的Budget场（决定更新位置与强度）与Style场（选择窄/宽上下文窗口），通过空间策略估计器与策略自适应解码块实现单次前向的空间自适应修复，无需任务标签、提示或测试时退化估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一体化图像修复方法的适应策略隐含编码于特征表示中，修复决策既不可显式表示也难以独立控制；且真实退化在空间上分布不均，全图统一更新会导致干净区域过平滑与局部退化欠校正。

Method: 将修复策略表示为两个显式逐像素场：Budget（决定在何处、以何强度更新）与Style（在窄窗口与宽窗口上下文间选择）。由Spatial Policy Estimator (SPE)预测这两个场，再由Policy-Adaptive Decoder Blocks (PADB)通过窄/宽窗口响应的自适应融合施加策略，实现独立控制与精确局部调制。

Result: 在三任务与五任务基准上的大量实验表明，ExplicitIR的性能与最强的一体化基线相当；且单次前向即可完成空间自适应修复，无需任务标签、提示或测试时退化估计器。

Conclusion: 显式化、可独立控制的逐像素修复策略是缓解空间非均匀退化的有效途径，为一体化图像修复提供了可控且高效的新范式。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to recover clean images blindly from inputs with various degradation types and levels using a single model. However, the adaptation policy in most existing methods remains implicitly encoded in feature representations, leaving the restoration decision neither explicitly represented nor independently controllable. Since real-world degradations vary spatially, applying uniform updates across the entire image leads to over-smoothing of clean areas and under-correction of localized degradations. To address these limitations, we present ExplicitIR, which represents the restoration policy as two explicit per-pixel fields: Budget, which determines where and how strongly to update, and Style, which selects between narrow and wide context windows. Specifically, ExplicitIR incorporates a Spatial Policy Estimator (SPE) to predict these fields, explicitly representing the restoration policy and capturing spatially-varying degradations, which are then applied by Policy-Adaptive Decoder Blocks (PADB) through adaptive fusion of narrow- and wide-window responses, enabling independent control and precise local modulation. This design enables spatially-adaptive restoration in a single forward pass, without requiring task labels, prompts, or test-time degradation estimators. Extensive experiments on three-task and five-task benchmarks demonstrate that ExplicitIR achieves performance competitive with the strongest all-in-one baselines.

</details>


### [47] [TRIDENT: Efficient Triple-Task Learning of Dehazing, Depth, and Uncertainty Estimation for Underwater 3-D Robot Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22224)
*Geonmo Yang,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级多任务模型TRIDENT，用共享主干加三个并行解码器同时完成水下图像增强、深度估计与不确定性建模，在多个标准数据集上性能超越现有方法且更省内存与推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下光线传播导致图像纹理模糊、色彩对比度低，严重削弱机器人稳定作业所需的视觉感知；且三维视觉系统需同时处理增强、深度与不确定性等多维任务。

Method: 一种基于学习的水下感知系统，采用快速轻量的TRIDENT模型：单一主干结构实现高效特征共享，配合三个并行解码器分别处理图像增强、深度估计与不确定性，并以联合学习方式支持复杂参数化表达。

Result: 在若干标准数据集上，图像增强与深度估计显著优于现有方法；在完成三项任务的同时，内存占用与推理时间均优于对比方法；联合学习在特征匹配上表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明共享主干的并行多解码器架构可高效统一水下2-D增强与3-D深度/不确定性任务，并平滑扩展至三维视觉系统。

Abstract: Underwater visual systems often suffer from blurry textures and low color contrast due to the inevitable light propagation. These issues can significantly degrade the perception of stable robotic operations. In this paper, we introduce a novel learning-based sensing system that tackles the multidimensional vision tasks in underwater; concretely, we deal with image enhancement, depth estimation, and uncertainty for 3-D visual systems. Also, we propose a TRIDENT model in a fast and lightweight manner; TRIDENT consists of three parallelized decoders and one backbone structure for efficient feature sharing. In addition, it is designed to be trained to express complex parameterization. In experimental evaluation on several standard datasets, we demonstrate that TRIDENT significantly outperforms other existing methods on image enhancement and depth estimation. Despite performing three tasks, our model has better efficiency than the others for both memory size and inference time. Finally, our joint learning approach demonstrates robustness in feature matching and seamlessly extends from 2-D to 3-D vision tasks. Supplements are available at https://sites.google.com/view/underwater-trident/home

</details>


### [48] [CoSphere-SLAM: Multi-Agent Panoramic Gaussian Splatting SLAM with Spherical Loop Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22235)
*Qi Shao,Zhihao Cao,Shuhao Zhai,Taoyu Wu,Zhuoxiao Li,Anh Nguyen,Long Hu,Xianzhi Li,Hong Liang,Baoru Huang,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoSphere-SLAM：首个面向全景相机的多智能体高斯泼溅SLAM框架，通过SO(3)球面回环、全局Sim(3)共识剔除对称误闭、方向性全局优化与全向高斯融合，实现高精度轨迹与渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景相机可单帧覆盖全场景，适用于搜救、建筑巡检与仓储自动化。但全景智能体的重叠观测可在任意3D朝向下发生，全旋转搜索会因对称房间产生互相一致的误回环；单目连续尺度漂移使重访面处刚性合并的地图错位；短基线深度噪声会扭曲回环平移方向。

Method: 1) 球面回环：基于快速傅里叶相关在SO(3)上对齐球面特征，支持任意朝向；2) 利用全局Sim(3)共识拒绝对称误闭；3) 方向性全局优化：求解逐关键帧Sim(3)位姿图，并用对数尺度正则化校正尺度漂移，将光流平移方向与深度幅值分离；4) 全向高斯融合：以关键帧相似度变换各高斯，并在最大射线响应处渲染，联合优化位姿与地图。

Result: 在室内及跨会话多智能体全景基准上，CoSphere-SLAM在单智能体与多智能体方法中均取得最优轨迹精度与渲染质量。

Conclusion: CoSphere-SLAM验证了球面几何与Sim(3)一致性的结合可有效解决全景多智能体SLAM中的误回环、尺度漂移与深度噪声问题，为全景多智能体稠密协同建图提供了统一的高斯泼溅方案。

Abstract: Dense collaborative mapping is essential for search and rescue, building inspection, and warehouse automation, where panoramic cameras capture the entire surroundings in a single frame. Unlike pinhole agents that share views only at similar headings, panoramic agents observe overlapping scenes at any 3D orientation, yet searching full rotations also produces mutually consistent false loop closures across distinct symmetric rooms. Moreover, continuous monocular scale drift splits rigidly merged maps at revisited surfaces, while depth noise at short baselines distorts loop translation directions. To address these challenges, we propose CoSphere-SLAM, the first multi-agent Gaussian splatting SLAM framework for panoramic cameras, whose spherical loop closure aligns spherical features over $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ via fast Fourier correlation across arbitrary orientations and rejects symmetric false closures through global $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ consensus. Building on these closures, directional global optimization solves a per-keyframe $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ pose graph with log-scale regularization to correct scale drift while separating optical-flow translation directions from depth-based magnitudes. Finally, omnidirectional Gaussian fusion transforms each Gaussian with its keyframe similarity and renders Gaussians at their maximum ray response to jointly refine poses and maps. Experiments on indoor and cross-session multi-agent panoramic benchmarks demonstrate that CoSphere-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art trajectory accuracy and rendering quality among single-agent and multi-agent methods. Project website: https://cosphere-slam.github.io.

</details>


### [49] [Cross3DGS: Unified Vulkan-Based 3D Gaussian Splatting Training from Workstations to Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22240)
*Hiroyuki Sato,Yusuke Takimoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cross3DGS 是一个跨厂商、跨平台的 Vulkan 3DGS 训练器，通过按设备选择执行调度，实现在 NVIDIA/Intel/Arm/Qualcomm/AMD/Apple GPU（含 Android 与 iPhone）上完成标准质量训练，并优于现有可移植训练器 VkSplat 与 Brush。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持与可穿戴设备采集的场景当前仍需上传到 CUDA 工作站进行 3DGS 训练；在采集设备或其配套手机/笔记本上训练，不只是内核移植问题：不同 GPU 与驱动在并行特性、内存上限和命令提交上存在差异，这些差异决定训练调度能否正确执行并返回正确更新。

Method: 提出 Cross3DGS：统一的优化器，按设备两步选择执行调度。第一步用规则（基于设备限制、工作负载规模、观测到的驱动失败）排除已知无法正确运行的调度；第二步在剩余可行调度中按固定优先顺序选取一个。

Result: 在所有实测配置上均完成标准质量训练（30k 次更新、一百万高斯），覆盖 NVIDIA、Intel、Arm、Qualcomm、AMD、Apple GPU 及 Android 设备与 iPhone；VkSplat 和 Brush 在部分移动 GPU 上无法运行。工作站 11 个场景平均，质量高于 VkSplat 且训练时间无显著差异；Brush 在可比质量下训练时间约为其三倍。各设备最终质量几乎一致。

Conclusion: 按设备自适应调度可行且可移植性强，跨设备质量一致；并实现了移动应用，将手持采集完整在设备端转化为可查看模型。

Abstract: Scenes captured with handheld and wearable cameras are still shipped to a CUDA workstation for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) training. Training on the device that captured them, or on its companion phone or laptop, requires more than translating kernels: GPUs and drivers differ in parallel features, memory limits, and command submission, and these differences decide whether a training schedule executes and returns the correct update. We present Cross3DGS, a cross-vendor, cross-platform Vulkan trainer that keeps one optimizer and chooses its execution schedule per device in two steps: rules on device limits, workload size, and observed driver failures exclude the schedules known not to run correctly, and a fixed preference order selects one of the remaining feasible schedules. The same core completes standard-quality training (30k updates, one million Gaussians) on every configuration we measured, spanning NVIDIA, Intel, Arm, Qualcomm, AMD, and Apple GPUs and including Android devices and an iPhone, whereas the existing portable trainers VkSplat and Brush do not run on some of the mobile GPUs. Averaged over 11 scenes on a workstation, Cross3DGS has higher quality than VkSplat with no significant difference in training time, and Brush reports about three times the training time at comparable quality. The final quality of Cross3DGS is nearly identical across devices. A mobile application turns a handheld capture into a viewable model entirely on the device.

</details>


### [50] [PhyVGGT: Feed-Forward Articulated Object Reconstruction from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22244)
*Qi Shao,Zhihao Cao,Shuhao Zhai,Anh Nguyen,Hong Liang,Baoru Huang,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyVGGT：面向单目视频的关节物体单次重建框架，在冻结的3D基础模型上以静态底座几何为锚点完成可动部件分割与闭式关节估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无标定单目视频重建关节物体（3D可动部件、关节参数、未见关节状态）对机器人操作与交互式数字孪生至关重要。逐场景优化方法耗时数分钟至数小时且依赖外深度/相机位姿；前馈3D基础模型虽可一次输出稠密点图，但不分割可动部件、不推断关节。直接回归/拟合连续关节在随意拍摄视频上失效，原因包括：深层跨帧注意力模糊运动部件边界、低估沿视线方向的运动、小开合角下连续轴病态。

Method: 基于冻结3D基础模型的单遍框架：1) Part and Motion Decoder 融合逐帧中间token与全局token，并将部件查询以静态底座查询为参照，实现3D部件分割、在静态底座上对齐点图、预测学习到的Bingham先验；2) Kinematic Inference Layer 对静态表面法向拟合正交Manhattan框架，在结合成对运动散布与学习先验的Bingham后验下闭式选取关节轴，并求解线性系统得到铰链枢轴以驱动未见状态。

Result: 仅在合成数据上训练，在五个基准上取得单目SOTA：优于FAMOS与单目ArtiPoint，中位误差上超过有姿态RGB-D的ArtiPoint；48帧视频耗时6.8秒，而Articulat3D逐场景优化需数小时。

Conclusion: 将可动部件匹配与闭式关节估计锚定到无畸变的静态底座几何，可在冻结3D基础模型上实现快速、纯合成的单目关节物体重建与关节推断。

Abstract: Reconstructing an articulated object with its 3D movable parts, joint parameters and unseen articulation states from an uncalibrated monocular video is essential for robot manipulation and interactive digital twins. Existing per-scene optimisation methods require minutes to hours and external depth or camera poses, whereas feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct dense point maps in one pass without segmenting movable parts or inferring joints. Even on these monocular point maps, regressing or fitting continuous joints directly fails on casual videos, since cross-frame attention blurs moving part boundaries in deep layers, underestimates motion along the viewing ray and leaves continuous axes ill-conditioned under small opening angles. To address these challenges, we propose PhyVGGT, a single-pass framework on a frozen 3D foundation model that anchors moving-part matching and closed-form joint estimation to the undistorted geometry of the stationary base. First, our Part and Motion Decoder fuses intermediate per-frame tokens with global tokens and references part queries to a static-base query to segment 3D parts, align point maps on the static base and predict learned Bingham priors. Second, our Kinematic Inference Layer fits an orthogonal Manhattan frame to static surface normals, selects the joint axis in closed form under a Bingham posterior combining pairwise motion scatter with the learned priors, and solves a linear system for the hinge pivot to drive unseen states. Trained solely on synthetic data, PhyVGGT achieves state-of-the-art monocular performance across five benchmarks, outperforming FAMOS and monocular ArtiPoint, surpassing posed RGB-D ArtiPoint in median error, and running in 6.8 s per 48-frame video against hours of per-scene optimisation in Articulat3D. Project page: https://phyvggt.github.io.

</details>


### [51] [Multispectral-Polarization-Infrared Multi-Aperture Camouflaged Vehicle Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22246)
*Shu Wang,Xiong Lin,Yuan Song,Junjie Wang,Feng Huang,Wenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出光谱-偏振-红外三模态决策级共识融合框架，配合多孔径成像，在自建伪装车辆数据集上达82.7% mAP、端到端延迟16 ms，并在MID-1K上跨模态泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装抑制了各传感模态的判别性线索，单模态检测困难；现有方法依赖像素级精确配准与特征级融合，导致精度与延迟之间的权衡。

Method: 构建光谱-偏振-红外三模态检测框架，与多孔径成像装置协同设计，利用互补成像物理特性；采用共识引导的决策级融合策略，在有限配准容差内融合跨模态检测结果，降低对精确对齐的依赖；并构建含红外、偏振、光谱数据的三模态伪装车辆数据集。

Result: 自建数据集上82.7% mAP、端到端延迟16 ms，优于代表性特征级融合方法；在公开MID-1K数据集上无需改动架构即可迁移至RGB-Depth-Infrared模态，mAP@0.5较SOTA提升5.7%。

Conclusion: 决策级共识融合与多孔径感知在鲁棒、可部署的伪装车辆检测中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Camouflaged vehicle detection is challenging because camouflage suppresses discriminative cues across sensing modalities. Existing multimodal methods often require precise pixel-level registration and feature-level fusion, creating a trade-off between accuracy and latency. We propose a spectral-polarization-infrared tri-modal detection framework co-designed with a multi-aperture imaging setup to exploit complementary imaging physics. A consensus-guided decision-level fusion strategy combines cross-modal detections within bounded registration tolerances, reducing dependence on precise alignment while maintaining accuracy and real-time performance. We construct a tri-modal camouflaged vehicle dataset containing infrared, polarization, and spectral data. Experiments show that the proposed method achieves 82.7% mean Average Precision (mAP) with an end-to-end latency of 16 ms, outperforming representative feature-level fusion approaches. On the public MID-1K dataset, the framework generalizes to RGB-Depth-Infrared modalities without architectural modification and improves mAP@0.5 by 5.7% over state-of-the-art methods. These results demonstrate the potential of decision-level consensus fusion and multi-aperture sensing for robust and deployable camouflaged vehicle detection.

</details>


### [52] [SARVIA: Fast-Adapting the Strong SAR Foundation Models That Are Already in Your MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22273)
*Yuxuan Li,Zichen Tian,Qianru Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S A RV I A，一种基于多模态大模型(MLLM)的自适应后预训练方法，用自监督可计算答案的视觉问答任务对现成MLLM做轻量化领域特化，仅需600步、2 GPU时即可导出超越专用SAR基础模型的视觉编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR基础模型通常依赖昂贵的大规模SAR领域预训练。作者发现现成MLLM的视觉编码器作为下游初始化即可优于专用SAR基础模型，由此质疑大规模领域预训练的必要性，主张复用通用视觉能力、仅做简短特化。

Method: S A RV I A以无标注观测构造"有可计算答案"的自监督视觉问题：通用图像任务捕捉空间关系，SAR特有任务利用原生测量与SAR—光学对应关系；采用共享答案预测目标联合训练视觉与语言部分的低秩适配器(LoRA)，并按损失驱动进行任务采样；最后将适配后的视觉编码器导出为独立骨干。

Result: 仅600步适配、约2 GPU时，SARVIA在六个SAR检测与分割基准上全部超越对比的基础模型；相对专用SAR预训练，领域训练成本降低数个数量级，并在不同架构与规模上提升视觉迁移效果。

Conclusion: 该工作提出从"重复昂贵预训练"转向"可复用特化"的新范式，可直接受益于开源MLLM的进展。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) foundation models provide transferable representations for diverse perception tasks in all-weather Earth observation. However, their development often relies on costly large-scale SAR pretraining, raising the question of whether such extensive domain-specific pretraining is necessary. We find that modern multimodal large language models (MLLMs) already provide visual encoders that can outperform dedicated SAR foundation models as downstream initializations. This motivates a new paradigm: reuse general visual capabilities and devote domain training to brief specialization. We therefore introduce SARVIA, an adaptive post-pretraining method that specializes modern MLLMs using self-supervised visual questions with computable answers. These questions are constructed from unlabeled observations through general image tasks that capture spatial relations and SAR-specific tasks that exploit native measurements and SAR--optical correspondence. A shared answer-prediction objective jointly trains low-rank adapters across visual and language components, with loss-driven task sampling. We then export the adapted visual encoder as a standalone backbone. With 600 adaptation steps and 2 GPU-hours, SARVIA outperforms the compared foundation models on all six SAR detection and segmentation benchmarks. Compared with dedicated SAR pretraining, SARVIA reduces domain-training costs by orders of magnitude while improving visual transfer across architectures and scales. This new paradigm shifts the emphasis from repeated, costly pretraining to a reusable specialization process that can benefit from advances in open MLLMs. Dataset and code: https://github.com/zcablii/SARVIA.

</details>


### [53] [Decoding Without a Decoder: Direct Pixel Prediction from Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22287)
*Hansheng Chen,Shengqu Cai,Jan Ackermann,Rayhan Zirvi,Gordon Wetzstein,Xinchen Yan,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DWD（Decoding Without a Decoder），通过线性头让扩散transformer在最后一步直接输出像素，去除解码器、降低推理成本，并保持或提升像素质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间扩散模型依赖解码器将去噪潜变量映射到像素，增加了复杂度与推理成本；且以往方法中同一去噪输出既驱动采样又生成最终像素，角色混同。

Method: 分离两种角色：原去噪头继续驱动采样，新增DWD头（线性头）仅在最后一步预测像素。通过仅微调最后几个transformer block高效扩展预训练潜模型，DWD头用像素质量损失训练，同时将去噪头锚定到预训练模型以保持采样轨迹。

Result: 在潜空间图像与视频生成器上均去除解码器并降低推理成本，质量保持或提升；Wan视频生成中明显减少细节失真与闪烁，warping error降低23%；在无解码器的像素流模型上同样有效：文本生图纹理细节更好，ImageNet FID由1.55降至1.43，成为像素流模型新SOTA。

Conclusion: 将采样驱动与像素输出解耦是一种通用有效的策略，可同时降低推理成本与提升质量，且适用于潜空间模型和无解码器的像素扩散模型。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models rely on a decoder to map denoised latents to pixels, adding complexity and inference cost. We introduce Decoding Without a Decoder (DWD), which lets a finetuned diffusion transformer produce final pixels directly through a linear head. Unlike previous methods, where the denoising output leads to final pixels, DWD separates the two roles: the original denoising head continues to drive sampling, then the new DWD head predicts the pixels at the final step. We extend pretrained latent models with DWD efficiently by finetuning only the last few transformer blocks, training the DWD head with pixel quality losses while anchoring the denoising head to the pretrained model to preserve the sample trajectory. Across latent image and video generators, DWD removes the decoder and reduces inference cost while maintaining or even improving pixel quality. On Wan video generation, it visibly reduces detail distortion and flickering, lowering warping error by 23%. Remarkably, the same separation also improves pixel diffusion models that do not even use a decoder: a final-step DWD head produces substantially better texture detail in text-to-image generation, and improves ImageNet FID from 1.55 to 1.43, a new state of the art among pixel flow models.

</details>


### [54] [Cross-Modal StateKV Injection for Communication in Multi-LLM Systems across Temporal Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22304)
*Xiaoqian Zhang,Zhihao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨模态StateKV注入框架，用KV状态替代文本作为多模型间通信接口，在MLVU和Video-MME上取得有限但依赖任务精度的提升，且延迟低于文本通信基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型协同进行视频理解时需要能传递互补视觉观测的通信接口；文本通信虽可解释，但引入中间生成阶段并可能丢失后续推理所需的信息。

Method: Provider–carrier框架：provider模型观测互补视频帧，其视觉token位置的KV状态经对齐并投影到carrier表示空间，通过门控残差更新注入carrier；注入不改变carrier的KV-cache序列长度，但保留provider与通信模块的额外计算。实验在MLVU与Video-MME上对比仅carrier推理、文本通信、以及两种provider架构下的StateKV。

Result: 精度提升幅度有限且依赖任务，报告延迟低于所评估的文本通信基线，部分类别持平或下降；增加provider侧帧覆盖可在部分任务上带来额外增益且不扩展carrier缓存。

Conclusion: 结果表明视觉KV通信在受控观测与计算预算下值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Video understanding with multiple multimodal language models requires a communication interface that can transfer complementary visual observations. Text-based communication is interpretable, but introduces an intermediate generation stage and can omit information relevant to subsequent reasoning. We study Cross-Modal StateKV Injection, a provider--carrier framework that communicates through key--value (KV) states at visual-token positions. Provider models observe complementary video frames; their visual KV states are aligned and projected into the carrier representation space and integrated through gated residual updates. Injection preserves the carrier KV-cache sequence length, while retaining the additional computation required by the providers and the communication module. The reported experiments on MLVU and Video-MME compare carrier-only inference, text-based communication, and StateKV with two provider architectures. Results show modest, task-dependent accuracy gains and lower reported latency than the evaluated text-communication baseline, with some categories remaining unchanged or decreasing. Increasing provider-side frame coverage offers additional gains on selected tasks without extending the carrier cache. These findings motivate further study of visual KV communication under explicitly controlled observation and computation budgets.

</details>


### [55] [Inertial Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22314)
*Yuan Gao,Fan Xu,Yu Guo,Yuxu Lu,Zhengru Fang,Hao Wu,Xiaomeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 InertialWorld，将"惯性"作为自回归视频世界模型的结构先验，用可演化的惯性记忆替代逐步重建，缓解误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界具有惯性，状态由已有状态演化而来；而现有视频世界模型缺乏显式的惯性状态演化机制，运动与结构连续性只能隐式地在视频预测中表达。自回归推演中反复重建会累积误差并使预测动力学不稳定。

Method: Inertial Memory：把记忆从被动预测上下文变为可演化视频状态，含两视图互补表示——稠密欧拉网格保存场景对齐证据，持久拉格朗日载体将局部内容绑定到学习到的规范坐标。每步按动作条件运动传输载体内容，由传输后的载体与稠密网格预测完整下一视频状态，并用学习门控将传输内容与新预测内容融合为下一步记忆。

Result: 在机器人操作、3D游戏仿真、开放世界导航等多领域实验中，自回归预测性能与强视频世界模型基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 将惯性显式建模为结构先验、以跨时间携带场景证据代替逐步重建，是提升自回归视频世界模型长期稳定性与预测质量的有效途径。

Abstract: The physical world has inertia: it evolves from what already exists rather than being recreated at every instant. However, many existing video world models lack an explicit mechanism for inertial state evolution, leaving the continuity of motion and structure implicit in video prediction. During autoregressive rollouts, repeated reconstruction can accumulate errors and destabilize the predicted dynamics. To address this limitation, we introduce InertialWorld, an Inertial Video World Model that makes inertia a structural prior for autoregressive prediction: existing scene evidence is carried across time rather than reconstructed at every step. Central to this formulation is Inertial Memory, which turns memory from passive predictive context into an evolving video state with two complementary views: a dense Eulerian grid preserves scene-aligned evidence, while persistent Lagrangian carriers bind local content to learned canonical coordinates. At each step, the model transports the carrier content according to action-conditioned motion, predicts the complete next video state from the transported carriers and dense grid, and uses a learned gate to combine transported and newly predicted content into the memory for the next step. Extensive experiments across multiple domains, including robot manipulation, 3D game simulation, and open-world navigation, demonstrate that InertialWorld achieves competitive autoregressive prediction performance against strong video world model baselines. Codes link: https://github.com/YuanGao-YG/InertialWorld

</details>


### [56] [SIDUMN: Self-Supervised Image Denoising Under Symmetric Multiplicative Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22317)
*Nyan Htet Naing,Gyeongha Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIDUMN：针对均值为1的对称乘性噪声，用"对偶增噪对"构造自监督去噪框架，理论上证明对偶平均不劣于单次重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘性与信号相关噪声下的自监督去噪困难：现有Noise2Noise类方法依赖加性/零均值噪声假设，乘性噪声使目标偏离干净条件均值，且单一增噪样本估计方差大。

Method: 在均值为1的对称乘性噪声下，从同一噪声分布采样独立噪声因子及其关于1的反射，构造两张互补的增噪图像；训练网络由其一预测另一；推理时对K组对偶（antithetic）对的逐对重建取平均。

Result: 推导出训练损失的总体目标为干净条件均值减去一个乘性交叉项；证明对任意已训练网络，对偶平均后的期望平方误差不超过单张增噪输入重建的期望平方误差；数值实验验证理论结论与方法有效性。

Conclusion: 对偶采样为乘性自监督去噪提供了有理论保证的方差抑制策略，偏差可控且实证有效。

Abstract: Self-supervised denoising is challenging when the corruption is multiplicative and signal-dependent. In this letter, we propose SIDUMN, a symmetric noisier framework for self-supervised denoising under mean-one symmetric multiplicative noise. Given noisy observations and the noise law, the method samples an independent synthetic noise factor from the same law and constructs two complementary noisier images by applying the factor and its reflection about one. The denoising network is trained to predict one noisier image from the other, and at inference the reconstructions of $K$ such antithetic pairs are averaged. We show that the population target of the training loss is the clean conditional mean minus a multiplicative cross-term. We further prove that, for any trained network, antithetic averaging never increases the expected squared error of a single noisier-input reconstruction. Numerical results validate the theoretical findings and the proposed method.

</details>


### [57] [Breaking Visual Conventions: Spectrally Coupled Weight Perturbation for Creative Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22318)
*Fu Feng,Ruoheng Zhou,Yucheng Xie,Xu Yang,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CosPert，一种免训练方法，通过对注意力投影的谱域耦合扰动提升文生图模型的视觉创造力，在FLUX.1与SD 3.5上兼顾多样性与类别一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升T2I创造性的方法多依赖语义条件探索或去噪过程中的特征/引导修改，而预训练权重本身蕴含的创意变化潜力未被有效利用；无结构扰动往往视觉影响微弱或损害图像质量。

Method: 先通过一次性敏感性分析定位关键层中的注意力投影；对每个投影使用共享随机变换及其转置耦合更新奇异向量因子，同时扰动谱增益；采用统一相对预算校准各投影权重变化幅度；并用sigmoid调度使扰动作用集中于去噪早期、在细化阶段逐渐衰减。方法免训练、即插即用，采样时无需辅助网络或额外模型评估。

Result: 在FLUX.1和Stable Diffusion 3.5上提升视觉创造力，生成更多样的物体结构并保持类别身份；在50个物体类别上取得所评估方法中最高的平均CREward，同时保持较强的提示对齐。

Conclusion: 预训练权重谱域的定向结构化扰动是提升T2I创造性的有效补充途径，可在不训练、不增加推理开销的前提下实现创意增强与质量、对齐的平衡。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models often produce high-quality images that follow familiar visual conventions, even in response to open-ended prompts such as ``a creative [CLS]''. Existing methods encourage novelty by exploring semantic conditions or modifying features and guidance during denoising. Pretrained weights offer a complementary source of creative variation, but unstructured perturbations can have little visual effect or degrade image quality. We introduce Coupled Spectral Perturbation (CosPert), a training-free method that targets attention projections in layers identified by a one-time sensitivity analysis. For each projection, CosPert uses a shared random transformation and its transpose to couple updates to the singular-vector factors while perturbing the spectral gains. A common relative budget calibrates the magnitude of weight changes across projections, and a sigmoid schedule concentrates their influence in early denoising and gradually attenuates them during refinement. CosPert provides plug-and-play creativity enhancement at inference time. Once the perturbations are prepared, sampling requires no auxiliary networks or additional model evaluations. On both FLUX.1 and Stable Diffusion 3.5, CosPert enhances visual creativity, producing more varied object structures while preserving category identity. Across 50 object categories, CosPert achieves the highest mean CREward among the evaluated methods while maintaining strong prompt alignment.

</details>


### [58] [M$^3$-Test: Coverage Gaps in Joint-Perturbation Testing of Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22329)
*Enhui Wan,Xiaowan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 M3-Test，一种在请求级（request-level）对齐干净输入与单模态变体、5x5 联合扰动库的覆盖审计方法，用于揭示 VQA 模型中"单模态各自通过、联合扰动却失败"的隐藏脆弱性，并实现既有评测结果的再利用与风险排序测试分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准鲁棒性报告对各条件取平均，无法说明哪些"已被接受的请求"仍然脆弱；模型可能通过所有仅图像和仅问题的测试，却在两者同时变化时失败。这种联合失效被平均值掩盖，导致接受决策与回归审查缺乏细粒度依据。

Method: 构建 M3-Test 覆盖审计：将每个干净输入与其单模态变体及完整的 5x5 家族、4x4 强度联合库对齐，使任何缩减测试集都能在同一批请求上重放。基于四个视觉语言模型共 260 万条响应进行分析；通过复执行归档失败、匹配对照组的翻转率、复核最脆弱失败记录来排除执行噪声与评分误差；并利用对齐网格从其他模型学习风险排序，在留出图像上评估测试分配效率。

Result: 在 Qwen 通过全部 40 项单模态测试的问题中，26-29% 存在联合失效，而其联合记录失败率不足 0.6%；通过 25 个最大强度角点后仍有 11-14% 的已接受问题在库内其他位置失败。复执行可复现约 90% 的归档失败，匹配通过对照翻转率仅 0.3-0.6%，脆弱失败记录复核支持率 80-89%。风险排序在 25 次额外查询内发现约 80% 受影响问题，优于最大优先（65-68%）与成对覆盖阵列（53-57%）。

Conclusion: 执行噪声与评分误差均无法完全解释该失效差距。M3-Test 将"通过"的鲁棒性套件转化为请求级的"仍未被测试内容"清单，支持接受决策、回归审查与测试预算分配，并使既往评测结果具备可复用性。

Abstract: A visual question answering (VQA) model can pass every image-only and question-only test yet fail when the same changes occur together. Standard robustness reports average over conditions and therefore cannot say which accepted requests remain vulnerable. We introduce M3-Test, a coverage audit that aligns each clean input with its single-modality variants and a complete 5x5 family, 4x4 strength joint library, so that any reduced test suite can be replayed on the same requests. Across 2.6 million responses from four vision-language models, joint failures affect 26-29% of the Qwen questions that pass all 40 single-modality tests, although fewer than 0.6% of their joint records fail. Passing the 25 maximum-strength corners does not close this gap: 11-14% of the accepted questions still fail elsewhere in the library. Re-executing the archived failures reproduces about 90% of them, against a 0.3-0.6% flip rate among matched passing controls, and re-checking the most fragile failure records supports 80-89% of them. On the evidence examined, neither execution noise nor grading error can fully account for the gap. The same aligned grids make previous evaluations reusable. On held-out images, a risk order learned from other models discovers about 80% of affected Qwen questions within 25 additional queries, compared with 65-68% for maximum-first and 53-57% for pairwise covering arrays. M3-Test thus turns a passing robustness suite into a request-level account of what remains untested, supporting acceptance, regression review and test allocation.

</details>


### [59] [DAP-MR: Degradation-Aware Prompt-Magnitude Routing for Real-World Ultrasound Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22331)
*Ngoc-Hoang-Lam Le,Thi-An Le,Dinh-Hung Le,Manh-Hung Nguyen,Kai-Lung Hua,Ching-Chun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出退化感知提示—强度路由（DAP-MR），将"退化类型"与"退化强度"解耦：类型由专用 token 表示，强度由幅度路由器预测连续、输入相关的调制信号，可插入单步与多步扩散超分框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像超分模型直接用于超声图像时，依赖语义文本提示不可靠：生成的标签与实际修复需求不匹配，专业医学术语易被通用文本编码器误解；退而使用通用图像退化类提示更有效，但单一提示同时描述退化类型与严重程度会使二者纠缠，模型难以判断退化强度。

Method: DAP-MR：①为每类退化设置专用 token，初始化为退化导向提示的上下文化 EOS 嵌入，并在超声超分训练中自适应；②独立的幅度路由器预测连续、依赖输入的幅度值，对相应 token 进行调制。语言提供"修复什么"的退化感知初始化，幅度路由提供"影响多强"的显式信号；不改动单步/多步扩散超分核心结构即可接入。

Result: 在含域内与解剖域偏移的合成数据集，以及无配对真实超声数据上，DAP-MR 取得更好的复原质量，并在不同域间表现更稳定的一致泛化。

Conclusion: 通过解耦退化类型与强度、以专用 token 加幅度调制替代语义文本提示，可为超声扩散超分提供更可靠的条件引导，且具备框架无关的即插即用性。

Abstract: When super-resolution models designed for natural images are used on ultrasound scans, relying on semantic text prompts can lead to unreliable results. The tags these models generate often do not match the actual restoration needs, and specialized medical terms may be misunderstood by general-purpose text encoders. Instead, prompts focused on common image degradations are more directly useful for guiding restoration. However, using a single prompt to describe both the type and severity of image degradation can entangle these factors, making it hard for the model to know how strong the degradation is. To address this limitation, we introduce Degradation-Aware Prompt--Magnitude Routing (DAP-MR). Each degradation type is represented by a dedicated token initialized from the contextualized EOS embedding of a degradation-oriented prompt and subsequently adapted during ultrasound SR training. A separate magnitude router predicts a continuous, input-dependent magnitude that modulates the corresponding token. Thus, language provides a degradation-aware initialization for what should be restored, while magnitude routing supplies an explicit signal for how strongly it should influence restoration. DAP-MR can be added to both one-step and multi-step diffusion super-resolution frameworks without changing their core structures. Our experiments--on synthetic datasets with both in-domain and under anatomical-domain shift, as well as unpaired real-world ultrasound data--show that DAP-MR delivers better restoration quality and generalizes more consistently across different domains.

</details>


### [60] [PURE: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Multi-Granularity Multimodal Identity Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22343)
*Hengyu You,Xintong Wang,Jin Cao,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PURE——电商场景下多粒度多模态身份匹配诊断基准，覆盖感知/理解/推理/证据判断四维、14任务共1457道选择题，揭示聚合准确率掩盖的细粒度能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份匹配的正确性依赖目标粒度：两条记录可能在基础层匹配（共享底层设计）而在变体层不匹配（颜色、尺寸等属性不同），而单一的最终匹配准确率无法分别刻画背后涉及的各项能力，因此需要一个可诊断的细粒度评测。

Method: 选取电商作为真实且有挑战性的领域，构建 PURE 诊断基准：由真实匹配对派生、两位领域专家校验，包含14个任务、1457道多选题，覆盖感知、理解、推理、证据支撑判断四个能力维度；并在十个近期 MLLM 上开展评测、分维度分析以及模态消融实验。

Result: 十个 MLLM 总体得分 47.1%–86.0%；理解维度平均分最高、判断维度最低，视觉差异定位与变体层规则判断为薄弱环节；模态消融显示模型能从多模态证据中获益，但会错误识别匹配记录间的冲突；直接匹配中，多数模型在基础层更可靠地识别匹配对而非不匹配对，变体层则相反。

Conclusion: PURE 揭示了聚合匹配准确率所掩盖的问题：不仅能判断模型是否给出正确判定，还能定位其在证据解读与匹配准则应用上的具体困难，为多粒度多模态身份匹配能力提供细粒度诊断。

Abstract: Identity matching asks whether two records denote the same identity, yet the answer depends on the target granularity. Two records may match at the base level but not at the variant level: they share an underlying design while differing in attributes such as color or size. Final matching accuracy does not separately characterize the capabilities involved. To study this problem, we use e-commerce as a challenging real-world domain. We introduce PURE, a diagnostic benchmark for multi-granularity multimodal identity matching covering four capability dimensions: Perception, Understanding, Reasoning, and Evidence-grounded Judgment. The benchmark comprises 1,457 multiple-choice questions across 14 tasks, derived from real-world matching pairs and verified by two domain experts. Across ten recent MLLMs, overall scores range from 47.1% to 86.0%. Understanding receives the highest mean score and Judgment the lowest, with weaknesses in visual difference localization and variant-level rule judgment. Modality ablations show that models benefit from multimodal evidence but can incorrectly identify conflicts between matching records. In direct matching, most models recognize matching pairs more reliably than non-matching pairs at the base level, whereas the reverse holds at the variant level. PURE therefore reveals what aggregate matching accuracy hides: not only whether models judge a pair correctly, but where they struggle to interpret evidence and apply matching criteria.

</details>


### [61] [SandBAR: A Band-Asymmetric Architecture for Underwater Image Restoration with Removable Frequency Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22355)
*Yanzi Liu,Haoran Hou,Jiachi Tong,Shen Chen,Shuang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SandBAR，一种面向水下图像复原的统一"频带非对称"架构，在模块、编码器与目标函数三个层级围绕频带非对称组织复原流程；其中SPARK模块对低频带做大感受野通道感知校正、对高频带做轻量局部精修，并配合低频引导路径、三尺度空间-频率监督与可移除的PaST训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下光散射、吸收与浑浊造成互补的频带退化：大尺度、通道相关的表观失真与局部高频细节丢失。这种频带非对称性意味着全局颜色复原与局部结构复原需要不同的处理方式。

Method: 提出SandBAR统一频带非对称架构：(1) 频带非对称三明治块，预留注意力后修复槽位；(2) SPARK（Split-band Parallel Asymmetric Repair Kernel）模块，低频带做通道感知大支撑校正，高频带做轻量局部精修；(3) 可学习低频引导路径，将输入低通视图注入深层编码器，并以三尺度空间-频率目标在解码器全程监督；(4) Post-attention Scaffold Training (PaST)，利用预留槽位在训练时施加频带特定容量、推理时移除非线性"脚手架"。

Result: 在EUVP / LSUI / UIEB上分别达到29.89 / 28.92 / 22.77 dB，在该基准族上取得新的三域平均SOTA，超过最强已发表结果约1.0 dB；PaST在推理时不增加参数与FLOPs，并保持同等延迟。

Conclusion: 将频带非对称作为设计原则贯穿块级、编码器级与目标级，可有效同时恢复全局颜色与精细结构；可移除的脚手架训练能在零推理开销下提升性能。

Abstract: Light scattering, absorption and turbidity degrade underwater imagery through complementary frequency tendencies: large-scale, channel-dependent appearance distortions and local high-frequency detail loss. This band asymmetry motivates different forms of restoration for global colour and local structure. Our primary contribution is SandBAR, a novel unified band-asymmetric architecture for underwater image restoration. Rather than treating frequency processing as an isolated module, SandBAR organises the restoration pipeline around band asymmetry at the block, encoder and objective levels. Within this architecture, we introduce a novel band-asymmetric sandwich block with a reserved post-attention repair slot and the Split-band Parallel Asymmetric Repair Kernel (SPARK), a novel module that applies channel-aware large-support correction to the low band, which predominantly carries large-scale appearance distortions, and lightweight local refinement to the detail-bearing high band, thereby restoring global colour while preserving fine structure. To deliver the global cast to the depth at which the network represents global structure, a learnable low-frequency guidance path injects a low-pass view of the input into the deep encoder, and a three-scale spatial-frequency objective supervises the same axis throughout the decoder. Building on the reserved post-attention slot, we further introduce Post-attention Scaffold Training (PaST), a novel removable nonlinear scaffolding scheme that trains the permanent SandBAR graph under band-specific capacity and runs it at inference without that capacity. SandBAR reaches 29.89 / 28.92 / 22.77 dB on EUVP / LSUI / UIEB and establishes a new three-domain mean state of the art on the benchmark family, exceeding the strongest published result by approximately 1.0 dB. PaST adds no parameters or FLOPs at inference and preserves matched latency.

</details>


### [62] [Can Vision-Language Models Truly Understand Images? A Benchmark Beyond Commonsense Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22360)
*Ling Lin,Heng Su,Xiangzeng Liu,Yang Bai,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ContraSense基准，通过构建违反常识的图像-文本对来评估MLLM是否真正基于视觉内容推理，发现最强闭源模型仅约60%准确率，远低于人类的96.04%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基准样本大多符合人类常识，模型可依赖统计共现与数据集先验而非真正理解图像内容，难以检验其预测是否"忠实于视觉证据"。

Method: 系统构建ContraSense基准，由精心筛选的反直觉图像-文本对组成，刻意违背常见预期以削弱统计偏置，迫使模型依赖视觉证据进行推理；并对多种代表性开源与闭源MLLM开展全面评测。

Result: 当前MLLM与人类存在显著差距：最强闭源模型准确率仅约60%，而人类参与者达96.04%。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM在忠实视觉理解上仍存在明显不足，需要更鲁棒、真正基于图像内容的多模态推理；基准已公开。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance on a wide range of vision-language tasks. However, it remains unclear whether their predictions are grounded in the visual content itself or are largely driven by statistical co-occurrence patterns and commonsense correlations learned from large-scale image-text corpora. Existing vision-language benchmarks predominantly consist of samples that conform to human commonsense, allowing models to achieve strong performance by exploiting dataset priors rather than genuinely understanding image content. To address this limitation, we present ContraSense, a systematically constructed benchmark for evaluating faithful vision-language understanding. ContraSense consists of carefully curated counter-intuitive image-text pairs that deliberately violate common expectations, substantially reducing the effectiveness of statistical biases and requiring models to rely on visual evidence for reasoning. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on a diverse set of representative open- and closed-source MLLMs. The results reveal a substantial performance gap between current MLLMs and humans: even the strongest closed-source model achieves only about 60\% accuracy, compared with 96.04\% for human participants. These findings demonstrate that current MLLMs still struggle with faithful visual understanding and highlight the need for more robust multimodal reasoning grounded in image content. The ContraSense benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/LingLin00/ContraSense.

</details>


### [63] [Leveraging Segmentation Representations for Efficient Classification: A Self-Configuring Framework Based on nnU-Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22380)
*Renjun Cai,Antonio Javier Gallego,Félix Fuentes-Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自配置框架，将nnU-Net工作流扩展到3D医学影像的分割与分类任务，通过复用分割模型表示进行分类，在FLARE 2026 Task 2中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在nnU-Net已有的自配置分割流程基础上，探索如何低成本、简洁地复用分割模型所学的表示来完成图像级分类，避免为分类任务单独设计复杂架构。

Method: 以分割模型的编码器特征与预测的分割概率作为输入，构建轻量分类器；比较两种信息共享策略：(1) 冻结骨干分支，特征融合感兴趣区域信息、预测前景体积与分割置信度；(2) 适配分支，对编码器末级进行部分微调，并加入辅助分割损失以保留分割相关信息。

Result: 五折实验表明更复杂的配置并未稳定提升分类性能；公开验证排行榜Overall得分为0.8056；FLARE 2026 Task 2官方最终评测中总分第一，在全部四项测试任务（含两项隐藏任务）上取得最高DSC、NSD与AUROC；本地推理满足每例60秒、4GB显存的限制。

Conclusion: 复用分割表示进行分类具有实际价值，且能在保持框架简单高效的同时取得领先性能。

Abstract: We propose a self-configuring framework that extends the nnU-Net workflow to both segmentation and classification in 3D medical imaging. Building on nnU-Net's self-configuring method, the framework reuses representations learned by a segmentation model for image-level classification. Its encoder features and predicted segmentation probabilities are used by a lightweight classifier. Two information-sharing strategies are investigated. In the frozen-backbone branch, features are augmented with region-of-interest information, predicted foreground volume, and segmentation confidence. In the adaptation branch, the final encoder stage is partially fine-tuned for classification, with an auxiliary segmentation loss to preserve segmentation-related information. Additional five-fold experiments showed that more complex configurations did not consistently improve classification performance. On the public validation leaderboard, our selected framework achieved an Overall score of 0.8056. In the official FLARE 2026 Task 2 final evaluation, our framework ranked first overall, achieving the highest DSC, NSD, and AUROC across all four testing tasks, including two hidden testing tasks. Local inference measurements also remained below the challenge limits of $60 \mathrm{s}$ per case and $4 \mathrm{GB}$ GPU memory. These results demonstrate the practical value of reusing segmentation representations for classification while maintaining a simple and efficient framework.

</details>


### [64] [CoBoVLA: Decoupling Language from Action with Discrete Codes for Robust and Interpretable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22395)
*Mehul Arora,Dhruv Agarwal,Ambuj Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoBoVLA 是 250M 参数的 VLA 模型，通过学习到的离散可解释码将语言理解、规划与控制解耦，显著提升对指令改写的鲁棒性并保持低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 在 LIBERO 上成功率高但对改写敏感（0.6B–7.5B 模型在 LIBERO-Para 下降 22–52 点，SmolVLA 从 83% 跌至 8%）；且语言理解与动作生成联合学习，难以定位失败源于误解指令还是执行错误。

Method: 提出 CoBoVLA：文本编码器将指令映射为共享步骤码，视觉规划器将其转为动作码序列，由语言无关的执行器执行；采用离散可解释码解耦三阶段，指令仅编码一次，各组件轻量。

Result: LIBERO-Goal 91.4%（SmolVLA 82.8%），单步控制延迟 17 ms；LIBERO-Para 上 4,092 条未见改写达 81.1%，匹配回合 78.5%（改写微调的 SmolVLA LoRA 仅 36.5%）；无两步演示时完成 160 个自由两步任务的 48.8%；指令正确编码时成功率 91%，否则降至 27.2%。

Conclusion: 解耦语言理解与控制可提升指令鲁棒性，并使 VLA 失败更易诊断；剩余失败主要来自语言理解阶段。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve high success rates on the LIBERO benchmark but remain sensitive to paraphrasing. Existing VLAs ranging from 0.6B to 7.5B parameters experience a 22-52 point performance drop on LIBERO-Para while SmolVLA exhibits an even larger decline, from 83\% to 8\%. Because most VLAs jointly learn language understanding and action generation, it is difficult to determine whether a failure originates from misunderstanding an instruction or executing it incorrectly. To address these challenges, we introduce CoBoVLA, or CodeBookVLA, a 250M-parameter VLA that decouples language understanding, planning, and control through learned discrete interpretable codes. A text encoder maps the instructions to shared step codes, which a visual planner then converts into action code sequences executed by a language-independent actor. This design improves robustness to instruction paraphrasing while allowing failures to be traced to individual stages. It also enables efficient real-time control on resource-constrained hardware by encoding instructions only once and using compact discrete representations to keep each component lightweight. CoBoVLA achieves 91.4\% success on LIBERO-Goal, compared with 82.8\% for SmolVLA, under a matched protocol, with 17 ms per control step latency. On LIBERO-Para, it achieves 81.1\% across 4,092 unseen paraphrases and 78.5\% on matched episodes, compared with 36.5\% for SmolVLA LoRA fine-tuned on paraphrases. Without two-step demonstrations, CoBoVLA completes 48.8\% of 160 free-form two-step tasks. Finally, success reaches 91\% when instructions are correctly encoded but falls to 27.2\% otherwise, identifying language understanding as the main source of remaining failures. Together, these results demonstrate that decoupling language understanding and control can improve instruction robustness while making VLA failures easier to diagnose.

</details>


### [65] [SignAligner: Dual-Granularity Span Generation for Sign Language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22409)
*Jongbhin Woo,Youngjoon Jang,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SignAligner，一种提示条件化的视觉-语言时段生成器，将手语视频加字幕句或目标词查询映射为起止帧区间，联合利用字幕时段与词时段训练，并用重叠度奖励微调，在 BOBSL 字幕对齐任务上取得 66.68 F1@0.50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语语料在字幕级与词级两种时间尺度上需要时间标签：字幕时段用于字幕对齐，词时段用于手语词定位。已有手语定位能在语料规模上定位词，但将字幕对齐与词时段联合训练仍缺乏探索。

Method: 构建统一的提示条件化时段生成器：输入手语视频与字幕句/目标词查询，输出文本形式的起止帧区间。两阶段训练：先同时在字幕时段与词时段（来自手语定位）上联合训练；再以基于重叠度（temporal IoU）的奖励进行微调，并保留辅助词样本。

Result: BOBSL 字幕测试集上，微调模型达 66.68 F1@0.50，为无外部手语视频资源对比系统中最佳；重叠奖励微调优于同一联合训练模型的交叉熵续训。代码、标签与最大更新预算匹配的一对生成器中，加入词时段联合训练提升字幕对齐，且重叠奖励微调后优势仍保持。对词查询，模型跟随所查询词而非视频固定位置，当词远离视频中心时显著优于仅依赖位置的预测。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用单一共享时段生成器并经重叠奖励微调的方案，可同时完成字幕对齐并为人工审核产出词时段。

Abstract: Large sign-language corpora benefit from temporal labels at two scales: subtitle spans for subtitle alignment and word spans for sign spotting. Sign spottings already locate words in time at corpus scale, but training subtitle alignment jointly on subtitle and word spans remains underexplored. We present SignAligner, a prompt-conditioned visual-language span generator that maps a sign-language video and a subtitle-sentence or target-word query to a textual start/end frame interval. It is first trained jointly on subtitle spans and word spans from sign spottings, then refined with overlap-based rewards while retaining auxiliary word examples. On the subtitle test set of the British Broadcasting Corporation-Oxford British Sign Language (BOBSL) corpus, the refined model reaches 66.68 F1@0.50 (F1 with a temporal intersection-over-union threshold of one half), the best among compared systems without external sign-video resources. This overlap-reward refinement outperforms cross-entropy continuation from the same jointly trained model. Joint training with word spans improves subtitle alignment in a pair of our generators matched in code, labels and maximum update budget. The advantage remains after both models of the pair are refined with overlap rewards. For word queries, the refined model follows the queried word, not a fixed position in the video: when the word's sign lies away from the video centre, it is far more accurate than predictions that use position alone. Together, these findings support one shared span generator refined with overlap rewards, aligning subtitles and producing word spans for human review.

</details>


### [66] [SpatialMind: Adaptive Multi-Level Geometric Fusion for Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22415)
*Shibin Zhao,Hao Tan,Fan Yang,Haixin Wang,Feilong Chen,Yidong Ma,Chaoyang Zhao,Ming Tang,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpatialMind任务自适应框架，通过门控网络动态融合多层级几何表示，解决MLLM三维空间推理中固定融合策略的不足，在VSI-Bench取得72.3的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在2D视觉理解表现良好但3D空间推理受限。现有将3D重建模型引入MLLM的方法要么在所有任务上使用单一几何粒度，要么采用固定的多层级融合方案，忽略了任务特定的推理需求。实验表明，不同认知层级的空间任务（如距离估计与路径规划）需要不同粒度的几何表示，固定融合无法满足多样化需求。

Method: 提出SpatialMind任务自适应框架。核心为GeoFus多层几何融合模块，基于同时以文本指令、视觉语义和几何表示为条件的门控网络，在LLM解码器各层自适应调节浅层、中层、深层几何特征的贡献；并引入负熵正则化损失以缓解门控坍缩。

Result: 在多个空间基准上取得有竞争力的表现，VSI-Bench达到72.3的SOTA分数；门控可视化表明GeoFus能根据任务需求自适应调整几何粒度。

Conclusion: 空间推理任务对几何粒度的需求是任务相关的，固定融合策略不可取；任务自适应、以指令与语义为条件的动态多层级几何融合能有效提升MLLM的3D空间推理能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate promising capabilities in 2D visual understanding, yet remain limited in 3D spatial reasoning. Recent studies explore integrating 3D reconstruction models into MLLMs. However, these approaches either use a single geometric granularity across all tasks or adopt a fixed multi-level fusion scheme, regardless of task-specific reasoning requirements. Through systematic experiments on multi-level geometric fusion, we find that spatial tasks at different cognitive levels (e.g., distance estimation vs. route planning) require geometric representations of different granularities, revealing that fixed fusion cannot accommodate diverse spatial reasoning requirements. To bridge this gap, we propose SpatialMind, a task-adaptive framework for dynamically integrating multi-level geometric representations. SpatialMind employs GeoFus, a multi-layer geometric fusion module built around a gating network jointly conditioned on textual instructions, visual semantics, and geometric representations. This network adaptively modulates the contributions of shallow, intermediate, and deep geometric features across the layers of the LLM decoder. Furthermore, we introduce a negative-entropy regularization loss to mitigate gate collapse. Gate visualizations indicate that GeoFus adapts geometric granularity to task requirements. Experiments demonstrate that SpatialMind achieves competitive performance across spatial benchmarks, attaining a state-of-the-art score of 72.3 on VSI-Bench. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/zhaosb-star/SpatialMind.

</details>


### [67] [CutAgent: Creative-Gene-Driven Planning and Experience-Guided Self-Refinement for High-CTR E-commerce Advertising Video Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22418)
*Xuyue Huang,Guanbo Huang,Jiasong Feng,Run Ling,Ao Ma,Xi Zhao,Yaoyu Li,Jie Gao,Ke Cao,Wei Feng,Zhanjie Zhang,Hao Wang,Jinghe Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CutAgent将电商广告视频创作建模为"规划—执行—自进化"过程，用GeneAgent生成"创意基因"（叙事单元+制作规则）指导VideoAgent完成分镜脚本与多模态制作，并通过长期记忆复用失败—修正经验，线上CTR达2.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将商品属性直接映射为脚本或剪辑决策，未显式建模广告策略、叙事结构与制作风格，且难以复用过往制作失败经验。

Method: 双智能体框架：GeneAgent将文本属性与商品图像等多模态输入映射为结构化"创意基因"（叙事单元与制作规则），底层构建约70K样本的CutAgent-70K数据集，采用高CTR监督微调加CTR引导的GRPO训练；VideoAgent以该基因为先验，从长期记忆中检索"类目—基因"两级失败—修正经验作为约束，完成分镜脚本规划，并协调素材检索、旁白、字幕、音乐与时间线渲染；另有反思回路诊断缺陷、修订决策并把验证过的修正写回记忆。

Result: 在CutAgent-Eval上优于代表性基线，取得最高线上CTR 2.21%。

Conclusion: 以显式创意基因桥接商品理解与视频生产、并引入可累积的失败—修正记忆，可有效提升电商广告视频的创作质量与实际投放效果。

Abstract: E-commerce advertising video creation requires both high-level creative planning and reliable multi-modal production. However, existing methods often map product attributes directly to scripts or editing decisions without explicitly modeling advertising strategies, narrative structures, and production styles. They also have limited ability to reuse past production failures. To address these limitations, we propose $\textbf{CutAgent}$, a dual-agent framework that formulates advertising video creation as a Plan--Execute--Self-Evolve process. CutAgent comprises two collaborative agents, $\textbf{GeneAgent}$ and $\textbf{VideoAgent}$, connected by a structured creative gene that bridges product understanding and video production. GeneAgent maps multi-modal product inputs---textual attributes together with product images---to creative genes that specify narrative units and production rules. To train GeneAgent, we construct $\textbf{CutAgent-70K}$ with approximately 70K samples of product attributes/images, reverse-extracted creative genes, cleaned clip pools, and real-world CTR, and apply high-CTR supervised fine-tuning followed by CTR-guided GRPO.
