Guided by the generated gene, VideoAgent retrieves category-then-gene failure--correction experiences from a long-term memory as constraints for shot-script planning, then coordinates material retrieval, narration, subtitles, music, and timeline rendering. Independently, a reflection loop diagnoses quality defects, revises deficient production decisions, and writes verified corrections back to memory for later retrieval. Experiments on $\textbf{CutAgent-Eval}$ show that CutAgent outperforms representative baselines in e-commerce advertising video creation, achieving the highest online CTR of $\textbf{2.21\%}$.

</details>


### [68] [Do Diffusion Models Learn to Generalize Basic Visual Skills?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22427)
*Amish Sethi,Boya Zeng,Wenhao Chai,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Visual Generative Lab (VGL)，一个通过规则化合成数据可控地测量扩散模型基础视觉技能习得与泛化能力的实验框架，并据此发现外推能力有限、组合泛化取决于技能组合多样性而非数据规模、技能间大多独立失败（位置除外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估手段（人类偏好、提示词遵循基准、FID/IS 等分布级指标）无法区分模型是真正学到了基本视觉技能，还是仅仅拟合了训练数据模式，因此需要一个可控、可精确度量的泛化评估框架。

Method: 构建 VGL 框架：针对尺寸、位置、旋转、计数、颜色、形状及其组合等基础视觉技能，依据显式规则生成合成训练数据，并设计任务特定、基于规则的精确指标；在受控条件下从零训练扩散模型进行系统研究。

Result: (1) 默认设置下外推受限：尺寸与位置超出训练范围几个像素即退化，计数在外推区间准确率仅约 50%，旋转外推准确率随随机种子波动很大；(2) 组合泛化中，不同基础技能组合的数量比数据集规模更重要；(3) 各技能大多独立失败，位置技能除外。

Conclusion: 分布级指标与偏好评估掩盖了模型对基础视觉规则的真实掌握程度；可控合成基准为诊断扩散模型的技能级泛化提供了有效工具，也表明当前模型在系统性外推与组合泛化上仍存在明显短板。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-quality images, but how well they generalize is hard to measure. Current evaluations rely on human preference and prompt-following benchmarks for text-to-image models and on distribution-level metrics such as Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and Inception Score (IS) for class-conditional models, but these cannot tell whether models learn basic visual skills or largely match training patterns. To address this gap, we introduce the Visual Generative Lab (VGL), a controlled experimental framework for studying how diffusion models learn and generalize basic visual skills, including size, position, rotation, count, color, shape, and their compositions. By generating synthetic training data for each skill from explicit rules, VGL allows precise measurement of generalization with task-specific, rule-based metrics. Training models from scratch on these data reveals three patterns in this controlled setting: (1) with our default design, extrapolation is limited: size and position degrade within a few pixels of the training range, count reaches only about 50% accuracy on out-of-range counts, and rotation extrapolation accuracy varies widely across seeds; (2) for compositional generalization, the number of distinct basic visual skill combinations matters more than dataset size; (3) skills mostly fail independently, except position. Code and data-generation scripts: https://github.com/AmishSethi/visual-generative-lab.

</details>


### [69] [When Neural Collapse Fails to Route: Routing Drift in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22429)
*Nicolas Larue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将渐进式神经坍缩分类器的类增量学习失败归因于“路由漂移”（routing drift），并提出轻量级事后分类器可靠性校准路由（RCR）来修复全局路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进式神经坍缩（NC）分类器能学到强的任务内决策边界，却难以完成全局类增量（Class-IL）决策；同时小回放缓冲区会因样本被反复优化而掩盖该问题，使失败难以被准确诊断。

Method: 1) 形式化“路由漂移”：在扩张的等角紧框架（ETF）分类器下，样本在真实任务块内可分，但其全局最大得分来自其他块；2) 直接从全局类别argmax定义路由准确率，将任务内判别能力与跨任务得分可比性分离；3) 提出RCR：保留全局ETF框架，在传输后类别度量偏离NC理想时加入类局部可靠性证据，将标准化ETF对齐与围绕NC收缩类代表的径向合理性组合，作为事后分类器且无需重训表征。

Result: 在三个数据集、两种固定回放缓冲区预算下，RCR在其中五种设置的Class-IL FAA最优，并在全部六种设置上降低Class-IL遗忘；增益集中于较难路由场景，最高较参考方法提升+6.75 FAA，配对评测中最高提升+8.03 FAA和+8.62最终路由准确率，同时保持Task-IL性能。

Conclusion: 路由漂移是ETF类持续学习的一种可测量失效模式；可靠性校准的类证据可在不重训表征的前提下修复部分丢失的全局路由。

Abstract: Progressive neural-collapse classifiers can learn strong task-local decision boundaries while failing the global Class-IL decision. We identify the failure as routing drift: under an expanding equiangular tight frame (ETF) classifier, a held-out sample can remain separable within its true task block, yet receive its largest global score from another block. This produces high Task-IL accuracy but low Class-IL accuracy, and small replay buffers can mask the problem because stored samples are optimized repeatedly and often remain well routed. We formalize route accuracy directly from the global class argmax and use it to separate within-task discrimination from cross-task score comparability. This decomposition suggests a classifier-level correction: preserve the global ETF frame, but add class-local reliability evidence when transported class measures deviate from the neural-collapse ideal. We develop Reliability-Calibrated Routing (RCR), a lightweight post-hoc classifier that composes standardized ETF alignment with radial plausibility around transported, NC-shrunk class representatives. Across three datasets and two fixed replay-buffer budgets, this NC-consistent correction gives the best Class-IL FAA on five of six settings and reduces Class-IL forgetting on all six. The largest gains occur in harder routing regimes: up to $+6.75$ FAA over the reported reference, and up to $+8.03$ FAA and $+8.62$ final route accuracy in paired evaluations, while preserving Task-IL performance. These results position routing drift as a measurable failure mode of ETF-based continual learning and show that reliability-calibrated class evidence can repair part of the lost global routing without retraining the representation. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://towzeur.github.io/RCR/.

</details>


### [70] [Where Demand Meets Supply: Spatial Elastic Consensus for Low-Bandwidth Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22439)
*Linshan Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Spatial Elastic Consensus（SEC），一种带宽自适应的协同感知框架，以双边供需共识分配token预算，并通过"先精炼后掩码"流水线保证严格带宽合规，在64KiB预算下于OPV2V、V2XSet和DAIR-V2X上取得最佳AP@0.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单车3D感知受遮挡和传感器视野限制，协同感知可缓解但受限于车联网通信预算。现有空间选择器多为发送端驱动，把稀缺带宽浪费在自车已可见的目标上；且在掩码后再卷积会使孤立token扩散到邻域，隐性违反低带宽约束。

Method: SEC以双边需求—供给共识为核心：自车构建内容感知的需求先验，各协作者评估本地供给，通过单轮共识将token预算分配给自车遗漏的关键证据，且不传输稠密图。为保证预算精确合规，采用anti-dilation的refine-before-mask流水线，在离散token选择前先精炼特征以避免感受野泄漏；再用轻量可变形采样器以学习到的局部偏移聚合稀疏消息，补偿token选择损失的空间上下文并缓解跨车姿态漂移。

Result: 在严格64KiB预算下，SEC在仿真数据集OPV2V、V2XSet及真实数据集DAIR-V2X上均持续取得最佳AP@0.7。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将双边共识与预算精确传输相结合，是实现高效低带宽协同感知的关键。

Abstract: Occlusions and limited sensor range make ego-only 3D perception insufficient for safe driving. Collaborative perception addresses this by enabling connected vehicles to share complementary observations, yet it remains constrained by tight vehicular communication budgets. While spatial selection is more flexible than fixed-rate feature compression, existing selectors remain largely sender-driven and waste scarce bandwidth on ego-visible targets. Furthermore, applying convolutions after masking spreads isolated tokens into their neighborhoods, silently violating low-bandwidth constraints. To address this, we propose Spatial Elastic Consensus (SEC), a bandwidth-adaptive framework centered on bilateral demand-supply consensus. Instead of unilateral token selection, the ego vehicle formulates a content-aware demand prior while each collaborator evaluates its local supply. This single-round consensus allocates the token budget to critical ego-missed evidence rather than ego-visible redundancy, without transmitting dense maps. To guarantee exact budget compliance, an anti-dilation refine-before-mask pipeline refines features prior to discrete token selection, preventing receptive-field leakage. A lightweight deformable sampler then aggregates these sparse messages with learned local offsets, compensating for the spatial context lost to token selection and mitigating inter-agent pose drift. Evaluated at a stringent 64KiB budget, SEC consistently achieves the best AP@0.7 on the simulated OPV2V and V2XSet datasets and the real-world DAIR-V2X dataset. These results demonstrate that combining bilateral consensus with budget-exact transmission is key to efficient low-bandwidth collaborative perception.

</details>


### [71] [As Student Rollouts Continue, Visual Backdoors Keep Growing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22446)
*Zane Fu,Yanzhao Zheng,Jinze Liu,Ziting Qin,Qianshan Wei,Jihuai Zhu,Yipeng Yu,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示On-Policy Distillation (OPD)中存在隐蔽的视觉后门传播风险：低投毒率下后门几乎不显现，但超过临界阈值后攻击成功率骤升至近100%；传播不依赖显式恶意回答，而由教师分配异常概率质量的持续累积驱动，并据此提出零显式触发注入的触发设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏中教师后门可传给学生，而OPD作为VLM重要后训练范式，其安全性研究不足。由于OPD不需教师生成回答、仅用教师概率分布监督学生在线生成的轨迹，且初始学生生成良性回答、无恶意目标回答监督，直觉上似具抗后门传播能力；本文旨在挑战该直觉并揭示更隐蔽的风险。

Method: 在多个模型家族与数据集上开展实验并结合理论分析，考察OPD中视觉后门的传播规律；分析后门传播与教师概率质量分配的关系；据此设计新型触发器。

Result: 三点发现：(1) 与传统离线蒸馏的渐进传播不同，OPD在低投毒率下后门很少出现，但越过临界阈值后ASR迅速逼近100%；(2) 传播不依赖显式恶意回答，而由教师持续累积的异常概率质量驱动；(3) 基于上述机制提出零显式触发注入即可实现视觉后门传播的触发设计。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了OPD中被忽视的后门传播机制，凸显其潜在安全风险，并为该后训练范式的安全性评估与防御提供了新视角。

Abstract: Conventional knowledge distillation can facilitate the propagation of backdoors from teacher models to student models. However, how this risk manifests in On-Policy Distillation (OPD) remains insufficiently studied. OPD has recently emerged as an important post-training paradigm for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Unlike traditional offline distillation, OPD does not require teacher-generated responses. Instead, it uses the teacher's probability distributions to supervise response trajectories generated online by the student. Since the initial student model typically generates benign responses and receives no explicit supervision from malicious target responses, OPD appears to offer some inherent resistance to backdoor propagation.
  In this paper, we challenge this intuition and reveal a more subtle risk of visual backdoor propagation in OPD. Through experiments across multiple model families and datasets, together with theoretical analysis, we identify three main findings. First, unlike the relatively gradual propagation observed in traditional offline distillation, backdoors rarely emerge in OPD at low poisoning ratios. However, the attack success rate rapidly approaches 100% once a critical threshold is exceeded. Second, backdoor propagation does not depend on explicit malicious responses. Instead, it is driven by the continuous accumulation of anomalous probability mass assigned by the teacher. Finally, based on these findings, we propose a novel trigger design that enables visual backdoor propagation with zero explicit trigger injection. These findings further reveal an overlooked mechanism of backdoor propagation in OPD and highlight its potential security risks.

</details>


### [72] [MedImgAnnotator: batch wise medical image annotation and preprocessing tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22450)
*Ewunate Assaye Kassaw,Amit Mehndiratta,Peush Sahni,Anup Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出半自动化批量标注工具MedImgAnnotator，将主动标注时间从约206.5秒降至59秒，并显著提升易用性，适用于大队列医学影像标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模队列数据集标注/修订AI预测标签耗时巨大；现有开源标注工具导航步骤复杂、不支持批处理，效率低。

Method: 集成编辑工具、图像-掩膜配对逻辑、预处理函数、目标检测工具和插件管理系统，构建简易快速的半自动批量标注工具；在开源MR与CT扫描上，由开发者及12名用户评估主动标注时间与易用性，并实际用于MICCAI 2026 CARE Challenge肝脏分割的6412例MRI体数据标注。

Result: 主动标注时间由206.5±32.87秒降至59秒；12名用户评估的Wilcoxon符号秩检验显示标注时间P=0.032、易用性评分P=0.008，均显著改善；跨模态、跨部位ROI标注验证了通用性，并被该挑战赛最佳成绩团队采用。

Conclusion: MedImgAnnotator显著降低大影像队列的主动标注时间并提升易用性，支持跨模态与多ROI场景，以可执行文件和Python脚本形式开放获取。

Abstract: The curation of large cohort datasets for developing deep learning models demands annotating or editing over the AI-predicted labels. However, openly available annotation tools are time-consuming, as they involve complex navigational steps and do not support batch processing. This study aimed to develop a simple, fast, semiautomated batch-annotation tool named MedImgAnnotator. The tool was created by integrating editing tools, image mask pair matching logic, preprocessing functions, object detection tool, and a plugin management system. Its performance was evaluated in terms of active annotation time and ease of use by the developer and 12 users using open-source MR and CT scans. Additionally, the tool had been used by the MICCAI 2026 CARE Challenge liver segmentation best performance winner to annotate liver masks from 6412 MRI volumes. The evaluation results showed that MedImgAnnotator reduced active annotation time from 206.5+/-32.87 (average active annotation time of openly available tools) seconds to 59 seconds. The Wilcoxon signed-rank test of 12 users evaluation results recorded P values of 0.032 and 0.008 for the active annotation time and higher ease of use rating metrics, respectively, indicating that the current tool significantly reduces annotation time and enhances ease of use. Moreover, the tool utilization for annotating different regions of interest from different modality scans and body regions shows its usability across modalities and areas of interest. MedImgAnnotator, which significantly reduces active annotation time for large imaging cohorts, is available as an executable file and as a Python script. Access link: https://ewunateassaye.github.io/developed-tools/medimgannotator.html

</details>


### [73] [Sample-Aligned Decoders for Latent Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22458)
*Yu Lei,Yi Li,Hongcheng Gao,Yipeng Wei,Liang Li,Li Su,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 潜生成模型的解码器仅在编码器潜变量上训练，测试时却解码生成潜变量，导致生成残差直接渲染进图像。作者发现生成潜变量虽贴近编码器潜流形但分布不同，扰动鲁棒化假设不成立，因而提出仅微调解码器的后训练框架SAD：用冻结基础模型特征空间做生成样本分布对齐（无配对监督），并用编码器锚点保持防止潜空间漂移，推理零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器训练与测试的潜变量分布不匹配：解码器从未见过测试时解码的生成潜变量，生成器残差因此直接进入最终图像。现有方案需重训生成器、增加每步采样开销，或假设生成误差类似对真实潜变量的扰动并据此鲁棒化解码器；但实验表明生成潜变量与真实潜变量在编码器潜流形上的接近程度相当，却服从不同分布，上述假设不成立，需针对生成潜变量分布调整解码器。

Method: SAD为后训练框架，冻结生成器、仅微调解码器，推理零额外成本，应对两大挑战：(1) 无配对监督——生成潜变量无真值图像；(2) 潜空间漂移——脱离配对目标后解码器会遗忘须解码的潜空间。具体含两组件：生成样本分布对齐（Generated-Sample Distribution Alignment）在冻结基础模型特征空间中将解码后的生成潜变量与无配对真实图像对齐；编码器锚点保持（Encoder Anchor Preservation）约束解码器在编码器潜变量上贴近原始解码器。分析表明对齐仅在生成潜变量上有效，其修正量与生成器相对编码器产生该潜变量的超额频率成正比；在编码器潜变量上对齐只与锚点相互抵消。

Result: 在三种分词器、四个SOTA ImageNet生成器上均有提升，如REPA-E的FID-50K由1.15降至0.98；与判别器引导（discriminator guidance）互补，并可扩展至文生图生成。

Conclusion: 解码器应适配生成潜变量的分布而非仅在编码器潜变量上重构；通过分布对齐加锚点保持的纯解码器后训练，可在不改变生成器、不增加推理成本的前提下系统性降低生成残差造成的图像误差，且与现有采样期引导手段正交。

Abstract: Latent generative models synthesize images by decoding generated latents, yet their decoder is trained only to reconstruct encoder latents and never sees the latents it decodes at test time, so the residual errors of the generator are rendered directly into the final image. Existing remedies retrain the generator, add cost at every sampling step, or make the decoder robust to perturbed real latents, assuming that generator errors resemble such perturbations. We find that they do not. Generated latents lie on the encoder latent manifold as closely as real ones, yet follow a different distribution. The decoder should therefore be adapted to the distribution of generated latents, which raises two challenges: (1) unpaired supervision, since a generated latent has no ground-truth image, and (2) latent-space drift, since a decoder freed from paired targets forgets the latent space it must decode. We propose Sample-Aligned Decoders (SAD), a post-training framework that fine-tunes only the decoder of a frozen generator at no inference cost. Generated-Sample Distribution Alignment matches decoded generated latents to unpaired real images in a frozen foundation-model feature space, and Encoder Anchor Preservation keeps the decoder close to the original one on encoder latents. Our analysis shows that the alignment is effective only on generated latents, where it corrects each sample in proportion to how much more often the generator produces such latents than the encoder; on encoder latents it can only work against the anchor. SAD improves four state-of-the-art ImageNet generators across three tokenizers, e.g., FID-50K from 1.15 to 0.98 for REPA-E, complements discriminator guidance, and extends to text-to-image generation. Our code and models are available at https://torpedo2003.github.io/SAD/.

</details>


### [74] [MAVE: Reconstructing Editable 3D Blockouts from Film Shots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22464)
*Yifan Chang,Jian Yang,Jiangnan Shao,Xiaofeng Wang,Zhaoxin Fan,Renjing Xu,Ming Lu,Xingang Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAVEBench基准与MAVE视频到3D预演（blockout）框架，通过对齐"度量观测-智能体建模-证据核验"，低成本生成可编辑Blender场景，并发布3,000对训练数据集MAVEData-3K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D动画blockout是预演与视频生成的结构化条件，但从参考视频手工重建成本高昂，既制约创作者使用，也导致成对训练数据稀缺。

Method: MAVE由三部分组成：(1) Metric观测，在统一度量坐标系中恢复相机位姿、人体运动与几何证据；(2) Agentic建模，智能体在观测约束下构建可编辑环境；(3) 基于证据的VErification，用程序化检查报告不一致以引导迭代精化。输出为可编辑Blender场景与逐帧对齐的blockout视频。

Result: 在50个镜头、416个关键帧的MAVEBench上，三种智能体相比vanilla基线总体得分提升27.5–41.1分；DeepSeek-1017配置下得分73.2，平均API成本仅0.054美元/镜头，超过最强vanilla基线，重建总时长减少53%。

Conclusion: 该工作以自动化、可验证的管线实现低成本高质量视频到blockout重建，并提供MAVEData-3K训练数据；创作者可修改视角与角色路径并渲染编辑结果作为视频生成条件。

Abstract: Animated 3D blockouts support previsualization and provide structural conditions for video generation, but manually reconstructing them from reference videos is costly. This limits both their use by creators and the production of paired training data. We introduce MAVEBench, a benchmark of 50 film shots and 416 keyframes for evaluating camera motion, spatial layout, and character blocking. We propose MAVE, a video-to-blockout framework combining Metric observation, Agentic modeling, and evidence-based VErification. It first recovers cameras, human motion, and geometric evidence in a shared metric coordinate system. An agent then constructs an editable environment under constraints from these observations, while programmatic checks report inconsistencies to guide refinement. The outputs are editable Blender scenes and frame-aligned blockout videos. On MAVEBench, MAVE improves overall scores by 27.5-41.1 points across three agents over their corresponding vanilla baselines. With DeepSeek-1017, it achieves a score of 73.2 at an average API cost of only USD 0.054 per shot, outperforming the strongest vanilla baseline while reducing total reconstruction time by 53%. We also construct MAVEData-3K, a dataset of 3,000 blockout-video pairs for model training, and will release it publicly. Creators can change viewpoints and character paths, then render the edited scenes to condition video generation. Code is available at https://github.com/yfChang-cv/MAVE.

</details>


### [75] [Disagree to Prune: Reallocating Visual Tokens Where a Small VLM Dissents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22472)
*Yanchao Li,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Yusu Zhang,Beilun Wang,Yanbo Wang,Xiaohua Xu,Zhongyu Huang,Tianfan Fu,Nanqing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRUDENT：先用约一半视觉token预算作答，再将节省的token用于以双倍预算重答"小VLM看全图后与剪枝答案不一致"的题目，在同等平均预算下优于八种剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法主要改进token排序，并对所有问题使用相同预算一次性作答。由于排序较强，剪枝只影响少数问题，且基于保留token计算的信号难以察觉被删除的信息。作者发现：在多数此类问题上，一个小VLM读取完整图像后的答案与剪枝答案不一致，说明裁剪答案仍缺失证据。

Method: 提出PRUDENT两阶段流程：第一阶段以约一半token预算作答；用小VLM在全图下的答案作为不一致检测器，将存在分歧的问题在第二阶段以双倍预算重答，从而自适应地分配token预算。论文还分析了重答何时值得、以及小VLM为何应读取全图。

Result: 在18个基准上，PRUDENT在相同平均视觉token预算下优于八种现有剪枝方法；预算越紧优势越明显，并在五种InternVL规模及另两个模型家族中保持领先。小VLM的分歧信号能标记八种方法的大多数剪枝失败案例；即使对两个最强方法配合其自有排序进行重答也能提升。其他检查器亦可获得类似增益，且额外token恰好流向首轮出错的问题。

Conclusion: 识别哪些剪枝答案可信，为视觉token剪枝提供了超越token排序的第二条杠杆；将预算自适应地再分配给高风险问题是一种有效且通用的策略。

Abstract: Visual token pruning speeds up large vision-language models (VLMs) by keeping only a part of their visual tokens. Most existing methods improve the ranking of the tokens and then answer every question once with the same budget. With strong rankings, pruning hurts only a few questions, and signals computed on the kept tokens see little of what it removed. We find that, on most of these questions, a small VLM given the full image disagrees with the pruned answer, since it still sees the missing evidence. We propose PRUDENT, which first answers with about half the budget and spends the saved tokens on re-answering the disagreeing questions at twice the budget. We show when such re-answering pays and why the small VLM should read the full image. On 18 benchmarks, PRUDENT outperforms eight existing pruning methods at the same average budget of visual tokens. Its lead widens as the budget tightens, and holds across five InternVL sizes. PRUDENT also has the highest average in two more model families. The disagreement of the small VLM flags most pruning failures of all eight methods, and re-answering on it also improves the two strongest with their own rankings. Other checkers give similar gains, and the extra tokens go where the first pass hurts the answer. These results suggest that identifying which pruned answers are trustworthy provides an additional lever for visual token pruning beyond the ranking of tokens. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/PRUDENT.

</details>


### [76] [Fast and Strong Image Protection against Diffusion Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22494)
*Jiwon Park,Jihye Kang,Namhyuk Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出保护性扩散模型 PDM，一次训练即可为新图像生成扰动，以约十分之一成本接近迭代教师方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化文生图扩散模型仅凭少量照片即可复现人物身份，现有图像保护方法需对每张图从头优化扰动，成本随图像数量线性增长。

Method: 训练一个扩散模型，经多步去噪采样得到新图像的扰动；先用蒸馏方式学习迭代式教师模型，再以目标残差投影（TRP）损失作为奖励进行后训练。

Result: 在人脸与艺术作品上优于九个基线方法，效果接近教师模型，而成本约为其十分之一。

Conclusion: 将逐图优化转为一次性训练的生成式扰动器，可实现低成本、可扩展的身份保护，TRP 奖励优于既有目标。

Abstract: Personalized text-to-image diffusion models can reproduce a person from a handful of photographs. Image protection perturbs each image before publication so that fine-tuning on it fails, but it optimizes every perturbation from scratch, so its cost grows with the number of images. We present the protective diffusion model (PDM), which is trained once and samples the perturbation of a new image over many denoising steps. PDM distills an iterative teacher and is then post-trained with the target residual projection (TRP) loss as a reward, a stronger objective than existing ones. PDM outperforms nine baselines on faces and artworks and nearly matches its teacher at a tenth of the cost.

</details>


### [77] [Motion-Aware Reward Modeling for Instruction-Guided Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22495)
*Li Li,Xiangbo Gao,Bo Ni,Samyadeep Basu,Viet Dac Lai,Jing Shi,Seunghyun Yoon,Gang Wu,Yu Wang,Thien Huu Nguyen,Tyler Derr,Franck Dernoncourt,Zhengzhong Tu,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示指令引导视频编辑的奖励模型存在"运动塌缩"：偏好时间一致性而牺牲运动。在VE-Bench上，复合奖励在97%情况下偏好被抑制运动的候选；线性探针接近随机水平。作者提出运动感知奖励模型，将RGB帧差流与针对"抑制运动/伪造运动"负样本的偏好训练结合，并以蒸馏锚保持与人类判断对齐。结果将选择运动抑制候选的比例降至0%，与人类评分的Spearman相关为0.57，并能检测未见抑制变体、在两条编辑流水线上保持运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑奖励模型偏向时间一致性，导致"运动塌缩"——奖励随运动减少而升高，且该缺陷被潜变量优化与奖励引导微调所利用，损害编辑质量。

Method: 用真实编辑构造受控的运动抑制变体来诊断该失效；提出运动感知奖励模型：RGB帧差流显式建模帧间动态，配合针对运动抑制与伪造运动负样本的偏好训练，并加入蒸馏锚以对齐人类质量判断。

Result: 在VE-Bench运动阶梯上，选择运动抑制候选的比例由97%降至0%，与人类质量评分的Spearman相关达0.57；可检测未见的抑制变体，并在两条编辑流水线的奖励引导微调中保持运动。

Conclusion: 视频编辑奖励模型需显式建模运动；引入帧间动态输入与针对运动负样本的偏好训练可有效缓解运动塌缩。

Abstract: Reward models for instruction-guided video editing can favor temporal consistency at the expense of motion. We study this failure, termed \emph{motion collapse}, using controlled motion-suppression variants of real edits. On VE-Bench, the evaluated composite reward prefers a motion-suppressed candidate in $97\%$ of cases. Learned video rewards and a VLM judge also struggle with mild suppression, while latent optimization and reward-guided fine-tuning exploit this weakness to increase reward as motion declines. A linear probe of our base reward backbone performs near chance on mild suppression, motivating an explicit inter-frame dynamics input. We propose a motion-aware reward model that combines an RGB frame-difference stream with preference training against motion-suppressed and fabricated-motion negatives. A distillation anchor preserves alignment with human quality judgments. On the VE-Bench motion ladder, our model reduces the rate of selecting motion-suppressed candidates to $0\%$ while achieving a Spearman correlation of $0.57$ with human quality ratings. It also detects unseen suppression variants and preserves motion during reward-guided fine-tuning on two editing pipelines. These results support explicitly modeling motion in rewards for video editing.

</details>


### [78] [Efficient Self-Supervised Adaptation of 3D Abdominal Vision-Language Model for Institution-Specific HCC Classification via Full Fine-Tuning and PEFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22504)
*Febryan Putra Kartika,Cheng-Yu Ma,Ying-Jia Lin,Chi-Tung Cheng,Kuan-Fu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将预训练3D医学视觉-语言模型迁移至外机构腹部CT的HCC分类任务，采用CT-报告对比学习完成自监督适配，对比全量微调与PEFT（视觉端ConvLoRA、文本端LoRA），PEFT仅更新约1%参数即达可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM虽能建模图像-文本跨模态关系，但其向机构特定临床任务的适配仍研究不足；跨机构影像采集特性差异带来域偏移，同时缺乏病灶级标注与分类标签，需探索低成本、少数据条件下的适配策略。

Method: 以配对的腹部CT与放射报告为监督信号，对预训练3D医学VLM进行CT-报告对比学习微调，无需病灶级标注或HCC分类标签；比较全量微调与PEFT两种策略，其中图像编码器使用ConvLoRA、文本编码器使用LoRA；并分析对比学习温度初始化的影响。

Result: 在跨机构域偏移下两种策略均取得较强下游性能：PEFT的AUC为0.79、F1为0.70；全量微调AUC为0.82、F1为0.71，均未超过监督式HCC分类方法，但相对预训练模型有提升。全量微调收敛快但数个epoch内即过拟合；PEFT以约1%参数更新量获得可比性能，但需更多训练步。对比温度初始化显著影响分类性能。

Conclusion: 3D医学VLM可通过自监督的CT-报告对比学习高效适配机构特定的HCC分类任务；全量微调与PEFT各有实际权衡，PEFT在参数效率与泛化稳定性上更具优势，温度等超参初始化需谨慎设置。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) capture cross-modal relationships between image and text, yet their adaptation to institution-specific clinical tasks remains underexplored. In this study, we fine-tuned a pretrained 3D medical VLM for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) classification using paired abdominal CT scans and radiology reports from a different institution with acquisition characteristics that differ from the model's original pretraining corpus. We compared two adaptation strategies: full fine-tuning and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), motivated by the common use of PEFT to reduce computational cost and enable adaptation under limited-data constraints. Both approaches achieve strong downstream HCC classification performance despite the cross-institutional domain shift, with PEFT reaching an AUC of 0.79 and F1 of 0.70, and full fine-tuning achieving an AUC of 0.82 and F1 of 0.71. Although these results remain below the performance reported by supervised HCC classification approaches, our method achieves improvements over the pretrained model through CT-report contrastive learning without requiring lesion-level annotations or HCC classification labels as training targets. Full fine-tuning converges rapidly but overfits within a few epochs, whereas PEFT (ConvLoRA for the image encoder and LoRA for the text encoder) attains comparable performance while updating approximately 1% of the model parameters, although requiring more training steps. We also examine the role of contrastive temperature, observing that temperature initialization significantly affects classification performance. This study demonstrates that a 3D medical VLM can be efficiently adapted to institution-specific HCC classification using self-supervised CT-report contrastive learning, while highlighting the practical trade-offs between full fine-tuning and parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [79] [EfficientVPR: Toward Efficient Visual Place Recognition via Scene-Aware Prompt Tuning and Adaptive Feature Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22506)
*Wenjing Tang,Chuanguang Yang,Zhulin An,Libo Huang,Yongjun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量单阶段视觉位置识别框架 EfficientVPR，通过场景感知视觉提示调优与实例相关关键局部特征增强两项创新，实现速度-精度权衡，在同规模模型中达新 SOTA 并在近 SOTA 精度下获得 73 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VPR 方法面对极端环境变化时，主要依赖扩大模型容量或计算代价高昂的重排序（reranking）阶段，导致明显的效率瓶颈，难以满足实际部署的计算约束。

Method: EfficientVPR 为轻量单阶段框架，包含两项关键设计：i) 场景感知视觉提示调优（scene-aware visual prompt tuning），以较少参数适配预训练特征并根据样本特性动态调整；ii) 实例相关关键局部特征增强模块（instance-dependent key local feature enhancement），进一步强化判别性区域。

Result: 在 Pitts250k、MSLS、Eynsham、AmsterTime 与 SVOX 上评测，方法在同尺度 DINOv2-small 模型中优于所有竞品、建立新 SOTA；相较 DINOv2-large 的两阶段 SOTA 方法，实现 73 倍加速、特征维度降低 60%，且平均 R@1 差距在 2.5% 以内。

Conclusion: 以提示调优+局部特征增强的轻量单阶段设计，可在避免重排序与大模型开销的前提下取得与重型两阶段方案接近的检索精度，说明效率与精度可有效兼顾，为实际部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Visual place recognition (VPR) faces critical challenges in handling extreme environmental variations while meeting the computational constraints of practical applications. Current methods predominantly address these challenges by either scaling up model capacity or employing computationally intensive reranking stages, creating a significant efficiency bottleneck. To overcome this limitation, we propose EfficientVPR, a lightweight one-stage framework that achieves speed-accuracy trade-offs through two key innovations: i) a scene-aware visual prompt tuning method which adapts pretrained features with less parameters while dynamically adjusting to sample-specific characteristics, and ii) an instance-dependent key local feature enhancement module that further reinforces discriminative regions. Comprehensive evaluations on Pitts250k, MSLS, Eynsham, AmsterTime and SVOX demonstrate that our method establishes a new SOTA for DINOv2-small models by outperforming all same-scale competitors, and delivers a $73\times$ speedup with 60\% lower-dimensional features while maintaining competitive (within 2.5% average R@1 gap) against the SOTA DINOv2-large-based two-stage method. Our code will be available at https://github.com/WiniTang/EfficientVPR.

</details>


### [80] [Reliability-Driven Feature Correction for Hyperspectral and LiDAR Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22508)
*Lianming Zhang,Xiaolu Wang,Qiya Song,Jinyang Liu,Renwei Dian,Xudong Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可靠性驱动的ReDF框架，通过类别证据量化各模态各空间位置的特征可靠性，并据此引导特征校正与融合，用于HSI-LiDAR地物分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSI与LiDAR在光谱与高程信息上互补，但模态特征中类别判别可靠性随空间位置而变化的现象未被显式建模，弱或模糊特征会干扰跨模态交互与融合。

Method: 提出ReDF框架：(1) 可靠性引导特征校正模块为每个模态构建类别原型库，将特征-原型相似度转换为类别证据以估计可靠性，并据此构造可靠性加权的共享表示，基于邻域一致性引导双向跨模态特征校正，用另一模态信息修正类别证据弱的特征；(2) 可靠性引导特征重加权模块以估计的可靠性和跨模态不一致性为条件生成低秩调制，作用于校正后的模态特异性描述子，自适应调整各模态对融合表示的贡献，用于最终分类。

Result: 在三个公开HSI-LiDAR数据集上，平均总体精度(OA)与Kappa系数均高于对比方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模并利用模态特征的逐位置类别可靠性，可有效抑制弱特征干扰，改善跨模态交互与融合，从而提升HSI-LiDAR地物分类性能。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) and LiDAR data provide complementary spectral and elevation information for land-cover classification. However, the spatially varying class-discriminative reliability of modality features is rarely modeled explicitly, allowing weak or ambiguous features to adversely affect cross-modal interaction and fusion. To address this issue, we propose ReDF, a reliability-driven framework that quantifies the reliability of each modality's features at each spatial location through the strength of class-related evidence they provide, and uses it to guide feature correction and fusion. Specifically, a Reliability-Guided Feature Correction module introduces a class prototype bank for each modality and converts feature-prototype similarities into class evidence for reliability estimation. The estimated reliabilities are used to construct a reliability-weighted shared representation and to guide bidirectional cross-modal feature correction based on neighborhood agreement, refining modality features with weak class evidence through information from the other modality. Building on these outputs, a Reliability-Guided Feature Reweighting module conditions a low-rank modulation on the estimated reliabilities and the cross-modal disagreement, and applies it to the corrected modality-specific descriptors to adaptively rescale each modality's contribution to the fused representation used for final classification. Experiments on three public HSI-LiDAR datasets show higher mean overall accuracy and Kappa than the compared methods.

</details>


### [81] [ATS: Anti-Aliased Triangle Splatting with Exact Per-Primitive Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22512)
*Brent Zoomers,Joni Vanherck,Leander Winters,Jeroen Put,Lode Jorissen,Nick Michiels*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Triangle Splatting窗口在像素上的积分的精确闭式解，用格林公式将积分及梯度化为每条子三角形边裁剪像素后的单项，从而实现无需重训的抗锯齿、保守剔除与多尺度训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Triangle Splatting仅在像素中心评估三角形，并以<1像素内切半径进行剔除，导致严重走样；在低分辨率下还会剔除掉场景中的大部分三角形。

Method: 推导窗口积分的闭式解：用角平分线把三角形划为三个子三角形，每个子三角形内窗口为到某条边距离的幂函数，具有闭式原函数；再用格林定理将积分及其梯度归约为子三角形边与像素裁剪后的每边一项，整个三角形的积分也有闭式形式。

Result: 精确积分可用于保守剔除（仅剔除在任何像素都无法达到alpha阈值的三角形）；无需重训即可对现有模型抗锯齿；时间走样少于2x2超采样；支持多尺度训练，在降低分辨率时匹配或超过超采样训练效果。

Conclusion: 以闭式像素积分替代中心采样与内切半径剔除，为Triangle Splatting提供了原理清晰、可微且高效的抗锯齿与多尺度渲染方案。

Abstract: Triangle Splatting represents scenes using semi-transparent triangles, but it evaluates each triangle at the pixel center and culls any triangle with an inradius smaller than one pixel. The former causes severe aliasing, while the latter culls most of the scene at lower resolutions. We show that the integral of the Triangle Splatting window over a pixel admits an exact closed-form solution. The angle bisectors split the triangle into three sub-triangles, in each of which the window is a power of the distance to one edge. Each piece therefore has a closed-form antiderivative, and Green's theorem reduces the integral and its gradients to one term per side of the sub-triangle clipped against the pixel. The integral over the whole triangle, which we show has a closed form, then allows conservative culling, removing only triangles that cannot meet the renderer's alpha cutoff at any pixel. This exact formulation anti-aliases existing models without retraining, exhibits less temporal aliasing than 2x2 supersampling, and enables multi-scale training, matching or outperforming supersampled training at reduced resolutions.

</details>


### [82] [From Perception to Simulation: Unveiling and Empowering Mental Spatial Dynamics in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22541)
*Jiachun Li,Zhuoran Jin,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MentalGrid基准，用于评估MLLM在无中间视觉反馈条件下的动态空间状态模拟能力，并基于此发现现有模型的核心缺陷，进而用三阶段课程学习框架训练出SpatialWalker，显著提升空间模拟性能并保持良好泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦静态场景中的空间关系感知，而"动态空间模拟"（需在无中间视觉反馈下推演空间状态演化）尚未被系统探索，缺少相应评测手段，模型该能力存在明显缺口。

Method: 构建MentalGrid基准，专门考察模型对演化空间状态的模拟；通过大规模评测对现有模型进行能力诊断与失效归因；随后提出三阶段课程学习框架，使用16k条专门构造的空间推理样本进行训练，得到SpatialWalker模型。

Result: 评测显示现有模型空间模拟能力显著不足：简单场景受感知能力限制，复杂度上升后受多步推理不足制约。SpatialWalker相较基座模型平均提升33.8%，在空间模拟上超过Gemini-3-Flash等闭源模型；在域外基准上仍保持4.9%的平均增益。

Conclusion: 动态空间模拟是更广泛空间智能的基础构件；针对性的课程学习可有效弥补感知与多步推理短板，且提升可迁移至域外任务，说明该能力具有基础性与可迁移价值。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental component of world understanding for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While existing research has advanced the perception of spatial relations in static scenes, the domain of dynamic spatial simulation remains relatively unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce MentalGrid, a benchmark focused on spatial reasoning tasks that require models to simulate evolving spatial states without intermediate visual feedback. Extensive evaluation on our benchmark reveals a significant deficiency in the spatial simulation capabilities of existing models. Specifically, we find that performance is constrained by perceptual limitations in simple simulation scenarios and hindered by inadequate multi-step reasoning as complexity increases. To address these challenges, we propose a three-stage curriculum learning framework utilizing 16k specialized spatial reasoning samples. This approach yields SpatialWalker, which achieves a 33.8% average improvement over its base model, surpassing proprietary models like Gemini-3-Flash in spatial simulation. Furthermore, SpatialWalker demonstrates robust generalization, maintaining a 4.9% average gain on out-of-domain benchmarks, demonstrating the pivotal role of spatial simulation as a fundamental building block for broader spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [83] [Fast Training-Free Low-Light Video Enhancement with a Frozen Image Diffusion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22551)
*Hon Ming Yam,Chun Pong Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种因果、免训练的低光照视频增强流水线，通过两个时序组件（时序输入融合、晚启动偏移传播）改造冻结的MR. Illuminate图像先验，在ZVRD分辨率下比ZVRD快261倍且五项指标均更优，在1280×720的SDSD片段上优于逐帧八步处理（PSNR+0.55 dB、LPIPS+0.034）且耗时少10%，但低分辨率下感知增益不迁移（LPIPS差0.028），0.65 fps仍属离线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练图像扩散模型可在无低光照训练数据、无需针对新相机重训的条件下增强低光照视频，但现有唯一已发表的免训练低光照视频方法ZVRD过慢（256×256单帧超过一分钟），难以实用。

Method: 在冻结的MR. Illuminate图像先验上增加两个时序组件：(1) 时序输入融合——在扩散反演前对运动补偿后的低光照帧做递归平均；(2) 晚启动偏移传播——仅在关键帧上运行完整采样轨迹，每个非关键帧只反演自身轨迹的低噪声部分，并叠加由最近关键帧传播（warp）而来的增强偏移。另用线性高斯反演模型解释观测现象。

Result: 在ZVRD自身分辨率下比ZVRD快261倍，且所报告的五项指标全部更好；在13个留出的1280×720 SDSD片段上，相比逐帧八步处理PSNR提升0.55 dB、LPIPS提升0.034，warping误差减半，耗时减少10%。模型内部测量显示：融合在反演放大噪声之前去除帧间噪声；光流误差对传输偏移的扰动比对传输状态小十倍。低分辨率（346×260）下LPIPS反而差0.028。

Conclusion: 时序输入融合与偏移传播能显著加速并改善免训练低光照视频增强，其有效性可由线性高斯反演模型解释；但感知增益不随分辨率迁移，且0.65 fps的速度使其仍为离线方法。

Abstract: Pretrained image diffusion models can enhance low-light video without low-light training data and without retraining for a new camera. ZVRD, the only published training-free method for low-light video, is too slow to use: our implementation needs over a minute per $256 \times 256$ frame. We present a causal, training-free pipeline that adds two temporal components to a frozen MR. Illuminate image prior. Temporal input fusion recursively averages motion-compensated low-light frames before diffusion inversion. Late-start offset propagation runs the full sampling trajectory only on key frames. Each non-key frame inverts the low-noise part of its own trajectory and adds an enhancement offset warped from the latest key frame. At ZVRD's own resolution the pipeline is $261\times$ faster than ZVRD and scores better on all five metrics we report. On 13 held-out SDSD clips at $1280 \times 720$, it improves on framewise eight-step processing by 0.55 dB in PSNR and 0.034 in LPIPS and halves the warping error, in 10% less time. Measurements inside the model show why, and a linear-Gaussian model of inversion explains them. Fusion removes frame-to-frame noise before inversion amplifies it. A flow error perturbs the transported offset ten times less than it perturbs a transported state. The perceptual gain does not transfer to low resolution: at $346 \times 260$, LPIPS is worse by 0.028. At 0.65 frames per second, the pipeline is an offline method.

</details>


### [84] [Strong Fused Performance Can Mask Branch Collapse in a Dual-View Heart-Sound Classifier: A Three-Part Diagnostic Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22556)
*Safayet Khan,Hasan Zohirul Islam,Md. Tauhid Bin Iqbal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在双视角心音分类中，融合模型的高分可能掩盖分支塌缩：AudioFuse的频谱图分支实际未被使用，提出的三部分诊断可检测该现象，并建议随融合分数报告分支删除与未训练对照探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合模型通常仅以融合分数评价，隐含假设每个分支都学到了有用表征。作者指出该假设在双视角心音分类中可能"静默失效"，需要一种显式的分支塌缩诊断方法。

Method: 以AudioFuse（频谱图Transformer + 波形CNN）为对象，提出三部分诊断：(1)删除某分支是否改变融合分数；(2)该分支特征上的线性探针是否优于同初始化的未训练副本；(3)该分支单独训练时能否学到其视角。辅以更新统计与初始化实验定位早期优化失衡，并通过在输入间迁移强人工标签线索验证分支依赖的可切换性。

Result: 从头联合训练时，频谱图分支未被使用但融合性能仍很强：删除该分支影响可忽略，探针相对未训练副本提升甚微；然而同架构单独训练AUC超过0.9，说明其视角本可学习，而联合训练模型在须依赖该分支时接近随机。PhysioNet上观察到分支塌缩，ICBHI与CirCor上未满足完整诊断。

Conclusion: 仅凭融合性能无法证明每个分支都学到了有意义表征；分支塌缩源于早期优化失衡而非视角不可学习。建议在报告融合分数时同时报告分支删除与未训练对照探针结果。

Abstract: Fusion models are usually judged by their fused score, a practice that assumes that every branch has learned something useful. We show that this assumption can fail silently in dual-view heart-sound classification. In AudioFuse, which fuses a spectrogram transformer with a waveform CNN, the spectrogram branch is unused despite strong fused performance when both branches are trained from scratch. We introduce a three-part diagnostic for branch collapse: whether deleting a branch changes the fused score, whether a linear probe on its features beats an untrained copy of the same branch, and whether the branch can learn its view when trained alone. Deleting the branch has negligible effect, and probing its features shows little improvement over an identically initialised, untrained copy. Yet the same architecture trained alone exceeds 0.9 AUC, so its view is learnable, while the jointly trained model is near chance when it must rely on this branch. Update statistics and an initialisation experiment point to an early optimisation imbalance. Moving a strong artificial label cue from one input to the other can switch which branch the model relies on, even though the branch architectures stay the same. We observe branch collapse on PhysioNet, but the full diagnostic is not met on ICBHI or CirCor. Fused performance alone therefore cannot establish that every branch learned a meaningful representation, and we recommend reporting branch deletion and an untrained-control probe alongside it.

</details>


### [85] [Plausible Videos Can Lead to Poor Decisions: Diagnosing and CURE-ing World Models for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22561)
*Raphael C. Kim,Michele Santacatterina,Jingsen Zhu,Ramin Zabih*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CURE（Certify、Upweight、Rollout、Explore）证书引导方法，判断动作条件世界模型何时可被信任用于规划，并据此决定加权去偏、测试时增采样或探索采集新数据，显著降低决策遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型虽能生成逼真且与训练数据一致的视频，却可能导向差的决策；因此需要回答两个问题：(1) 何时可以信任世界模型进行规划；(2) 不可信任时应当怎么做。

Method: CURE 基于"支撑度"（训练数据对规划器决策的覆盖程度）构建证书来评估世界模型的可信性，并依据证书结果采取三种后续动作：对训练数据加权以去偏模型、在测试时滚动采样更多样本、或在支撑缺失区域探索并采集新数据。

Result: 在 PhyWorld 模拟物理、MuJoCo 机械臂控制、Language-Table 仿真与真实视频等多领域验证：被证书信任的决策遗憾更低（Language-Table 仿真 0.06 vs 0.38，MuJoCo 接触 0.07 vs 0.38），而视频感知质量无法区分决策好坏；在相同数据下，CURE 相对最优悲观决策规则降低遗憾 10–25%，相对世界-动作模型、搜索与策略学习方法降低 41–64%。

Conclusion: 决策质量应与感知质量解耦：以支撑度为核心的证书指导可在不可信时给出可操作的补救策略，从而在多种仿真与真实场景中稳定提升规划决策质量。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are increasingly used as world models for planning, yet a world model that makes plausible, realistic-looking videos that are consistent with the training data can produce poor decisions. We address two key questions: (1) when can we trust a world model for planning, and (2) what should we do when we cannot? We introduce CURE (Certify, Upweight, Rollout, Explore), a certificate-guided technique that assesses whether a world model can be trusted for planning and determines what to do next: whether to upweight the training data to debias the model; roll out more samples at test time; or explore by collecting new data where support (how well the training data cover the planner's decisions) is missing. We deploy CURE across multiple domains, spanning simulated physics based on PhyWorld, MuJoCo arm control, and Language-Table pushing in simulation and on real videos. The decisions our certificate trusts have lower regret than those it does not (e.g., 0.06 vs. 0.38 on Language-Table sim and 0.07 vs. 0.38 on MuJoCo contact), whereas video perceptual quality does not identify good decisions. By focusing on decision quality rather than perceptual quality, CURE lowers regret over the best pessimistic decision rule by 10-25% across support levels, and over world-action models, search and policy learning by 41-64%, given the same data.

</details>


### [86] [Lost in Read-out: Locating the Image Quality Gap in Vision-Language Models with a Closed-Form Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22564)
*Zhuangzi Li,Shilv Cai,Jian Jin,Zhiyi Pan,Haihong Xiao,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 层析探针表明VLM的质量信息在视觉编码器和末层语言状态中线性可解码，远超其答案所体现；主要损失发生在最终读出环节，可通过闭式编辑单个末层神经元定向修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型作为零样本图像质量评估器时，其好/差判断与人类意见仅中度一致。作者要回答核心问题：模型是"看不见"质量，还是"说不出"质量。

Method: 在五个VLM上做逐层线性探针，检验质量可从哪一层线性解码；拟合约百个标签评估数据量需求。进一步用干预实验验证读出假说：对单个末层神经元做闭式编辑，基于多提示一次性拟合；对比相同提示下末层状态的ridge head；并在未参与拟合的提示上测试，考察迁移、方向效应、从语言模型中段起才有效等现象，以及与无约束微调的区别。

Result: 质量在视觉编码器及末层语言层均可线性解码，远优于答案表现，约100个标签即可恢复大部分信息。读出偏差表现为：五个模型均相对人类意见过度奖励色彩丰富度、低估锐度。干预验证了假说：编辑在未拟合提示上恢复KonIQ上ridge head相对答案增益的86–100%；增益小处提升也小（两个最大模型未达迁移标准）；ridge head本身低于答案时编辑反而降分；仅在从语言模型中段起写入时才有效。编辑不像无约束微调那样破坏感知问答，但不迁移到是/否或打分题，且在含情感句子后会使好–差对比反转。

Conclusion: VLM的质量评估缺陷主要是读出而非感知问题，可被针对单神经元的闭式编辑部分修正，但修正脆弱、任务与语境依赖强，难以泛化。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are widely used as zero-shot image quality assessors, yet their good-vs-poor answers agree only moderately with human opinion. We ask whether these models fail to see quality or fail to say it. Layer-wise probes on five VLMs point to the second: quality is linearly decodable from the vision encoder and still from the last language layer, far better than the answer reveals, and about a hundred labels recover most of it. The loss lies mainly in the final read-out, which partly reads something else: relative to human opinion, it over-rewards colorfulness and under-rewards sharpness on all five models. We test this by intervention: if the read-out is at fault, a closed-form edit of a single last-layer neuron, fitted to many prompts at once, should close the gap and no more. Both hold. Under prompts never used for fitting, the edit recovers 86--100\% of what a ridge head on the last-layer state, fitted under the same prompts, finds beyond the answer on KonIQ. Where that gap is small, it gains little, too little for our transfer criterion on the two largest models; where the head itself is below the answer, it lowers the score; and it works only when written from the middle of the language model on. Unlike unconstrained fine-tuning, the edit leaves perception question answering intact. It does not carry over to yes-or-no or rating questions, and it reverses the good--poor contrast after sentiment-laden sentences. Code will be made available.

</details>


### [87] [Semantic-Guided Geometric Decoding for Token-Efficient Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22577)
*Zhaojie Chen,Junyan Yang,Le Yang,Qi Dang,Yucheng Huang,Qianyi Che,Zhaoqiang Chen,Xinqi Zhu,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SemGeo，用单个网格token粗定位+几何解码器并行恢复精确坐标，实现token高效的视觉定位，兼顾精度与吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素到序列的自回归坐标生成需每个框多步解码，推理开销随实例数增长；像素到嵌入方法用共享特殊token时缺乏显式空间编码。

Method: 每个实例用单个网格token表示做粗定位；几何解码器复用视觉特征与隐藏状态并行输出离散框坐标；引入高斯软标签编码坐标距离用于SFT联合训练提案生成与框定位；强化后训练将框集奖励与坐标监督结合，在模型自身预测上训练以缓解teacher-forcing与推理的失配。

Result: 在涵盖目标检测、指代目标检测与文档版面定位的八个基准中，六个取得最高F1@mIoU；在vLLM与Transformers下平均框生成吞吐分别为代表性基线2.07倍与1.25倍，且精度更高。

Conclusion: 单个网格token+并行几何解码可有效降低视觉定位的token与推理开销，配合软标签与强化后训练缩小训练-推理差距，兼顾效率与准确性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide a unified interface for language-conditioned visual perception. In pixel-to-sequence grounding, autoregressive coordinate generation requires multiple sequential decoding steps per box, increasing inference cost with the number of instances. Pixel-to-embedding methods reduce this overhead but lack explicit spatial encoding when using a shared special token. We introduce SemGeo, a token-efficient visual grounding framework that represents each instance with a single grid token for coarse localization. A geometric decoder reuses visual features and hidden states to recover precise discrete box coordinates in parallel. To train proposal generation and box localization together, we introduce Gaussian soft labels that encode coordinate distances during supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Reinforcement post-training further combines box-set rewards with coordinate supervision on model-generated proposals. This stage trains the language model and geometric decoder on their own predictions to mitigate the mismatch between teacher-forced training and inference. Across eight benchmarks spanning object detection, referring object detection, and document layout grounding, SemGeo achieves the highest F1@mIou among compared MLLMs on six benchmarks. It also delivers $2.07\times$ and $1.25\times$ the mean box-generation throughput of representative grounding baselines under vLLM and Transformers, respectively, while achieving higher accuracy in both comparisons.

</details>


### [88] [Distilling World Action Models for Efficient Bimanual Manipulation via Anchored Path Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22592)
*Jialong Liu,Junjie He,Yandu Chen,Bin Zhou,Heng Su,Peijun Tang,Jianan Wang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Anchored Path Matching（APM），从学生模型到达的状态出发，用教师模型的端点与首个中间态作为双目标监督，结合模态路由适配器与锚点优先梯度更新，实现一个检查点支持 1/2/4 步联合视频-动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 通过迭代去噪联合生成未来视频与机器人动作，少步蒸馏虽减少采样评估次数，但粗粒度更新会使学生偏离教师轨迹，造成累积误差与生成质量下降。

Method: APM 在学生实际到达的状态上构造监督：从该状态继续运行教师至零噪声，取其端点与首个中间态作为目标；模态路由适配器（modality-routed adapters）学习由同一初始状态同时匹配两个目标；当两条梯度冲突时，采用锚点优先（anchor-priority）更新，保留端点梯度并剔除局部梯度中的对抗分量（分模态进行）。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上仅用 Clean 演示训练、直接在域随机化 Random 测试：单步设置下闭环成功率较同预算的基线蒸馏在 Clean 提升 2.0 个百分点、Random 提升 1.4 个百分点；视频 PSNR 由 30.169 dB 升至 32.317 dB，SSIM 与 LPIPS 亦有改善。两项真机任务上成功率优于单步教师，控制周期时延相对八步教师降低 21.3×。

Conclusion: 以轨迹锚定而非强制对齐学生自身路径的蒸馏目标，可有效抑制少步蒸馏的轨迹漂移；单一检查点在部署时可按推理预算选择采样步数，无需重训或教师查询，兼顾成功率与实时性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative denoising. Few-step distillation reduces the number of model evaluations, but its coarse updates can move the student away from the teacher's trajectory. We propose Anchored Path Matching (APM), which supervises endpoint and short-transition predictions from states reached by the student. From each state, we continue the teacher to zero noise and use its endpoint and first intermediate state as targets. Modality-routed adapters learn to match both targets from the same starting state. When the two gradients conflict, anchor-priority updates preserve the endpoint gradient and remove the opposing component of the local gradient within each modality. One checkpoint supports 1-, 2-, and 4-step joint generation. The sampling budget can be chosen at deployment without retraining or teacher queries. On RoboTwin 2.0, we train only on Clean demonstrations and evaluate directly on the domain-randomized Random setting. At one step, APM improves closed-loop success over continued base distillation at matched inference budgets by 2.0 percentage points on Clean and 1.4 on Random. Video peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) rises from 30.169 to 32.317 dB, with gains in structural similarity (SSIM) and learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS). On two real-robot tasks, APM improves success over the one-step teacher and reduces control-cycle latency by $21.3\times$ relative to the eight-step teacher.Project page: https://jialonglong.github.io/Anchored-Path-Matching/.

</details>


### [89] [Propose with the Gradient, Accept with the Loss: One Hedged Attack for Pixel-Sparse Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22596)
*Hon Ming Yan,Chun Pong Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出梯度引导随机搜索（GRS），一种单次运行、每步仅需一次前向与一次反向传播的像素稀疏对抗攻击，性能可匹配或超越稀疏AutoAttack（sAA）的最佳成员，并在多数设置下接近或超过sAA整体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素稀疏对抗攻击的鲁棒性评估依赖稀疏AutoAttack（sAA）集成，但其成员各有缺陷：稀疏投影梯度下降（sPGD）依赖一阶模型，在全像素改变时失效；稀疏随机搜索（Sparse-RS）在有信息时也忽略梯度。作者将两者统一视为“提议分布+接受规则”的局部搜索，探讨此类搜索应如何利用梯度。

Method: 基于Taylor界确定应度量什么，并在搜索访问的状态上进行测量，得出五条规则：梯度符号是单像素处最可靠的用途，用于设定新像素值；候选像素的梯度排序应宽松遵循；待移除像素的排序更可靠可更严格遵循；在一次移动被接受前不应重复提议同一像素；接受应仅由损失决定。据此构造GRS，每步一次前向、一次反向。

Result: 在13个CIFAR-10配置和5个ImageNet模型上，GRS匹配或超越sAA最佳单一成员，在18个设置中的17个上距sAA不超过1个百分点或更强，在8个设置上显著强于sAA；仅用3个（而非最多7个）前向传播单位，CIFAR-10上比sAA成员完整运行快3.7至5.0倍。

Conclusion: 将稀疏攻击统一为局部搜索并系统分析梯度的最佳使用方式，可得到更简单、更高效且更强的单次攻击GRS，兼具性能与计算优势，为像素稀疏鲁棒性评估提供更优选择。

Abstract: A pixel-sparse adversary may change a few pixels of an image to any value. Robustness to such attacks is currently measured with an ensemble, sparse-AutoAttack (sAA), because its members fail in different places: sparse projected gradient descent (sPGD) trusts a first-order model that a full pixel change breaks, and sparse random search (Sparse-RS) ignores the gradient even where it is informative. We show that both attacks are local searches, each a proposal distribution plus an acceptance rule, and ask what such a search can learn from the gradient. A Taylor bound says what to measure, and measurements at the states the search visits give five rules. The sign of the gradient is its most reliable use at a single pixel and sets a new pixel's value. Its ranking of candidate pixels should be followed loosely, and its ranking of the pixels to remove, which is often more reliable, can be followed more closely. A pixel should not be proposed twice before a move is accepted, and acceptance should be decided by the loss alone. These rules define Gradient-guided Random Search (GRS), which runs once and costs one forward and one backward pass per step. On 13 CIFAR-10 configurations and 5 ImageNet models, GRS matches or beats the best single member of sAA and is within one point of sAA itself or stronger on 17 of 18 settings, and it is significantly stronger than sAA on 8. It uses 3 instead of up to 7 forward-pass units, and on CIFAR-10 it runs 3.7 to 5.0 times faster than the members of sAA run in full.

</details>


### [90] [PlaceTally: A Test-Time Memory Model for Constructing Place Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22608)
*Alibay Osmanli,Shaogang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PlaceTally，一个问答无关的测试时记忆模型：通过构建“地点图”将视频按地点分组，使同一地点仅在首次/最佳匹配时被计数一次，从而解决相机重返旧地点导致的重复计数问题，实现可扩展的物体计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中相机返回已到过的地方时计数困难：整体读取视频的视觉语言模型只给出少量小数字，分段读取的模型会在每个片段重复计数，目标跟踪器则需要预先知道类别或固定词表。作者观察到计数记忆无需知道在找什么，只需知道去过哪里——物体很少在两次访问间离开房间，因此按地点分组的视频片段几乎不共享物体，且地点可在提问前被识别。

Method: PlaceTally为每个视频构建地点图：每个视频片段与最佳早期匹配归入同一地点，否则开启新地点（强匹配很少出错，而真正的重返往往弱匹配）。冻结的视觉语言模型对每个地点仅从其至多128帧中计数一次，使每次读取输入有界、记忆随地点数增长；图构建速度比实时快15倍。

Result: 在RevisitCount（真实房间重访录制基准）上，测试房间平均相对准确率62.5%、留出房间61.2%，而同一模型整体读取视频为51.2%和44.3%。当房间重访频率变化时性能保持稳定，而对访问求和的固定校正从38.5%波动到61.4%。

Conclusion: 基于地点记忆的问答无关计数方法能在不预设类别的情况下有效避免重复计数，对重访频率鲁棒，且计算高效、输入有界。

Abstract: Counting distinct objects in a video is hard when the camera returns to places it has seen. A vision-language model that reads the whole video answers with a few small numbers, and one that reads it in parts counts an object again in every part that shows it. Object trackers need the category before the video is read, or a fixed vocabulary. We observe that a memory for counting does not need to know what it is looking for, only where it has been: objects rarely leave a room between visits, so parts of a video grouped by place share almost no objects, and a place can be recognised before any question is asked. Building on this, we introduce PlaceTally, a question-blind test-time memory model that constructs a place graph for each video. Each video fragment joins the place of its best earlier match or opens a new place, because a strong match is rarely wrong while a true return often matches weakly. A frozen vision-language model counts each place once from at most 128 of its frames, so every read has a bounded input and the memory grows with the number of places; the graph is built 15 times faster than real time. On RevisitCount, our benchmark of real room re-recordings, it scores 62.5% mean relative accuracy on test rooms and 61.2% on held-out rooms, against 51.2% and 44.3% for the same model reading the whole video. It stays level as rooms return more or less often, while a fixed correction of summed visits moves from 38.5% to 61.4%.

</details>


### [91] [Sub-Meter Canopy Height Synthesis from Sentinel-2 via Anchored Residual Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22636)
*Kiarie Ndegwa,Tony Chang,Andreas Gros,Vincent A. Landau,David D. Diaz,Luke Zachmann,Nathan E. Rutenbeck,Guy C Bayes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用免费Sentinel-2影像生成0.5 m冠层高度模型，提出确定性回归器Anchor(低失真)与残差流匹配模型AR-flow(高感知保真)两个工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚米级冠层高度模型依赖多年更新一次的机载LiDAR，成本高、时效差，需要廉价且高频的替代方案。

Method: 将10 m光学影像到0.5 m高度图视为20倍跨模态超分辨率任务；基于感知-失真权衡设计共享条件的两个模型：Anchor为确定性回归器，AR-flow在学习到的CHM隐空间中对该回归残差做条件流匹配；在69个生态区197k个LiDAR配对瓦片训练，2530个留出瓦片对8个基线评估。

Result: Anchor仅用免费卫星输入达到2.83 m MAE；AR-flow将切片Wasserstein距离由1.13降至0.208，但MAE升至3.68 m；冠尺度统计改善，单木检测仍弱。

Conclusion: 生成的亚像素细节属合理结构而非实测单木；季度大陆尺度更新可行，可用卫星监测为昂贵数据采集提供时机与范围指引。

Abstract: Canopy height models (CHMs) - per-pixel maps of vegetation top height - are essential for forest carbon accounting, wildfire fuel mapping, and tree-level ecology, yet sub-meter CHMs require airborne LiDAR refreshed on multi-year national cycles. We produce 0.5 m CHMs from free 10 m Sentinel-2 imagery, a 20$\times$ cross-modal super-resolution task where optical reflectance must predict geometric height it does not directly observe. Motivated by the perception-distortion trade-off, we derive two operating points with shared conditioning. A deterministic regressor (Anchor) targets low distortion; a conditional flow matching model, trained on the Anchor's residual in a learned CHM latent space (AR-flow), targets perceptual fidelity. Trained on 197k LiDAR-paired tiles spanning 69 ecoregions across the contiguous United States and evaluated on 2,530 held-out tiles against eight baselines, the Anchor achieves 2.83 m MAE using only free satellite input. The AR-flow reduces sliced Wasserstein distance from the Anchor's 1.13 to 0.208, at a higher MAE of 3.68 m. Crown-scale statistics improve, but strict individual-tree detection remains weak: generated sub-pixel detail represents plausible structure rather than measured individual trees. Quarterly continental refresh becomes tractable, letting satellite monitoring inform timing and extent for more expensive data acquisitions.

</details>


### [92] [OVET-Consensus: Rank-Preserving Counterfactual Event Verification for Video Moment Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22637)
*Alibay Osmanli,Shaogang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视频时刻检索中的"打分器瓶颈"：候选窗口已与目标事件重叠却仍被选错。作者提出 OVET-Consensus 反事实事件验证层，在保持时序专家排序的前提下仅允许高置信度的 rank-1 覆盖，在 QVHighlights 上将 R1@0.5 从 64.71 提升至 65.35。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频时刻检索系统常生成与目标事件重叠的候选窗口，但最终仍选中错误窗口，说明瓶颈不在候选生成而在打分/选择环节。诊断性 oracle 显示同一候选族中存在显著提升空间（父级重排池 75.68 R1@0.5，全 100 候选池 94.97 R1@0.5），表明验证器设计是关键突破口。

Method: 以固定 QD-DETR 派生的时序专家（parent-aware temporal expert，64.71 R1@0.5）为基线，构建 OVET-Consensus：一个保序的反事实事件验证层。它保留时序专家的排序列表为默认输出，仅在基于留出训练视频校准的策略下允许高置信度的 rank-1 覆盖。验证器融合父级感知区间证据、结构化 Qwen3-VL 事件判断、VideoLLaMA3 成对时序信号与稠密时序证据，但不允许这些信号自由重排 top-K 列表。

Result: 在 QVHighlights 验证集上，R1@0.5 由 64.71 提升至 65.35，配对 bootstrap 区间为正；mAP 基本不变（42.67→42.74）。共发生 20 次覆盖，其中 15 次为有益纠正、3 次为有害纠正。

Conclusion: 结果揭示验证器设计的实用约束：语义兼容性是有用证据，但时序定位仍需判断事件在视觉兼容场景内的起止与变化位置；保序的保守覆盖策略能在极小风险下带来稳定增益。

Abstract: Modern video moment retrieval systems often propose windows that overlap the target event, yet still select the wrong one. We study this scorer bottleneck on QVHighlights with a fixed QD-DETR-derived temporal expert: the parent-aware temporal expert reaches 64.71 R1@0.5, while diagnostic oracles over the same candidate family reach 75.68 R1@0.5 within the parent-reranked pool and 94.97 R1@0.5 over the full 100-candidate evidence pool. We introduce OVET-Consensus, a rank-preserving counterfactual event verification layer that keeps the temporal expert's ranked list as the default and permits only high-confidence rank-1 overrides from a policy calibrated on held-out training videos. The verifier uses parent-aware interval evidence, structured Qwen3-VL event judgments, VideoLLaMA3 pairwise temporal signals, and dense temporal evidence, but never lets those signals freely reorder the top-K list. On QVHighlights validation, OVET-Consensus improves the parent-aware temporal expert from 64.71 to 65.35 R1@0.5 with a positive paired-bootstrap interval, while mAP is largely unchanged, from 42.67 to 42.74; it makes 20 overrides, with 15 helpful corrections and 3 harmful corrections. The results point to a practical constraint on verifier design: semantic compatibility is useful evidence, but temporal grounding still requires deciding where the event starts, changes, and ends inside a visually compatible scene.

</details>


### [93] [REVEAL: Reconstructing Video Amodal Content via Language and Flow Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22645)
*Minh Tran,Winston Bounsavy,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REVEAL：融合光流与文本先验的统一扩散框架，实现视频非模态补全（分割+纹理），并贡献长视频 benchmark LAVAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的视频非模态补全方法缺乏辅助先验：难以重建严重遮挡区域的内容，且长序列上时序一致性不足。

Method: 提出统一扩散框架 REVEAL。用户文本查询兼具双重作用：驱动开放词汇视频分割得到可见掩码，并为纹理重建提供语义引导。非模态分割引入光流引导：将前帧可见掩码 warp 到当前帧，把可见内容传播到遮挡区域并近似当前形状，在形变与遮挡同时发生时提供强形状先验。纹理补全以文本引导约束被遮挡外观重建，并提供稳定语义参考以维持视觉连贯性。

Result: 在现有 benchmark 上取得 SOTA；在长序列上保持高质量时序一致性。同时构建 LAVAT 新 benchmark，包含长视频序列与文本描述，用于评估重度遮挡下的文本引导视频非模态补全。

Conclusion: 将运动（光流）与语义（文本）先验统一用于视频非模态补全，可有效提升遮挡重建与时序稳定性，并推动长视频场景下的评测标准化。

Abstract: Amodal perception enables humans to perceive entire objects even when parts are occluded, a remarkable cognitive skill that artificial intelligence struggles to replicate. Recent diffusion-based methods have extended amodal completion from images to videos, yet they lack auxiliary priors to guide the reconstruction of heavily occluded content and to maintain temporal consistency over long sequences. We present REVEAL (REconstructing VidEo Amodal content via Language and flow guidance), a unified diffusion-based framework that integrates complementary motion and semantic priors for video amodal completion. A user-provided text query serves a dual role: it drives open-vocabulary video segmentation to obtain visible masks, and it provides semantic guidance for texture reconstruction. For amodal segmentation, we introduce optical flow guidance: by warping visible masks from previous frames to the current frame, the flow-warped mask propagates visible content into occluded regions and approximates the object's current shape, providing a strong shape prior even under simultaneous deformation and occlusion. For texture completion, text guidance constrains the reconstruction of occluded appearance while providing a stable semantic reference that helps maintain visual coherence throughout the reconstruction. We also contribute LAVAT, a new benchmark featuring long video sequences paired with text descriptions, enabling evaluation of text-guided video amodal completion under heavy occlusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that REVEAL achieves state-of-the-art performance on existing benchmarks while maintaining high-quality temporal consistency over extended sequences. The project page is available at: https://uark-aicv.github.io/REVEAL/.

</details>


### [94] [Must You See Fakes to Catch Fakes? Language Says What to Look For, Real Images What to Ignore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22650)
*Tianchi Xu,Yuxuan Zhou,Yuheng Zhang,Yanan Guan,Tao Dai,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练检测器SaySee，不在生成图像上训练，用视觉基础模型的文本-视觉对齐构建"生成方向"并打分，再用真实图像校正偏移，24个评测集平均AUC 98.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器面临两大问题：其一，训练集内少数生成器的特有痕迹导致对新型生成器泛化差；其二，训练时真实图像与生成图像在格式、内容上的差异被检测器当作真实性证据，使决策受与真实性无关的因素影响，损害鲁棒性与泛化性。二者根源都在于"用生成图像训练"，故作者尝试完全不在生成图像上训练。

Method: SaySee为免训练检测器，分两步：Say——基于仅在真实性上存在差异的配对描述集合，在基础模型嵌入空间中构建"生成方向"，以图像嵌入在该方向上的投影作为真实性得分；See——利用真实图像校正由格式、图像类型和内容引起的得分偏移。

Result: 在涵盖标准基准、in-the-wild数据及最新生成器的24个评测集上，配合MoE-ViE取得平均AUC 98.2%，比最佳现有检测器高4.2个点；在将格式与真实性解耦的条件下，仍为所有方法中最佳。

Conclusion: 检测无需在生成图像上训练；视觉基础模型预训练中已习得"AI生成"概念，文本可直接指向该概念，据此构建的训练无关检测器在泛化性与鲁棒性上均优于依赖生成图像训练的现有方法。

Abstract: Must a detector see generated images in order to catch them? Detection of AI-generated images has long faced two problems. The cues a detector learns from the few generators in its training set carry traces specific to those generators and transfer poorly to new ones, which limits generalization. The real and generated training images also differ in format and content, so the detector takes these differences as evidence of authenticity, and its decisions change with factors that have nothing to do with authenticity, which harms robustness as well as generalization. Both problems stem from training on generated images. A detector never trained on them can largely avoid both at the source. Vision foundation models have already learned the concept of "AI-generated" during pretraining, and text can point to it directly. Building on this, we propose SaySee, a training-free detector: Say builds a generation direction from sets of paired descriptions that differ only in authenticity and scores an image by the projection of its embedding onto it, and See uses real images to correct the score offsets caused by format, image type and content. On 24 evaluation sets that span standard benchmarks, in-the-wild data and the latest generators, SaySee reaches a mean AUC of 98.2% with MoE-ViE, 4.2 points above the best existing detector. When format is decoupled from authenticity, it remains the best of all methods. Code will be released.

</details>


### [95] [OmniTongue: A Comprehensive MLLM Benchmark for Automated Tongue Diagnosis in Traditional Chinese Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22652)
*Liuyi Xu,Yun Guo,Shusheng Li,Xuewen Zhang,Yining Qian,An-Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniTongue——首个面向中医舌诊的综合、细粒度视觉问答基准，包含1351张专家校验图像与21616条指令对，揭示现有MLLM在舌诊任务上的系统性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在西医影像中表现优异，但在中医舌诊中的潜力尚未被系统评估；舌诊同时要求全局与局部感知，是检验模型视觉理解深度的严苛试金石。现有数据集存在维度单一、粒度粗糙、标签歧义等问题，难以评估真实临床能力。

Method: 构建OmniTongue基准：整合1351张专家验证图像与21616条指令对；核心设计包括16个临床维度的多维评估、面向病灶定位的区域级标注，以及66个细粒度标签以精确表征诊断结果；并在现有MLLM上开展广泛评测。

Result: 评测显示现有MLLM存在显著性能差距：医学专用模型并未体现出领域优势；普遍偏低的Macro-F1分数表明模型依赖统计捷径而非真正理解舌象病理。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了MLLM在解读区域性医学特征方面的局限，也说明OmniTongue对发展稳健的自动化舌诊模型具有重要意义；代码与数据集将于后续公开发布。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have excelled in Western medical imaging, yet their potential in Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) tongue diagnosis remains systematically unevaluated. Tongue diagnosis requires sophisticated global and local perception, serving as a rigorous touchstone for assessing the visual understanding depth of MLLMs. However, existing datasets are limited by single dimensions, coarse granularity, and ambiguous labels, hindering the evaluation of authentic clinical capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce OmniTongue, the first comprehensive, fine-grained Visual Question Answering benchmark for TCM tongue diagnosis. This benchmark integrates 1,351 expert-validated images and 21,616 instruction pairs. Its core design features 16 clinical dimensions for multidimensional evaluation, region-specific annotations for pathological localization, and 66 fine-grained labels for precise diagnostic representation. Extensive evaluations of existing MLLMs reveal significant performance gaps, demonstrating that medical-specific models lack domain advantages, while universally poor Macro-F1 scores expose a reliance on statistical shortcuts rather than genuine understanding of tongue pathology. These findings not only highlight the current limitations of MLLMs in interpreting regional medical features but also underscore OmniTongue's significance for developing robust automated tongue diagnosis models. The code and dataset will be made publicly available in a future release.

</details>


### [96] [Unified Residual Translation for Paired Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22655)
*Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Mridul Banik,Ibne Farabi Shihab,Md. Shifatul Ahsan Apurba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一残差翻译器URT，将通道与尺度映射同残差预测解耦，用统一预测规则和损失覆盖MRI对比合成、可见光-红外、SAR-光学翻译与真实相机超分四类任务，在多数设置下取得最优指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对图像复原任务在输入通道数与目标网格上差异显著，作者试图回答"预测流程中多少部分必须针对每个任务专门设计"，即探索一种跨异构任务的通用建模范式。

Method: URT将流程拆为两部分：一是确定性的参考映射，通过通道平均、复制或双三次插值把输入对齐到目标维度；二是由NAFNet编解码器从完整输入预测加性残差修正。不同任务的实例分别训练，但共享同一预测规则、ℓ1目标与优化器。另对四种光谱替代方案进行比较分析。

Result: 在六个任务-尺度设置中，URT在IXI、SEN1-2及RealSR ×3/×4上取得所评估基线中最高的PSNR与SSIM：IXI达30.774dB，SEN1-2达13.478dB，RealSR三尺度亮度PSNR为33.596/28.742/27.342dB；在LLVIP上PSNR与SSIM均列第二。光谱替代研究显示拟合参考与分带残差增益具有互补收益。

Conclusion: 显式维度对齐加学习式残差修正可作为异构成对成像任务的统一表述，说明大部分预测逻辑无需任务专门化，仅需保证维度对齐的确定性前端。

Abstract: Paired image restoration spans changes in imaging contrast, sensor modality, and spatial resolution. These settings differ in their input channels and target grids, raising the question of how much of the prediction pipeline must be specialized to each task. We present the Unified Residual Translator (URT), which separates fixed channel and scale mapping from learned residual prediction. A deterministic reference uses channel averaging, replication, or bicubic interpolation to match the target dimensions, while a NAFNet encoder-decoder predicts an additive correction from the complete input. Separately trained instances share the prediction rule, $\ell_1$ objective, and optimizer across MRI contrast synthesis, visible-to-infrared translation, SAR-to-optical translation, and real-camera super-resolution. Across six task and scale settings, URT achieves the highest PSNR and SSIM among the evaluated baselines on IXI, SEN1-2, and RealSR $\times3$ and $\times4$. It reaches 30.774dB on IXI and 13.478dB on SEN1-2, with luminance PSNR of 33.596, 28.742, and 27.342dB at the three RealSR scales. On LLVIP, it ranks second in PSNR and SSIM. A study of four spectral alternatives identifies complementary benefits of fitted references and bandwise residual gains. Together, these results support explicit dimensional alignment and learned residual correction as a common formulation for heterogeneous paired imaging tasks.

</details>


### [97] [SpatialCast: Reading the 3D World via Cast Shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22657)
*Enhan Li,Jinghao Zhou,Jiaze Zhang,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SpatialCast 基准，利用投影阴影评估多模态大模型将物理场景条件与视觉观察相联系的时空推理能力，包含 21000 道合成题与 200 道真实场景题，并发现开源模型接近随机水平，而 GRPO 后训练可提升性能并迁移到外部空间基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态空间基准已在空间关系、记忆与视角推理上取得进展，但较少系统评估模型如何将物理场景条件与视觉观察联系起来；而这一能力对理解场景结构、预测物理变化后果至关重要。

Method: 以投影阴影（cast shadow）作为可观察证据构建 SpatialCast 基准：阴影外观由物体几何与姿态、光照、视角共同决定。设计三个互补任务：场景—投影一致性评估、预测干预引起的变化、由视觉转变反推物理变化。数据含 21000 道合成题（场景与几何两个测试分区）及来自 20 个实拍场景的 200 道真实题；并用其训练数据进行 GRPO 后训练。

Result: 闭源 MLLM 优于通用开源与专为空间推理设计的 MLLM，后者在合成基准上接近随机水平；强闭源模型在阴影长度与光源高度判断上仍表现吃力。GRPO 后训练提升了开源 MLLM 在该基准上的表现，并在多个外部空间基准上取得改进，提示所学能力具有迁移性。

Conclusion: SpatialCast 可作为受控资源，用于诊断空间推理能力并研究其学习与泛化；基于阴影的物理—观察一致性推理仍是当前 MLLM 的薄弱环节，但有通过后训练改善的空间。

Abstract: As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) advance toward understanding and interacting with the physical world, spatial reasoning becomes essential for understanding scene structure and anticipating the effects of physical changes. Existing spatial benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating spatial relations, memory, and viewpoint reasoning, but less systematically assess how models connect physical scene conditions with visual observations. We introduce SpatialCast, a benchmark that uses cast shadows to evaluate this ability. Cast shadows provide observable evidence of a 3D scene. Their image appearance depends on object geometry and pose, illumination, and viewpoint. We examine this relationship through three complementary tasks that assess scene-projection consistency, predict intervention-induced changes, and infer the physical changes behind visual transitions. The benchmark contains 21,000 synthetic questions with separate scene and geometry test partitions, complemented by 200 SpatialCast-Real questions from 20 photographed scenes. Evaluations show that closed-source MLLMs outperform general-purpose open-weight MLLMs and MLLMs designed for spatial reasoning, which remain near chance on the synthetic benchmark, while even strong closed-source models struggle with shadow-length and light-height judgments. We also use SpatialCast training data for GRPO post-training, improving open-weight MLLM performance on the benchmark. Further evaluations show improvements on several external spatial benchmarks, suggesting transfer of the learned capabilities. These experiments support SpatialCast as a controlled resource for diagnosing spatial reasoning capabilities and studying their learning and generalization.

</details>


### [98] [VSpark: Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22666)
*Suho Yoo,Youngjoon Jang,Hyebin Cho,Taeyeop Lee,Jaewook Jeong,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VSpark通过校准动作噪声的 rollout、特征对齐和延续复用三项设计，提升自回归 VLA 推测解码在机器人控制中的加速效果，在四类 VLA 和四个操作基准上实现 1.28x–2.10x 无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）逐 token 生成动作，推理慢。推测解码可无损加速，但在机器人控制中，多个不同 token 编码几乎相同的动作，草稿模型即使接近正确动作也会被拒绝，导致加速受限。

Method: VSpark 采用三项设计：(i) 用带校准动作噪声的 rollout 采集数据，使草稿模型接触更广泛的机器人状态；(ii) 特征对齐，训练草稿模型匹配 VLA 的隐层特征而非仅输出 token；(iii) 延续复用，保留被拒草稿路径上 VLA 已计算出的预测，作为下一步验证的候选而非丢弃。

Result: 在四种采用不同动作分词器的 VLA 和四个操作基准上，VSpark 实现 1.28x–2.10x 加速，同时保持 VLA 解码无损。

Conclusion: 针对机器人控制中动作 token 多义性导致的草稿拒绝问题，VSpark 通过噪声校准 rollout、隐层特征对齐与已计算预测复用，显著提升推测解码加速比，并保持解码无损性，具备跨分词器与跨基准的泛化性。

Abstract: Our aim is to accelerate autoregressive vision-language-action models (VLAs), which generate robot actions one token at a time. Speculative decoding makes this possible: a lightweight draft model proposes several tokens at once, and the VLA verifies them in a single pass, accepting only those that match its own predictions, so decoding remains lossless. The speedup, however, is limited in robot control, where many different tokens encode nearly the same motion and a draft close to the correct action is still rejected. To address this, our approach, VSpark, is built upon three design choices: (i) rollouts collected with calibrated action noise, which expose the draft to a wider range of robot states; (ii) feature alignment, which trains the draft to match the VLA's hidden features rather than its output tokens alone; and (iii) continuation reuse, which keeps the predictions the VLA has already computed along rejected drafts as candidates for the next verification step, instead of discarding them. On four VLAs with different action tokenizers and four manipulation benchmarks, VSpark achieves 1.28x to 2.10x speedup while keeping the VLA's decoding lossless.

</details>


### [99] [Let Samples Sing in Chorus: Mitigating Spurious Correlations via Cross-view Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22668)
*Weiwei Li,Junzhuo Liu,Tong Chu,Hengfu Yu,Yinjie Lei,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHORD 提出多视图去偏框架，通过跨视图"合唱"共识聚合各类内划分作为候选偏置代理，在无偏置监督下纠正目标表示与分类器，七个基准的最差组准确率一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型依赖虚假相关（spurious correlations），在欠代表组上失效。现有方法要么纠正人工指定的偏置属性（可能与模型实际使用的捷径不一致），要么仅从单一表示空间推断未知偏置（证据不完整且含噪）。核心洞察：某一特征空间中被遮蔽的偏置，在另一空间可能更清晰。

Method: CHORD（Cross-view Harmonic Outlier Reversal for Debiasing）：多视图框架，令样本在多种表示空间中"合唱"（sing in chorus）以获得稳健的跨视图共识；在每个视图中聚合类内划分（within-class partitions）作为候选偏置的代理，从而在无偏置监督下对目标表示与分类器进行去偏。

Result: 在视觉、语言、语音、医学影像四个模态共七个基准上实验，最差组准确率（worst-group accuracy）取得一致提升；代码与检查点已开源。

Conclusion: 多视图表示空间的交叉共识可有效替代人工偏置标注，实现无需偏置监督的稳健去偏；跨模态验证表明方法具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Deep models often rely on spurious correlations that fail on underrepresented groups. Existing methods either correct human-specified bias attributes or infer unknown biases from the learned representation space. However, annotated attributes may differ from the shortcuts actually used by a model, while a single representation provides incomplete and potentially noisy evidence. Our key insight is that a bias obscured in one feature space may be clearer in another. In this work, we propose CHORD (Cross-view Harmonic Outlier Reversal for Debiasing), a multi-view framework that allows samples to "sing in chorus" across diverse representation spaces to achieve robust cross-view consensus. CHORD aggregates aggregates within-class partitions in each view, which is a proxy for specifying candidate biases, to debias the target representation and classifier without bias supervision. Experiments on seven benchmarks across vision, language, speech, and medical imaging show consistent improvements in worst-group accuracy. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/davelee-uestc/chord_debiasing_public .

</details>


### [100] [Beyond the Camera: A Tri-Modal Driving World Model for Future RGB, Thermal, and LiDAR Forecasting in Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22669)
*Faysal Mehrab Chowdhury,Md. Mahfuzur Rahman,Ahmed Rafi Hasan,Kishor Datta Gupta,Mohd Ariful Haque,Savannah Shannon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在DSERT-RoLL恶劣天气真实序列上，研究RGB、热成像与LiDAR三传感器联合驾驶世界模型预测：将LiDAR表示为与RGB对齐的深度视频，后训练预训练视频世界模型；经与模型同tokenizer匹配的持续性基线比较，RGB与热成像预测在全部58条验证序列上均优于压缩匹配持续性基线，联合训练使2s处LiDAR误差降低2.1m，且仅三传感器模型的LiDAR预测在平均误差上超过持续性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型多预测相机视频、少数加入LiDAR，热成像相机因暗光与部分恶劣天气仍可成像却极少被作为预测目标，据作者所知尚无世界模型同时预测三种传感器；同时，持续性基线若未与模型经过相同tokenizer处理，会造成不公平比较（如热成像模型在雾天"劣于持续性"的假象）。

Method: 在DSERT-RoLL恶劣天气真实序列上研究RGB、热成像、LiDAR的联合预测；对每个传感器后训练一个预训练视频世界模型；LiDAR表征为与RGB相机对齐的深度视频，其稠密化与编码方式依据模型视频tokenizer的可重建性来选取；构建将三传感器视为单一视频的三传感器模型；以米为单位对实测回波评估LiDAR，并与经过同一tokenizer的持续性基线对比；进行单传感器置空消融。

Result: 在与模型匹配的持续性基线比较下，热成像模型在雾天对持续性基线的表面劣势消失；在六种天气条件的全部58条验证序列上，后训练的RGB与热成像预测均优于压缩匹配持续性基线；联合训练使2s处LiDAR误差降低2.1m，代价是相机保真度小幅下降；仅三传感器模型的LiDAR预测在平均误差上优于持续性基线；置空某一传感器会使其他传感器预测退化，且雪天退化程度大于雾天。

Conclusion: 三传感器（RGB+热成像+LiDAR）联合世界模型预测可行且互补增益明显；公平的、经相同tokenizer匹配的持续性基线对结论至关重要；联合训练提升LiDAR精度并带来跨传感器依赖，但对相机保真度存在轻微代价。

Abstract: Driving world models forecast what a vehicle's sensors will record next. Most forecast camera video and some add LiDAR, but thermal cameras, which still see in darkness and some adverse weather, are rarely a forecast target, and to our knowledge no world model forecasts all three sensors. We study joint forecasting of RGB, thermal, and LiDAR on real adverse-weather sequences of the DSERT-RoLL dataset. We post-train a pretrained video world model for each sensor and represent LiDAR as a depth video aligned with the RGB camera, with densification and encoding chosen by what the model's video tokenizer can reconstruct. A tri-sensor model sees all three sensors as one video. We score LiDAR in meters against measured returns and compare against persistence baselines passed through the same tokenizer as the model. With this matching, an apparent loss of the thermal model to persistence in fog disappears. On all 58 validation sequences in six weather conditions, post-trained RGB and thermal forecasts beat compression-matched persistence. Joint training lowers the LiDAR error at 2s by 2.1m at a small cost in camera fidelity, and only the tri-sensor model's LiDAR forecasts beat persistence in mean error. Blanking one sensor degrades the forecasts of the others more in snow than in fog.

</details>


### [101] [FVScore: Specification-Driven Neuro-Symbolic Video Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22673)
*Do Xuan Long,Long T. Le,Yiwen Song,Min-Yen Kan,Tomas Pfister,Yale Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出神经符号框架 FVSCORE，实现规格驱动、可溯源到具体时间戳的视频生成评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估器依赖 MLLM 整体判断或视觉-语言指标，难以与人类判断对齐，也难以将分数追溯到具体的时间性违规。

Method: 将视频编译为两类命题：捕捉提示要求的显式命题，以及捕捉未言明的物理、时间与跨模态约束的隐式命题；用专门的感知工具对每个命题做接地，并用受线性时序逻辑启发的软时序算子评估置信度轨迹；再经显著性感知校准，仅保留感知上显著的违规，输出带时间戳与证据的报告。

Result: 在公开及复杂多事件基准上，与当前最强视频模型进行广泛实验，FVSCORE 在人类相关性上优于整体式与基于智能体的评估器，并提供高准确、可解释的反馈。

Conclusion: 规格驱动＋神经符号＋时序逻辑＋显著性校准的评估范式可同时提升人类对齐度与可解释性。

Abstract: We introduce Faithfulness Video Score (FVSCORE), a neuro-symbolic framework for specification-driven video generation evaluation. Existing evaluators rely on holistic MLLM judgments or vision-language metrics, yet aligning their scores with human judgments and tracing them to specific temporal violations remain open challenges. FVSCORE addresses these limitations by compiling each video into explicit propositions capturing prompt requirements and implicit propositions capturing unstated physical, temporal, and cross-modal constraints. It grounds each proposition using specialized perceptual tools and evaluates the resulting confidence traces with soft temporal operators inspired by Linear Temporal Logic. A Significance-Aware Calibration step then aligns verification with human perception by retaining only perceptually significant violations before producing a timestamp- and evidence-grounded report.
