We conduct extensive experiments across public and complex multi-event benchmarks with the strongest video models to date, demonstrating that FVSCORE outperforms holistic and agent-based evaluators in human correlation while providing highly accurate and interpretable feedback.

</details>


### [102] [Classification of Tissues on MALDI-MSI Image Modality for Pancreatic Cancer Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22680)
*Abubakar Qahir,Jenny Benois-Pineau,Victoria Bourgeais,Fabien Baldacci,Sandrine Dabernat,Nicolas Desbenoit,Mélanie Moreau,Mehdi Boubaddi,Marion Marty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一套全自动MALDI-MSI数据处理流水线，将原始质谱成像数据转化为可直接用于深度学习的图像块，用于胰腺组织肿瘤/非肿瘤二分类，在图像质量与参考交互式流程相当的前提下将处理时间从约一个工作日缩短至平均20分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质谱成像（MSI）虽能在组织切片上空间映射分子分布，且已有多种预处理工具，但构建面向深度学习的端到端工作流仍较为困难，现有多依赖人工交互操作、耗时且不易标准化。

Method: 开发全自动流水线，将原始MALDI-MSI数据转换为深度学习可用的图像块（patch），并针对光谱图像中肿瘤区域的识别分别训练独立模型，与参考交互式流程进行对比评估。

Result: 所提流水线输出图像与参考交互式工作流结果在均方误差（MSE）指标上非常接近；计算时间平均约20分钟，而参考流程需约一整个工作日；模型在高分辨率光谱上达到0.94准确率，在较低分辨率光谱上平均准确率为0.59。

Conclusion: 全自动流水线可在保证图像质量的前提下显著提升MALDI-MSI数据预处理效率，并支撑胰腺组织肿瘤区域识别的深度学习建模；但模型性能对光谱分辨率较为敏感，低分辨率数据下仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Mass Spectrometry Imaging (MSI) spatially maps molecular distributions in tissue sections. Despite the availability of preprocessing tools for MSI data, building end-to-end workflows suitable for deep learning remains challenging. We have developed a fully automated pipeline for transformation of raw Matrix-Assisted Laser Desorption/Ionization Mass Spectrometry imaging (MALDI-MSI) data into deep-learning-ready image patches for binary pancreatic tissue classification (tumoral vs non-tumoral) and built individual models for recognition of tumoral regions in spectral images. Images resulting from the proposed pipeline and from reference interactive workflow are very close in terms of image quality metric mean squared error (MSE). The proposed pipeline computational time is of 20 min in average compared to a full working day of the reference interactive workflow. The models achieved 0.94 accuracy on high-resolution spectra and a mean accuracy of 0.59 on lower-resolution spectra.

</details>


### [103] [CascadeJEPA: Enhancing 3D Geometric Representations via Spatially Coupled Cascaded Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22686)
*Kailai Zhou,Ziqi Chen,Xiaoning Xu,Yuefeng Wu,Zixuan Fu,Zijun Lin,Zhihao Li,Youwei Pang,Xiaoqi Zhao,Xun Cao,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CascadeJEPA通过全网格上下文编码与阶段间交叉注意力的级联预测，缓解I-JEPA中语义与几何表示失衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到I-JEPA在预训练中语义识别提升的同时3D几何表示退化，推测原因是：在空间上下文不完整的情况下预测高度抽象的潜在表示，使模型偏向语义规律性而损失细粒度几何信息。

Method: 提出CascadeJEPA，一种空间耦合的级联预测框架：通过全网格（full-grid）上下文编码获得更完整的空间信息，并在预测阶段之间引入交叉注意力（cross-attention）以增强patch级交互，同时基本保留原始I-JEPA架构。

Result: 在多个3D几何任务上显著优于I-JEPA，同时保持较强的语义识别性能；其优势还延伸到未来特征预测与世界模型规划任务。

Conclusion: 增强的几何表示能够更好地支撑预测式世界建模，表明在JEPA框架中通过稀疏-完整上下文耦合与级联预测可同时兼顾语义与几何学习。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for predictive visual representation learning. However, our analysis of I-JEPA reveals a growing discrepancy between semantic and geometric learning: improved semantic recognition during pretraining is accompanied by degraded 3D geometric representations. We hypothesize that this partly stems from predicting highly abstract latent representations with incomplete spatial context, favoring semantic regularities over fine-grained geometric information. To address this issue, we introduce CascadeJEPA, a spatially coupled cascaded prediction framework that promotes richer patch-level interactions through full-grid context encoding and cross-attention between prediction stages, while largely preserving the original I-JEPA architecture. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantial improvements over I-JEPA across multiple 3D geometric tasks while maintaining strong semantic recognition performance. Furthermore, its advantages extend to future feature forecasting and world model planning, highlighting the potential of enhanced geometric representations to support predictive world modeling.

</details>


### [104] [TANGO: Real-World Unregistered RGB-Guided Hyperspectral Super-Resolution with Two-Camera Acquisitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22699)
*Matteo Kolyszko,Marco Buzzelli,Simone Bianco,Raimondo Schettini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个大规模公开的未配准RGB引导高光谱超分辨数据集DUET（403个双相机室内外场景），并设计TANGO方法：先做全局端到端对齐补偿主导跨相机位移，再用由粗到细的局部特征对齐修正剩余不一致，并用学习门控在无法一致匹配处衰减RGB引导，在DUET与REAL上均达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未配准HSI超分需从低分辨率高光谱观测与未配准高分辨率RGB参考中恢复高分辨率HSI。现有方法虽已处理模态间几何错位，但缺乏基于真实双相机RGB-HSI采集、且带高分辨率HSI参考的大规模公开基准，评估受限。

Method: 构建DUET数据集（403个室内外场景，RGB与高光谱相机分离采集）；提出TANGO（Two-camera AligNment with Gated Occlusions）：端到端全局对齐补偿主导跨相机位移，由粗到细的局部特征对齐细化残余不一致，并用学习门控机制在双视图无法一致匹配处衰减RGB引导。

Result: TANGO在DUET与REAL数据集上所有评估尺度因子下均取得SOTA；在DUET上PSNR最高领先最强对手2.29 dB，同时FLOPs减少2.5倍。

Conclusion: 真实双相机场景下，显式全局+局部对齐与遮挡感知门控是解决未配准RGB引导HSI超分的有效途径；DUET为该任务提供了大规模真实基准，代码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: Unregistered hyperspectral image super-resolution aims to recover a high-resolution HSI from a low-resolution hyperspectral observation and an unregistered high-resolution RGB reference. Although recent methods have begun to address the geometric misalignment between the two modalities, their evaluation is still constrained by the lack of a large-scale public benchmark based on real two-camera RGB--HSI acquisitions with a high-resolution HSI reference. To address this gap, we introduce DUET, the first large-scale public dataset for unregistered RGB-guided hyperspectral super-resolution, comprising 403 indoor and outdoor scenes captured with two separate RGB and hyperspectral cameras. We further propose TANGO (Two-camera AligNment with Gated Occlusions), which first compensates for the dominant cross-camera displacement through end-to-end global alignment and then refines the remaining inconsistencies with a coarse-to-fine local feature alignment. A learned gating mechanism further attenuates the RGB guidance where the two views cannot be consistently matched. Experimental results show that TANGO achieves state-of-the-art performance on both DUET and REAL across all evaluated scale factors, outperforming the strongest competitor on DUET by up to 2.29 dB in PSNR while requiring 2.5$\times$ fewer FLOPs. The code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/FagiolottoConSombrero/TANGO-DUET

</details>


### [105] [Social Signal Bench: Evidence-Weighted Evaluation of Co-occurring Social Signals in Video and Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22704)
*Siddharth Ravi,Anders R. Bargum,Hugo Anselme,Patrizio Cugia di Sant'Orsola,Alberto Cardaci,Flora Kincso Parragi,Vincent Borowiec,Line H. Clemmensen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Social Signal Bench（47 小时密集标注社会信号基准，含视频 25 小时/10 类信号与音频 22 小时/6 类信号）及 Evidence-Weighted F1（EW-F1）评测指标，公开训练集+私有专家测试集覆盖四个对话领域；在其上后训练的 7B 开源模型在视频上超越通用前沿模型、音频上持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会信号（兴趣、犹豫等）具有上下文依赖且可能在对话中共现，现有情感计算基准很少评测共现信号；传统多标签 F1 又忽略语义关联与标注者分歧。

Method: 构建 Social Signal Bench：47 小时密集标注数据，分 Social Signal Bench Video（25 小时、10 类信号、音视频）与 Social Signal Bench Audio（22 小时、6 类信号、纯音频）；每套由公开训练集（10 小时专家标注 + 其余伪标签的在线对话视频）与私有专家标注测试集（覆盖四个对话领域）配对。提出 EW-F1：以标注者证据加权参考标签，并通过逐片段最优匹配对语义相关预测给予分级信用。

Result: 受控扰动实验表明 EW-F1 能区分语义相关错误与无关错误，而传统 F1 无法做到；在统一评测协议下，经 Social Signal Bench 后训练的 7B 开源模型在视频任务上优于通用前沿模型，音频任务上与其持平。

Conclusion: 共现社会信号的识别需要专门的基准与考虑语义/标注分歧的指标；EW-F1 与小规模后训练模型的有效性表明，领域对齐的数据与评测可显著缩小与通用大模型的差距。

Abstract: Recognizing social signals such as interest and hesitation requires interpreting context-dependent behaviors that may co-occur in conversation. Existing affective computing benchmarks rarely evaluate such co-occurring signals, and conventional multi-label F1 overlooks semantic relationships and annotator disagreement. We introduce Social Signal Bench, a 47-hour densely annotated benchmark for the evaluation of social signal recognition and engagement, comprising two datasets: Social Signal Bench Video (25 hours), covering ten social signals in audiovisual recordings, and Social Signal Bench Audio (22 hours), covering six signals in audio-only recordings. Each dataset pairs a public training set of online conversational video, of which 10 hours are expert-annotated and the remainder pseudo-labeled, with a private expert-labeled test set spanning four conversational domains. We also propose Evidence-Weighted F1 (EW-F1), which weights reference labels by annotator evidence and assigns graded credit to semantically related predictions through optimal per-clip matching. Controlled perturbations demonstrate that EW-F1 distinguishes semantically related errors from unrelated ones, unlike conventional F1. Under a shared evaluation protocol, 7B open models post-trained on Social Signal Bench outperform general-purpose frontier models on video and match them on audio.

</details>


### [106] [Know When to Zoom: How Small Is Too Small for Vision-Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22709)
*Siddharth Vohra,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文量化了在线视觉语言模型在大图像上的细节丢失问题：默认设置下模型可读的最小文字尺寸从≤6像素升至10–13像素（7680×4320图）；分块（≤9块）使Claude与Gemini准确率提升11.1–14.5个百分点，并使GPT-6.1 Sol从50.7%升至83.7%；两步式缩放以约40%的图像token达到与分块相当的效果；Claude与Gemini在细节丢失时约半数会承认无法判断，但在失败模式上差异显著（Gemini有43%–48%给出自信错误答案，Claude仅5%–9%）；以Claude自身"不确定"回复作为分块触发器可在62%–70% token下接近全量分块（差距1.4个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线视觉语言模型服务常通过缩小图像或直接拒收来处理大图，导致小目标（如8000×6000航拍图中20像素宽的车辆，实际到达模型时仅约6像素）细节在模型"看到"之前就已丢失，而这一损失程度及补救手段缺乏系统量化。

Method: 对来自四家厂商的七个模型进行受控评测：采用"视力表"式的印刷编码测试测量最小可辨细节尺寸，并使用601道关于真实照片中小物体的问答；比较默认设置、分块（最多9块）与两步式缩放（模型先指定所需区域再取景）等策略的准确率与图像token消耗；分析各模型在细节缺失时的拒答与错误模式；并评估基于细节尺寸的预检查与基于模型"不确定"回复的事后触发两种分块调度策略。

Result: 模型半数可读的最小文字尺寸由小图上的≤6像素增至7680×4320图默认设置下的10–13像素；分块（≤9块）使Claude与Gemini准确率提升11.1–14.5个百分点，并通过减少拒收使GPT-6.1 Sol从50.7%提升至83.7%；两步式缩放在Gemini上持平或优于分块，在Claude上差距在2.5个百分点内，且仅消耗约40%的图像token；细节丢失时Claude与Gemini约半数会表示无法判断，但Gemini有43%–48%给出自信的错误答案，Claude仅5%–9%；基于细节尺寸的预检查会丢失分块大部分收益，而以Claude的不确定回复为触发条件可在62%–70% token下达到距全量分块1.4个百分点的表现。

Conclusion: 论文将上述发现提炼为智能体何时应当放大的五条规则，并公开视力表测试、全部模型回答与代码（https://siddvoh.com/know-when-to-zoom）。核心结论是：在线视觉模型服务的图像缩放与拒收会显著削弱小目标识别能力，而分块与自适应缩放能有效恢复细节；同时不同模型在不可判情形下的可靠性差异巨大，选择"何时放大"的触发机制应结合模型的自我不确定性信号而非仅依赖图像侧预判。

Abstract: Online vision-language models often miss small details in large images, because the service shrinks the image, or rejects it, before the model ever sees it. A car 20 pixels wide in an 8000 by 6000 drone photo reaches Claude about 6 pixels wide, and OpenAI's default rejects the photo outright. We measure how much detail survives for seven models from four vendors, using an eye exam of printed codes and 601 questions about small objects in real photos. The smallest text a model reads half the time grows from 6 pixels or less on a small image to a predicted 10 to 13 pixels on a 7680 by 4320 photo at default settings. Zooming wins much of the detail back. Splitting each photo into up to nine tiles raises accuracy by 11.1 to 14.5 percentage points for Claude and Gemini, and by avoiding rejections it lifts GPT-6.1 Sol from 50.7% to 83.7%. A two-step zoom, in which the model first names the region it needs, matches or beats tiles on Gemini and comes within 2.5 points of them on Claude, with about 40% of their image tokens. When a detail is lost, Claude and Gemini both say they cannot tell about half the time, but they fail differently in the rest: Gemini gives a confident wrong answer in 43% to 48% of these cases, Claude in only 5% to 9%. A check based on detail size, run before the call, misses most of the gain from tiles on Claude and Gemini, but Claude's own reply is a reliable trigger: tiling only after an unsure reply comes within 1.4 points of always tiling with 62% to 70% of the image tokens. We turn these findings into five rules for when an agent should zoom, and release the eye exam, every model answer and code at https://siddvoh.com/know-when-to-zoom.

</details>


### [107] [PCRG: Training-Free Pruning and Caching of Reference Tokens for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22717)
*Siyuan Wang,Haoran Li,Yi Yue,Tak L. Fung,Quan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的即插即用方法PCRG，利用VAE低层冗余度r和VLM高层重要性g将参考token分为剪枝/缓存/保留三级，加速参考到视频扩散Transformer生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考到视频扩散Transformer将参考与生成token打包进全注意力序列，参考token约占一半，因注意力代价随序列长度平方增长而占据更大计算份额；但其中多数token是静态、平滑或与输出无关的冗余内容。

Method: 双信号三级分类：(1) 来自VAE latent的低层冗余度r，某token在时间或邻域上几乎不变则新增内容少；(2) 来自VLM条件器的高层重要性g，即prompt及首/尾帧对该token的注意力。非冗余token保留；冗余token若重要则缓存、否则剪枝。剪枝token在首步前移出序列且不改变RoPE位置；缓存token的K/V仅计算一次并复用；保留token正常处理。训练无关、即插即用，不改变注意力计算方式与执行步数。

Result: 在已蒸馏至8步并int8量化的MiniMax-H3上，V2V-Bench端到端加速1.57x、VACE-Benchmark加速1.49x，VBench质量分别仅下降0.24%和0.17%。与步缓存/稀疏注意力叠加：TeaCache下1.86x、Spark下1.82x、Veda下2.15x，保真度几乎不变。

Conclusion: 通过缩短序列而非改变注意力计算或采样步数，PCRG在保持生成质量的同时显著加速参考条件视频生成，并可与现有步缓存和稀疏注意力方法正交叠加。

Abstract: Reference-to-video diffusion transformers pack the variational autoencoder (VAE) latents of all references and the generated tokens into one full-attention sequence, in which reference tokens make up about half. Because attention cost grows quadratically with sequence length, these tokens take up an even larger share of the attention computation, yet most of them are static, smooth or irrelevant to the output. To accelerate reference-conditioned generation, we propose pruning and caching of reference tokens for video generation (PCRG), a training-free, plug-and-play method that uses two signals the model already computes to assign each reference token to one of three tiers, namely pruned, cached or kept. The first is a low-level redundancy r from VAE latents, since a token whose latent barely changes over time or across its neighbors adds little new content. The second is a high-level importance g from the vision-language-model (VLM) conditioner, where the attention that the prompt and any first or last frames pay to a token tells whether the output needs to read it. Tokens that are not redundant are kept, and redundant tokens are cached if important and pruned otherwise. Pruned tokens leave the sequence before the first step with all rotary position embedding positions unchanged. Cached tokens have their keys and values computed once and reused, and kept tokens are processed as usual. On MiniMax-H3, already distilled to 8 timesteps and quantized to int8, PCRG is 1.57x faster end to end on our V2V-Bench and 1.49x faster on the public VACE-Benchmark, while the quality of VBench drops by only 0.24% and 0.17%, respectively. PCRG shortens the sequence rather than changing how attention is computed or which steps are run, so it also stacks on step caching and sparse attention. On V2V-Bench it reaches 1.86x with TeaCache, 1.82x with Spark at nearly unchanged fidelity and 2.15x with Veda.

</details>


### [108] [Practice in Text, Act in Pixels: Correcting Where Perception Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22722)
*Zhongling Xu,Hairong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出，在低成本视觉屏幕阅读器支持的GUI智能体中，文本视图训练的策略迁移到截图时性能大幅下降。作者发现阅读器的误读具有系统性且集中复现在同一界面元素上，因此提出"阅读器对齐"（用截图-文本视图配对微调阅读器），而非对策略注入噪声或直接训练策略。同等截图预算下，对齐阅读器带来80.9%全任务成功率，而训练策略仅55.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体可从界面文本视图廉价学习，并通过视觉-语言屏幕阅读器在截图上执行动作。但在低成本阅读器的模拟移动平台上，文本上能完成多数任务的策略在截图上大量失败。核心问题是：如何用少量真实截图预算最有效地弥合这一文本-视觉差距。自然补救方案（按阅读器错误率匹配噪声）忽略误读的结构性。

Method: 先诊断误读性质：发现阅读器误读是系统性的，在同一界面元素上反复出现。设置预先指定的对照实验，比较噪声匹配与直接在截图上训练策略。提出"阅读器对齐"：用截图与其文本视图配对数据微调阅读器，使其输出每屏的文本视图（与文本训练时的输入一致），策略本身保持不变。还比较了相同标注截图下适配阅读器与适配策略的效果，并测试单个对齐阅读器对不同文本训练策略的增益。

Result: 在新任务实例上，将截图预算用于阅读器对齐时全任务成功率达80.9%，用于训练策略时为55.6%；噪声匹配在预设比较中未优于截图直接训练策略。相同标注截图下，适配阅读器优于适配策略；单个对齐阅读器无需重训即可提升所试的每一个文本训练策略。

Conclusion: 截图预算应投向感知端的"阅读器对齐"而非策略端。利用误读的结构性在源头修正，能高效弥合sim-to-real差距，且形成可复用的阅读器组件，具良好泛化性与性价比。

Abstract: GUI agents can learn cheaply from a text view of an interface and act on screenshots through a vision-language screen reader. Yet on a simulated mobile platform with a low-cost reader, policies that complete most tasks in text fail on many of the same tasks on screenshots. We ask where a small budget of real screenshots is best spent to close this gap. We find that the reader's misreads are systematic and recur on the same interface elements. Noise matched to the reader's error rates, a natural sim-to-real remedy, misses this structure, and in a pre-specified comparison it did not beat training the policy directly on screenshots. We therefore correct the misreads at their source with reader alignment, which fine-tunes the reader on screenshots paired with their text views. The reader learns to output each screen's text view, as in text practice, and the policy stays unchanged. Spent on the reader, the screenshot budget gives 80.9% full-task success on new task instances, against 55.6% when spent on training the policy. Given the same labeled screenshots, adapting the reader also beats adapting the policy, and a single aligned reader improves every text-trained policy we tried without retraining any of them.

</details>


### [109] [ConserveEdit: Source-Anchored Transactions for Drift-Bounded Iterative Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22724)
*Shuyang Jiang,Fucheng Deng,Yuchuan Luo,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ConserveEdit 是一个编辑器无关的迭代图像编辑控制器，把每步精修当作一次"合约事务"：用一次编译的源图合约区分"可改区域（含用于阴影/反射的有界光晕）"与"必须保持不变的内容（带明确容差）"；所有像素在授权区域外保持位精确，漂移与迭代次数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令驱动的图像编辑器单次局部编辑效果可信，但一旦放入迭代精修回路，每个被接受的输出都会成为下一次的输入，于是请求区域之外的意外改变会逐步累积。作者希望找到一种与具体编辑器解耦、能把这种累积漂移从机制上封死的方法。

Method: 把每步精修定义为对"合约"的事务：合约从源图一次性编译，分离可改的目标授权区域（带用于阴影与反射的有界光晕）与需存活的内容（含显式容差）。冻结编辑器产生的候选经"源锚定掩码"合成，并通过三项独立测试——目标进展、受保护区域漂移、图像质量——全部通过才提交，否则回滚并修复。

Result: 在 1053 条 MagicBrush 测试编辑上配合冻结的 Qwen-Image-Edit：直接调用即改变掩码外内容（掩码外 PSNR 12.1 dB）；经源锚定掩码合成后掩码外位精确，DINO 相似度由 0.67 升至 0.85。掩码必要但不充分：掩码直接编辑在 48% 的轮次上改动受保护的人脸或文字，掩码标量选择主要靠"放弃"规避（受限成功率 23%）。ConserveEdit 因漂移测试能指名违规实体而改为收缩支撑区域：违规率 2%、受限成功率 53%；在已知真值的模拟器中受限成功率由 55.0% 提升到 61.8%。

Conclusion: 分离式合约测试优于任何固定标量权衡，可把掩码外漂移从机制上锁死，且不随迭代次数增长。当前主要瓶颈在于自动定位/掩码生成；代码与日志将开源。

Abstract: Instruction-driven image editors produce convincing local edits in one pass, but inside an iterative refinement loop every accepted output becomes the next input, so unintended changes outside the requested region accumulate. We present ConserveEdit, an editor-agnostic controller that treats each refinement step as a transaction against a contract compiled once from the source image. The contract separates what may change (the authorized region of each target, with a bounded halo for shadows and reflections) from what must survive (protected content with explicit tolerances). Candidates from a frozen editor are composed through a source-anchored mask and committed only if target progress, drift on protected content, and image quality pass as separate tests; otherwise the step is rolled back and repaired. For any editor, this keeps every pixel outside the authorized region bit-exact and bounds drift independently of the number of iterations, whereas no fixed scalar trade-off reproduces the separate tests. On all 1053 MagicBrush test edits with a frozen Qwen-Image-Edit, masking is necessary but not sufficient. A direct call already alters the rest of the image (12.1 dB PSNR outside the mask); composed through the source-anchored mask, it stays bit-exact there and its DINO similarity to the ground truth rises from 0.67 to 0.85, matching ConserveEdit, so this gain comes from the mask. Next to automatic masks, however, masked direct editing changes a protected face or text on 48% of turns, and masked scalar selection avoids this mostly by abstaining (23% constrained success). Because its drift test names the violated entity, ConserveEdit shrinks the support instead (2% violations, 53% constrained success); in a simulator with known ground truth it raises constrained success from 55.0% to 61.8%. Automatic grounding remains the main bottleneck. Code and logs will be released.

</details>


### [110] [SlopeGuard: Towards Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection via Radial Spectral Slope Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22727)
*Cheng Fu,Yiheng Li,Yang Yang,Wenzheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SlopeGuard，将径向光谱斜率（Radial Spectral Slope）与语义特征结合，实现跨生成器的AI图像检测，在AIGCDetectBench、GenImage、AIGIBench上分别取得97.5%、97.8%、85.1%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器易过拟合生成器特有的伪影，难以泛化到未见合成管线；频域方法学习的高维光谱模式仍保留生成器依赖变化，需要更具迁移性的频谱描述符。

Method: 分析发现径向光谱斜率（全局光谱衰减的紧凑描述符）在真实与生成图像间存在系统性但依赖生成器的差异，故仅作为独立判据不足；据此提出SlopeGuard：斜率感知的数据处理 + 斜率引导的特征学习，构建基于patch的信息性视图，融合径向光谱斜率与语义特征，并按光谱异常程度自适应调整先验保留。

Result: AIGCDetectBench 97.5%、GenImage 97.8%、AIGIBench 85.1% 平均准确率（SOTA），并在模糊、裁剪、JPEG压缩与加性噪声下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 低维、可迁移的径向光谱斜率与语义表示互补结合，可显著提升跨生成器泛化性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors often overfit to generator-specific artifacts, hindering generalization to unseen synthesis pipelines. Existing frequency-based methods usually learn high-dimensional spectral patterns that may preserve generator-dependent variations. We explore Radial Spectral Slope, a compact descriptor of global spectral decay that captures transferable spectral trends while suppressing fine-grained variations. Our analysis shows that real and generated images exhibit systematic but generator-dependent slope differences, indicating that slope provides useful forensic evidence but is insufficient as a standalone decision criterion. This motivates combining spectral slope with semantic representations for more robust detection. Based on this insight, we propose SlopeGuard, which couples slope-aware data processing with slope guided feature learning to construct informative patch-based views, integrate Radial Spectral Slope with semantic features, and adapt prior preservation according to spectral atypicality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SlopeGuard achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracies of 97.5% on AIGCDetectBench, 97.8% on GenImage, and 85.1% on AIGIBench, while maintaining competitive performance under blur, cropping, JPEG compression, and additive noise.

</details>


### [111] [LoopZip: Visual Token Compression for Efficient Looped Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22728)
*Weizhong Huang,Zhicheng Liu,Yan Zhang,Fei Chao,Xiawu Zheng,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopZip：面向循环视觉语言模型（Looped VLMs）的免训练视觉token压缩算法，通过视觉聚合与跨调用选择及权重绑定值校正，在50%剪枝率保留97.16%性能，并在多推理框架下降低约58%显存、获得2.01–2.47倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环VLM通过复用共享层加深计算，以更少参数逼近大模型能力，但长视觉序列被反复处理带来显著的计算与显存开销。视觉token压缩可缓解该问题，然而现有方法多为直接选择原始token或选择+聚合：在循环模型中，前者忽视token重要性随调用次数变化、丢弃后续所需信息；后者缺乏对跨调用输出变化的联合考虑，信息保留受限。

Method: 提出免训练压缩算法LoopZip，包含两部分：(1) 视觉聚合——在循环主干前构建紧凑候选池；(2) 跨调用选择——对同一共享注意力层的前两次调用取指令注意力均值，以保留两种状态均需要的token；(3) 权重绑定值校正——对预选择层的两个输出联合拟合共享的视觉值调整，降低跨状态的输出偏差。

Result: 在2个开源循环VLM、11个基准、3种剪枝率上实验：LoopVL-1B在50%剪枝率保留原模型97.16%性能；87.5%剪枝率下超最强基线5.36%。在Hugging Face Transformers、vLLM、SGLang下，峰值显存降低58.36%–58.98%，端到端加速2.01–2.47倍。

Conclusion: LoopZip以免训练方式有效解决循环VLM中视觉token压缩的跨调用信息保留问题，在保持高精度的同时显著降低显存与延迟，具备良好部署实用性。

Abstract: Looped vision-language models (VLMs) deepen computation by reusing shared layers, approaching larger models' capabilities with fewer parameters and easing the storage and deployment burden of stacking independent layers. However, repeatedly processing long visual sequences still incurs substantial compute and GPU memory costs. Visual token compression reduces these costs by shortening sequences. Existing methods mainly select original tokens directly or combine selection with aggregation. In looped models, the former may overlook changing token importance across calls and discard information needed later; the latter lack joint consideration of output changes across calls, limiting information preservation. We propose LoopZip, a training-free visual token compression algorithm addressing both issues. Visual aggregation builds a compact candidate pool before the looped backbone, and cross-invocation selection averages instruction attention from the first two existing calls of the same shared attention layer to retain tokens needed by both states. Weight-tied value correction then jointly fits shared visual-value adjustments to both pre-selection layer outputs, reducing output deviations across states. To validate LoopZip's effectiveness, we conduct extensive experiments covering two open-source looped VLMs, eleven benchmarks, and three pruning ratios. On LoopVL-1B, LoopZip retains 97.16% of the original model's performance at 50% pruning ratio and exceeds the strongest baseline by 5.36% at 87.5% pruning ratio. Across Hugging Face Transformers, vLLM, and SGLang, LoopVL-1B with LoopZip reduces peak allocated GPU memory by 58.36%-58.98% and achieves 2.01-2.47x end-to-end speedups over the original model.

</details>


### [112] [Evolve in Panorama, Render in View: Panoramic World State Evolution for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22733)
*Longtao Jiang,Mingfei Han,Jianmin Bao,Tingjun Dai,Jinjie Wei,Zhiran Zhang,Xin Tao,Zhihui Li,Salman Khan,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoEvo 用全景视频扩散模型的潜空间持续演化世界状态，使相机转开再转回时场景仍自然演化，再经透视渲染器输出高清视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在视角转开又转回时，视野外区域要么被冻结、要么丢失物体或外观改变，因为未被观测的内容缺乏持续变化的状态表示。

Method: 提出全景演化器（Panoramic Evolver），在全景视频扩散模型潜空间中演化世界状态，保证每一时刻都存在完整视角方向；构建事件中心的真实 360° 数据集 PanoEvo-Data，并粗到细训练图像到全景视频模型；再用透视渲染器（基于透视视频扩散模型），通过潜空间投影与方向受限交叉注意力渲染清晰视图。

Result: 在 WRBench 上于 23 个公开评测模型中取得最佳综合得分，在 STEVO-Bench 上取得最高总分。

Conclusion: 以全景潜空间作为持久世界表示可有效解决视野外观一致性与持续演化问题，配合透视渲染器可兼顾全景一致性与视图质量。

Abstract: Video world models should maintain a world that keeps evolving as the viewpoint changes. Yet when the camera turns away and returns, current models often freeze the unobserved scene, lose objects, or change their appearance, because content outside the view has no representation that keeps changing. We present PanoEvo, which uses a Panoramic Evolver to evolve the world state in the latent space of a panoramic video diffusion model, where every viewing direction is present at every time step, so the scene evolves whether or not it is observed. Existing panoramic video models are not trained to evolve a scene from a single image or the objects within it, so we build PanoEvo-Data, an event-centric dataset from real 360° videos, and train the Panoramic Evolver coarse-to-fine as an image-to-panoramic-video model. Since each view covers only a small part of the low-resolution panorama, directly projected views are blurry and often show low-quality details or structural errors. A Perspective Renderer, built on a perspective video diffusion model, reads this state via latent-space projection and direction-restricted cross-attention to render sharp, high-quality views. PanoEvo achieves the best overall score among 23 publicly evaluated models on WRBench and the highest total score on STEVO-Bench. Code, models, and data will be released.

</details>


### [113] [RefCloth: Transferable Adversarial Tablecloths for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22736)
*Shaozhen Liu,Zhiwei Li,Jiayang Sun,Shuangjun Du,Yi Zhong,Shenrui Ma,Yaozu Liu,Qi Li,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RefCloth：在冻结源策略上优化一块持久的对抗桌布纹理，可零样本迁移到未见任务与目标模型（OpenVLA-OFT→π0/π0.5），平均成功率下降62.50和43.75个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环VLA策略持续依赖多视角相机，但现有视觉攻击多用2D补丁劫持传感器流或修改目标物体网格，后者在操作对象变化时需重新训练，缺乏跨任务、跨模型的持久性与可迁移性。

Method: 在世界坐标绑定的一块桌布上做有界纹理参数化优化，结合共享表面UV变换以泛化到变化视角，并用同路径干净参考在初始化时消除静态渲染偏差，桥接可微渲染与原生渲染器；不修改任务物体与相机。

Result: LIBERO四个套件原生仿真中，单块冻结纹理从OpenVLA-OFT迁移至π0与π0.5，相较未优化桌布平均成功率分别下降62.50与43.75个百分点，远超幅度与变化匹配的随机对照；双视角审计与目标闭环相机干预显示移动腕部视角暴露的单独影响为主视角的两倍以上且具强协同效应，消融验证UV多样化与路径校准的作用。

Conclusion: 世界绑定纹理可作为跨任务、跨模型、零样本的持久对抗载体，多视角（尤其移动腕部视角）暴露与UV多样化是攻击有效性的关键，为VLA策略鲁棒性评估提供新范式。

Abstract: Closed-loop vision-language-action (VLA) policies rely continuously on multi-view cameras, yet existing visual attacks typically hijack sensor streams with 2D patches or alter target object meshes that require retraining when manipulation objects change. We introduce RefCloth, a framework that optimizes one persistent adversarial tablecloth on a frozen source policy and deploys it zero-shot across unseen tasks and target models without modifying task objects or cameras. Throughout manipulation, static third-person and moving wrist views observe this shared world-bound texture under evolving occlusions. To generalize across shifting surface views while bridging the gap between differentiable and native renderers, RefCloth combines bounded texture parameterization with shared surface UV transformations and a same-path clean reference that removes static rendering offsets at initialization. Across four LIBERO suites in native simulation, one frozen texture transfers from OpenVLA-OFT to $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$, reducing success relative to an unoptimized cloth by 62.50 and 43.75 percentage points on average and far exceeding amplitude- and variation-matched random controls. Dual-view audits and target closed-loop camera interventions show that moving wrist exposure drives over twice the standalone drop of the main view with strong synergy, while ablations confirm the roles of UV diversification and path calibration.

</details>


### [114] [GaussianMamba: Geometric and Relational State Space Scanning of 3D Gaussian Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22742)
*A. Enes Doruk,Ozgur Erkent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussianMamba：面向3D语义占据预测的双分支Mamba框架，按各向异性几何与特征-语义关系构造高斯图元扫描序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D语义占据预测需表征复杂空间结构并建模场景上下文；3D高斯图元提供稀疏自适应表示，Mamba擅长序列建模，但高斯图元不规则、无序，常规空间排序难以体现其各向异性几何与特征-语义关系，序列构造困难。

Method: 双分支Mamba框架：(1) 基于图元尺度与方向推导各向异性高斯支撑，构造保局部性的遍历序；(2) 按特征-语义亲和度重排空间分组，并用几何加权的语义差异调整局部特征；两分支序列分别经独立Mamba模块处理，再通过感知支撑的融合机制（结合不同扫描路径带来的局部特征差异）进行特征融合。

Result: 在nuScenes-SurroundOcc与SSCBench-KITTI-360上，IoU与mIoU均稳定优于GaussianFormer与GaussianFormer-2。

Conclusion: 将高斯图元的几何各向异性与特征-语义关系显式引入互补扫描策略，可有效解决无序图元的序列化问题，双分支Mamba结合支撑感知融合能提升3D语义占据预测性能。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction requires representing complex spatial structures and modeling contextual relationships across a scene. 3D Gaussian primitives provide a sparse and adaptive representation of scene geometry and semantics, while Mamba offers efficient sequence modeling for capturing dependencies among primitives. However, Gaussian primitives are irregular and unordered, making sequence construction challenging, as conventional spatial ordering may overlook their anisotropic geometry and feature-semantic relationships. In this paper, we present GaussianMamba, a dual-branch Mamba framework that explicitly incorporates Gaussian-specific geometric and feature-semantic relationships into complementary scanning strategies. The first branch refines a locality-preserving traversal using anisotropic Gaussian support derived from primitive scale and orientation. The second branch reorders spatially organized groups according to feature-semantic affinity and adjusts local features using geometry-weighted semantic differences. The resulting sequences are processed by separate Mamba modules, and their features are fused through a support-aware mechanism that incorporates local feature discrepancies arising from the distinct scanning paths. Experiments on nuScenes-SurroundOcc and SSCBench-KITTI-360 demonstrate consistent improvements over GaussianFormer and GaussianFormer-2 in both IoU and mIoU.

</details>


### [115] [Teaching What to Look For: Privileged Perception Questions for Agentic On-Policy Distillation in Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22755)
*Yifan Xu,Ruopeng Gao,Zhixiao Wang,Jiafu Tang,Yichun Yang,Zihao Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PQ-OPD（感知问题-同策略蒸馏），用仅在训练期使用的感知问题（PQs）引导视频字幕智能体主动寻找关键片段与局部区域，并通过同策略蒸馏将该引导内化到学生模型的推理与工具调用中，推理时不再需要PQs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 详细视频字幕需要忠实覆盖物体、动作与时间事件，但固定帧采样易遗漏短暂动作、小目标等细粒度证据，因此需要主动探索视频的智能体。然而此类行为难以训练：与视频问答不同，通用字幕指令并不指明应获取哪些视觉证据。

Method: 提出PQ-OPD框架：训练阶段引入仅训练可见的感知问题（PQs），仅指明应检查什么而不泄露答案，促使教师模型更全面地探索视频并聚焦相关证据；在学生模型生成的轨迹上进行同策略蒸馏，将这种引导迁移到学生的推理过程、工具调用与字幕生成中，推理阶段无需PQs。

Result: 在三个详细字幕基准与两个字幕到问答基准上，PQ-OPD在不同教师-学生规模与输入帧预算下均稳定优于对应的未训练智能体；在既定评测协议下，于DREAM-1K、CaReBench、ShortVidBench、MotionBench与VidCapBench-AE上取得所比较开源模型中最优的主指标结果。消融实验、时间覆盖与答案泄露分析表明学生将训练期引导内化为更有效的视频探索策略。

Conclusion: 以不泄露答案的感知问题作为训练期引导、结合同策略蒸馏，可有效训练视频字幕智能体的主动感知行为，并在推理零额外开销的前提下提升多项基准表现。

Abstract: Detailed video captioning requires comprehensive and faithful descriptions of objects, actions, and temporal events. Fixed-frame sampling, however, can miss brief motions, small objects, and other fine-grained evidence, motivating captioning agents that actively seek relevant moments and inspect local regions. Training such behavior is challenging because, unlike video question answering, generic captioning instructions do not specify what visual evidence should be acquired. We propose Perception-Question On-Policy Distillation (PQ-OPD), which uses training-only Perception Questions (PQs) to guide active video perception. PQs specify what should be inspected without revealing the corresponding answers, encouraging the teacher to explore the video more comprehensively and focus on relevant visual evidence. Along trajectories generated by the student, on-policy distillation transfers this guidance to the student's reasoning, tool calls, and captions; no PQs are required at inference. Across three detailed-captioning and two caption-to-QA benchmarks, PQ-OPD consistently improves the corresponding untrained agents across teacher-student scales and input-frame budgets. Under our evaluation protocol, PQ-OPD achieves the strongest primary-metric results among the compared open-source models on DREAM-1K, CaReBench, ShortVidBench, MotionBench, and VidCapBench-AE. Our ablations, together with analysis of temporal coverage and answer leakage, further indicates that the student internalizes the training-time guidance as a more effective video exploration policy.

</details>


### [116] [ForceBody: Force-Paired Parametric Body Motion with Torque Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22769)
*Joonwoo Kwon,Yufei Zhang,Xiaoming Liu,Zijun Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForceBody 是首个将真值力监督引入参数化人体模型（SKEL）的数据集，覆盖 10,386 条运动序列（970 万帧、26.9 小时、140 名受试者），同时提供地面反作用力、逆动力学关节力矩及逐帧逐关节的力矩不确定性标注，并给出六种架构的基准结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力与力矩是人体运动的核心物理量，但学习式运动重建与生成流水线普遍缺失力信息。已有带力标注的数据集其运动序列与主流参数化人体模型不兼容；且现有数据集的关节力矩多由逆动力学计算而得，对输入运动学质量高度敏感，却未量化其估计可靠性。

Method: 将 SKEL 参数化人体模型与地面反作用力、逆动力学关节力矩配对构建 ForceBody；通过 Monte Carlo 采样贯穿逆动力学流程，为每条力矩标签生成逐帧、逐关节的不确定性标注；在 SKEL 运动序列上对 MLP、Conv1D、LSTM、GRU、Mamba2、Transformer 六种架构进行地面反作用力与关节力矩预测的基准评测。

Result: 数据规模为 10,386 条运动试验、970 万帧、26.9 小时、140 名受试者；六种架构完成基准对比；示例应用中以逐样本不确定性加权微调 Transformer，关节力矩估计 MAE 降低 5%。

Conclusion: ForceBody 填补了参数化人体表示下真值力监督与力矩可靠性量化的空白，为物理感知的运动建模提供了带不确定性标注的基准资源。

Abstract: Forces and torques drive human motion, central to biomechanics, robotics, and physics-aware animation, yet they are often absent from learning-based motion pipelines for body reconstruction and generation. Some motion datasets provide force annotations, but the paired motion sequences are incompatible with the parametric body models used in modern learning pipelines. In addition, existing datasets typically obtain joint torques through inverse dynamics, which is inherently sensitive to the quality of the input kinematics; however, they do not quantify the reliability of these torque estimates. We close this gap with ForceBody, the first dataset to bring ground truth force supervision into a parametric body representation, shipped with per-sample torque uncertainty. ForceBody pairs the SKEL body model with ground reaction forces and inverse-dynamics joint torques across 10,386 motion trials (9.7M frames, 26.9 hours) from 140 subjects. Each torque label is paired with a per-frame, per-joint uncertainty annotation obtained by Monte Carlo sampling through the inverse dynamics pipeline. We benchmark six architectures (MLP, Conv1D, LSTM, GRU, Mamba2, Transformer) on ground reaction force and joint torque prediction from SKEL motion. As one example use of the released uncertainty, finetuning a Transformer with per-sample uncertainty weighting reduces the MAE of joint torque estimation by 5%. Code and data are at https://github.com/DominoAI-Lab/ForceBody-NeurIPS-ED-2026.

</details>


### [117] [NexSight: Verifiable Tasks for Fine-Grained Agentic Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22790)
*Tianci Xie,Li Chen,Pinxue Guo,Rui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NexSight 通过可验证的 agentic 数据构建流程 SightForge 生成可追溯证据的任务集 SightLine，并配套多尺度评测框架 SightLoop 与步级监督训练，使 35B 模型交互式准确率接近翻倍并超越更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频细粒度问题（计数、时序、状态变化时刻）需在数小时视频中定位零散短片段；现有评测难以衡量 agentic 搜索能力，且仅答案监督无法显式监督"看哪里、何时看"的证据搜索过程。

Method: SightForge 流程：仅当盲于草稿答案的验证器能从原始视频稠密重采集的证据中重建答案、且反例检验无法反驳时才接受任务；产出 SightLine（108 个长视频、310 个证据可追溯任务），用 SightLoop 多尺度框架评测（时间探索、局部检查、证据整合、回看并记录观测）；在不相交视频上，求解器轨迹转为步级观测决策监督。

Result: 最强模型在 SightLine 上仅 54.52%，给出标注证据时达 79.35%；Sight-35B 用不足 6K 样本将交互准确率从 21.29% 提升到 42.26%，超越同家族 397B 模型，后训练后出现基座从未使用的显式空间检查行为，并在两个外部长视频基准上提升 8 点以上。

Conclusion: 可验证的证据中心任务构建 + 共享评测框架 + 求解轨迹监督，能有效评测并显著提升长视频 agentic 理解，验证了"监督搜索过程"相较仅答案监督的价值。

Abstract: Fine-grained questions about long videos ask how many times an action occurs, in what order events unfold, or exactly when a state changes. Answering them can require finding brief moments scattered across hours of footage. Agentic models that decide when and where to look promise to find these moments, yet this ability is hard both to measure and to learn. Sparse supporting evidence may establish that an action occurred without establishing its total count; verifying timeline-wide claims requires checking for missed occurrences and double counting. Answer-only supervision, in turn, does not explicitly supervise the search for evidence. We present NexSight, which builds verifiable tasks to both evaluate and train agentic long-video understanding. Its agentic pipeline, SightForge, accepts a task only if a verifier blind to the draft answer can reconstruct it from evidence densely reacquired from the raw video and counterexample checks fail to refute it. This yields SightLine, 310 evidence-traceable tasks from 108 long videos, evaluated through SightLoop, a shared multiscale harness supporting temporal exploration, local inspection, evidence integration, and revisiting while recording returned observations. On disjoint videos, solver trajectories on verified tasks become step-level supervision for observation decisions. The strongest model reaches only 54.52% on SightLine, yet 79.35% when given the annotated evidence. With under 6K examples, Sight-35B nearly doubles its base model's interactive accuracy (21.29% to 42.26%), outperforms the 397B model of its family, begins using explicit spatial inspection after post-training, which its base model never uses, and gains over 8 points on two external long-video benchmarks.

</details>


### [118] [Visual Harness Scales General VLMs to Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22815)
*Shaowen Wan,Hanqi Jiang,Zihao Wu,Junhao Chen,Weihang You,Ruiyu Yan,Bowen Chen,Tianming Liu,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"医学视觉挽具"(\ours)，为通用VLM配备交互式视觉工具与无损诊断证据记忆，通过迭代式智能体循环（初始作答→遗漏与可靠性评估→再检查测量→修订答案）在11个任务、4种模态上全面提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型在医学影像任务上表现欠佳，直接微调或专用模型成本高且泛化受限；需要一种无需重训练、能释放通用VLM潜力的通用框架。

Method: 构建视觉挽具：为VLM提供交互式视觉工具（探索、测量）与系统性诊断证据管理（无损记忆，支持检索复用）；采用智能体迭代循环——VLM先给出初始答案，随后获得潜在遗漏项与已识别发现可靠性的评估，据此进一步检查图像与测量并修订答案，直至证据充分。

Result: 在11项任务、4种成像模态上全部提升：Qwen3.8-27B 脾脏测量63.3%→98.3%，肾脏搜索57.5%→71.2%，肺结节检测0.0%→28.2%；GPT-6.1 Sol 肺结节检测10.8%→69.2%。

Conclusion: 视觉挽具可作为将通用VLM扩展到医学影像任务的新范式，且与更强的底层模型能力呈互补增益，无需领域重训练。

Abstract: We show that an appropriate visual harness can substantially improve the performance of general vision-language models (VLMs) across diverse medical imaging tasks. We introduce \ours, a medical visual harness that equips general VLMs with interactive visual tools and systematic diagnostic evidence management. With \ours, general VLMs interactively explore medical images and progressively acquire diagnostic evidence, which is preserved in a lossless memory for subsequent retrieval and use. After submitting an initial answer, the VLM receives an assessment of potential omissions and the reliability of identified findings. This assessment guides further image inspection and measurement, followed by answer revision in an iterative agentic loop until sufficient evidence is obtained. Across 11 tasks and four imaging modalities, \ours improves performance on all tasks with Qwen3.8-27B, with notable gains in spleen measurement (63.3\% to 98.3\%), renal search (57.5\% to 71.2\%), and pulmonary nodule detection (0.0\% to 28.2\%). High-capability API-based VLMs achieve even higher performance with \ours. For example, GPT-6.1 Sol improves pulmonary nodule detection from 10.8\% to 69.2\% with \ours, demonstrating the complementary benefits of stronger model capabilities and an effective medical visual harness. Our results highlight the potential of visual harnesses as a new paradigm for scaling general VLMs to medical imaging tasks.

</details>


### [119] [Pixel is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22820)
*Jie Chu,Haoting Shi,Fucheng Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Junwei Liao,Ying Wen,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证了纯像素空间生成模型可直接在画布上对文字进行符号推理，表现出与LLM类似的涌现行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像/视频/统一多模态生成器的视觉生成模块无法在像素上对文本做符号推理，只有依赖内部文本模块或提示改写器给出答案时才成功，生成器退化为渲染器，且后训练难以解决。作者希望厘清该缺陷源于像素本身还是当前训练范式。

Method: 从零训练一组无任何文本通路的纯像素空间生成模型，输入输出仅为图像，模型直接在画布上读题、书写中间步骤并输出答案；考察规模律、逐帧书写与单帧作答对比，以及强化学习后训练。

Result: 纯像素模型展现出大语言模型的标志性行为：性能随规模按幂律可预测提升；逐帧书写中间步骤显著优于单帧作答（类似思维链）；RL后训练进一步增强推理；单一画布使同一模型可跨模态推理。

Conclusion: 这是首个在无文本token的纯像素空间生成模型中对上述现象的系统性研究，表明该失败源自训练范式而非像素表征本身。

Abstract: Text, images, audio, touch, and even robot actions can, in principle, all be rendered onto a single canvas, suggesting an ambitious goal of unifying all modalities within vision. Language provides the natural first test. To subsume it, a visual model must reason directly over text in pixels. Across a wide range of image, video, and unified multimodal generators, however, we find that their visual generation modules cannot perform symbolic reasoning over text on the canvas. They succeed only when an underlying text module or prompt rewriter already supplies the answer, effectively reducing the generator to a mere renderer, a limitation that post-training does little to resolve. To test whether this failure stems from pixels themselves or from current training paradigms, we train a family of purely pixel-space generative models from scratch without any text pathway. With images as their sole input and output, these models read questions, write intermediate steps, and produce answers directly on the canvas. Remarkably, they exhibit hallmark behaviors of large language models. Performance improves predictably with scale following a power law. Writing intermediate steps frame by frame substantially outperforms single-frame answering, echoing chain-of-thought, while reinforcement-learning post-training further strengthens reasoning. Moreover, sharing one canvas allows the same model to reason across modalities, a capability that text-only language models lack. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of these phenomena in purely pixel-space generative models free of text tokens.

</details>


### [120] [A tiny object detection dataset for high-altitude open-water search and rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22825)
*Hui Xu,Chenfei Hou,Henglai Wei,Hao Wang,Yidong Jin,Xiulin Niu,Haoyang Zhang,Hui Zhang,Tianshi Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文构建了面向高空开放水域搜救（OW-SAR）的微小目标检测数据集TinyWOD，以救生圈为目标，含11,359张图像与19,505个标注实例，并提出APtiny指标。数据集中目标平均等效边长12.7像素，76.96%的实例ESL小于15像素，真实反映1.5–2.0 km高空成像条件，并以此为基准评估了主流与前沿检测网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空平台常在1.5 km以上作业以提升开放水域搜救效率，但长成像距离、大视场与有限传感器分辨率使目标仅占几个像素，易受环境干扰，检测难度远超常规小目标。已有数据集多聚焦低空场景，无法支撑1.5–2.0 km高度的自动检测研究，因而缺乏对应基准。

Method: 构建TinyWOD数据集：以救生圈为检测目标，包含11,359张人工标注图像、19,505个标注实例，覆盖多样环境、光照条件、水体颜色与水面波纹；通过等效边长（ESL）刻画目标尺度分布；定义微小目标检测指标APtiny，并用该指标与常规指标对主流及SOTA网络进行基准评测。

Result: 与现有数据集相比，TinyWOD目标尺寸显著更小、微小实例占比更高：平均ESL为12.7像素，76.96%的实例ESL低于15像素。所有被评估网络均能有效检测微小水上目标，验证了数据集的可用性与可复用性。

Conclusion: TinyWOD为高空OW-SAR微小目标检测提供了贴近实际成像条件的基准，作者期望其推动该方向研究；现有网络在该场景下已具备基本可行性，但微小目标检测仍具挑战。

Abstract: Large aerial platforms often operate above 1.5 km to improve the efficiency of open water search and rescue (OW-SAR). However, long imaging distances, wide fields of view, and limited sensor resolution reduce objects to a few pixels, increasing their susceptibility to environmental interference and making detection harder than for conventional small objects. To our knowledge, existing datasets focus on low-altitude scenarios and do not support automatic detection at 1.5-2.0 km. We therefore present TinyWOD, a tiny object detection dataset for high-altitude OW-SAR using lifebuoys as detection targets. It comprises 11,359 manually annotated images and 19,505 annotated instances spanning diverse environments, lighting conditions, water colors, and surface ripples. Compared with existing datasets, TinyWOD contains much smaller objects and a substantially higher proportion of tiny instances; its average equivalent side length (ESL) is 12.7 pixels, with 76.96% of in stances having ESLs below 15 pixels, reflecting actual high-altitude water-rescue imaging conditions. We define APtiny, a tiny object detection metric, and benchmark mainstream and state-of-the-art networks using this and conventional metrics. All evaluated networks effectively detect tiny waterborne objects, demonstrating TinyWOD's reusability and utility. We expect this dataset to support research on tiny object detection in high-altitude OW-SAR.

</details>


### [121] [Kolibri: Color Finds the Place, Refinement Sets the Pose for RGB-D Localization in Colored Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22829)
*Leonel Aguilar,Bartu Tuncay,Christoph Hoelscher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kolibri：仅55,712参数的图网络在10cm体素(cell)上编码颜色、局部形状与邻居，配合几何精配准实现高效RGB-D到彩色点云的重定位；在ScanNet未见过房间上与GeDi相当(81.4% vs 80.3%@5cm,5°)，单帧82ms，并可支持无房间标签的全局检索与真实医院场景应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已扫描建筑中工作的智能体/机器人需在训练时模型未见过的房间里定位相机。点云配准方法虽准确但代价高；作者观察到几何精配准可将邻近初始位姿收敛到同一最终位姿，因此学习模块只需解决"找位置"(place recognition)这一粗定位问题，从而大幅降低计算开销。

Method: 提出Kolibri：将点云划分为10cm立方体"cell"，用颜色、局部形状和邻居关系描述每个cell；采用仅55,712参数的图神经网络进行cell级描述与匹配，输出粗初始位姿；随后复用统一的几何精配准(refinement)得到最终位姿。学习部分只负责定位候选位置，几何部分负责精确对齐。

Result: 在96个未见过的ScanNet房间上与GeDi相当：81.4% vs 80.3%(5cm与5°内)，另10个留出测试房间结果一致；RTX 2080 Ti上82ms/帧。100个房间的单一点云中，无房间标签搜索仅比已知房间低1.3分，而"先检索房间"策略损失56.0分。真实医院急诊科扫描场景中，对渲染帧定位成功率86.5%，每帧384ms；GeDi在类似召回下需91分钟/帧。局限：要求帧与点云颜色一致，灰度帧或不同相机色彩响应下点云配准方法仍占优。

Conclusion: 将学习组件限定于粗定位、把精确对齐交给几何精配准，是一种以极小模型(55k参数)换取高精度与低延迟的有效设计；其速度优势使无需房间标签的全局搜索与大规模单点云定位成为可能。方法的有效性依赖帧与点云间颜色一致性，跨相机色彩差异或灰度输入时表现下降。

Abstract: An agent or robot in a scanned building must locate its camera in rooms that no model saw during training. Point cloud registration methods can align each RGB-D frame with the building's colored point cloud, accurately but at a high cost. We observe that geometric refinement brings nearby initial poses to the same final pose, so the learned component only needs to find the place. Kolibri does this with a graph network of 55,712 parameters that describes 10 cm cubes of points, called cells, by their color, local shape and neighbors. With the same refinement, it matches GeDi on 96 unseen ScanNet rooms (81.4% against 80.3% within 5 cm and 5°) and on a held-out test of 10 further rooms, in 82 ms per frame on an RTX 2080 Ti. The saved time allows for search without a room label. In one point cloud of 100 rooms, Kolibri loses 1.3 points against knowing the room, while retrieving the room first loses 56.0. In a hospital emergency department scene from a real scan, it localizes 86.5% of the frames rendered from the scan in 384 ms each, where GeDi needs 91 minutes per frame on cubes of the scan for a similar recall. The method needs colors that agree between the frame and the point cloud. On gray frames and across cameras with different color responses, point cloud registration methods keep the lead.

</details>


### [122] [Keeping Parts Honest: Isolated Part Graphs for Faithful Vision Transformer Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22853)
*Bernard Tomczyk,Gail Collyer-Hoar,Bryan Williams*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"孤立部件图"的可解释ViT分类器，通过让每个部件仅由自身patch编码来杜绝注意力造成的证据泄漏，并以原型图匹配使解释在构造上忠实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于部件/原型的ViT解释把决策归因于单个图像区域，但自注意力在patch间混合信息，导致部件级特征吸收全图上下文，无法忠实反映决策依据。实验表明这类解释型分类器高度依赖这种"泄漏"：孤立编码各解释区域时准确率崩溃，仅在孤立条件下重训也只能恢复部分精度。

Method: 将图像表示为孤立部件的图。每个部件仅由其自身patch经ViT编码，且最后若干block在孤立条件下微调，阻断跨区域证据泄漏。部件无需标注即可发现，并通过子部件层级化细化，使其跨图像更一致稳定。边由部件对计算，恢复原先被注意力在全物体上共享的证据而不影响部件本身。将图像图与每类学习到的原型图匹配，预测为节点与边相似度的直接组合，每个相似度仅由其所归属的patch计算。

Result: 在11个细粒度与通用物体基准上，本方法准确率超过依赖泄漏的现有可解释方法，同时提供的解释反映模型真实使用的证据。

Conclusion: 通过孤立部件编码与原型图匹配，可在不牺牲（甚至提升）准确率的前提下实现构造性忠实的ViT解释，说明消除证据泄漏是提升可解释分类器性能与可信度的有效途径。

Abstract: Part and prototype-based explanations for Vision Transformers (ViTs) present a decision as evidence from individual image regions. However, self-attention mixes information across patches, so part-level features absorb context from the whole image and consequently fail to faithfully reflect the decision. We show that existing interpretable ViT classifiers depend heavily on this leakage: when each explained region is encoded in isolation, their accuracy collapses, and retraining them under isolation recovers only part of the lost accuracy. We propose a classifier that represents each image as a graph of isolated parts. Each part is encoded only from its own patches by a ViT whose last blocks are fine-tuned under isolation, preventing evidence leakage from elsewhere in the image. Parts are discovered without annotation and refined hierarchically through their sub-parts, making them more consistent and stable across images than those of competing methods. Each edge is computed from part pairs, and recovers evidence that attention previously shared across the object without affecting the parts. Matching the image graph to a learned prototype graph per class makes the prediction a direct combination of node and edge similarities, each computed only from the patches it is credited to, so every explanation is faithful by construction. Across 11 fine-grained and general-object benchmarks, our method exceeds the accuracy of existing explainable methods that rely on leakage, while providing explanations that reflect what the model actually uses.

</details>


### [123] [Propose & Verify: Fast Reflection Symmetry Detection via Harmonic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22856)
*Yishai Lavi,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAST 是一个免训练的三维形状多反射对称检测框架，将几何成对投票表示为平面朝向信号，并在球谐域用 Funk-Hecke 定理把高斯平滑转为系数乘法，从而高效生成连续对称场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维形状多重反射对称检测需在巨大候选平面空间中聚合几何证据，传统投票方法计算代价高、效率低。

Method: 利用全局反射平面必过形状质心，将搜索降为平面朝向；用几何一致性过滤与独立见证抑制不可靠成对假设；将投票表示为平面朝向上的信号，借 Funk-Hecke 定理（旋转对称核下球面版的傅里叶卷积定理）把高斯平滑转为谐波系数相乘，实现高效连续的平滑对称场；再提取、细化并几何验证对称候选。

Result: 在两个基准（含一个高对称形状新基准）上取得最高干净多平面检测 F1（0.861 与 0.947）；GPU 推理每形状低于 4 ms，比次快的多平面 GPU 基线快 100 倍以上；对几何噪声鲁棒，干净单平面形状成功率达 94.9%。

Conclusion: FAST 以谱域计算替代传统大规模投票，在保持检测精度的同时实现数量级加速，兼具鲁棒性与单/多平面检测能力。

Abstract: Detecting multiple reflection symmetries in 3D shapes requires aggregating geometric evidence over a large space of candidate planes, making traditional voting approaches computationally expensive. We introduce the Fourier Accelerated Symmetry Transform (FAST), a training-free framework that combines geometric pair voting with efficient spectral computation. Exploiting the fact that global reflection planes pass through the shape centroid, we reduce the search to plane orientations, while geometric consistency filtering and independent witnesses suppress unreliable pair hypotheses. Our key insight is to represent these votes as a signal over plane orientations and efficiently compute a continuous, smoothed symmetry field in the spherical-harmonic domain. Specifically, we exploit the Funk-Hecke theorem, the spherical counterpart of the Fourier convolution theorem for rotationally symmetric kernels, to transform Gaussian smoothing into multiplication of harmonic coefficients. This enables efficient field evaluation, from which symmetry candidates are extracted, refined, and geometrically verified. Across two benchmarks, including a new benchmark of highly symmetric shapes, FAST achieves the highest clean multi-plane detection $F_1$ scores among evaluated methods (0.861 and 0.947), with GPU inference below 4 ms per shape: over $100\times$ faster than the next-fastest evaluated multi-plane GPU baseline. FAST also demonstrates strong robustness to geometric noise and competitive single-plane detection, achieving 94.9% success on clean single-plane shapes.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Co-Generation: Rethinking Jointness in Joint Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22857)
*Jihoo Jung,Ji-Hoon Kim,Suyeon Lee,Gia Thinh Pham,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"内在联合性"(intrinsic jointness)指标，用于区分T2AV生成中音视频一致性源于真实跨模态协调还是各自独立遵循文本；发现现有模型内在联合性不足，提出表示对齐后训练方法JointREPA，并构建语义欠指定基准UNSAIDBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联合文本到音频-视频(T2AV)生成中，详细文本提示会掩盖一致性来源：无法判断音视频一致是来自真正的跨模态协调，还是各自独立跟随文本。需通过语义欠指定提示（如"乐器演奏"而非"电吉他演奏"）来隔离并检验真实协调能力。

Method: 1) 定义intrinsic jointness：音视频在提示未指定细节上产生相同具体实现的能力；2) 使用语义欠指定提示评测四个近期模型；3) 提出JointREPA——基于表示对齐(representation alignment)的简单后训练方法以促进跨模态协调；4) 构建UNSAIDBench基准，专门评估语义欠指定下的内在联合性。

Result: 评估揭示现有四个模型内在联合性有限；在两个不同模型、三个基准（含UNSAIDBench）上的实验表明，JointREPA一致提升语义一致性。

Conclusion: 语义欠指定提示可有效隔离真实跨模态协调；内在联合性是T2AV语义一致性的关键缺口，表示对齐后训练(JointREPA)可稳定改善，UNSAIDBench为该研究提供评测基础。

Abstract: Our aim is to advance the understanding of genuine audio-visual coordination in joint text-to-audio-video (T2AV) generation, with particular emphasis on semantic consistency. Evaluating audio-visual consistency in T2AV generation is nontrivial, as detailed text prompts obscure whether such consistency stems from genuine cross-modal coordination or merely from independent text following. To isolate the former, we introduce intrinsic jointness: the ability of audio and video to produce the same realization for details left underspecified by the prompt. Using semantically underspecified prompts (e.g., "musical instrument playing" rather than "electric guitar playing"), we investigate four recent models and reveal their limited intrinsic jointness. To address this limitation, we propose JointREPA, a simple representation alignment-based post-training method to facilitate cross-modal coordination. Since existing benchmarks use semantically detailed prompts, we introduce UNSAIDBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate intrinsic jointness under semantic underspecification. Experiments across two distinct models and three benchmarks, including UNSAIDBench, demonstrate that JointREPA consistently improves semantic consistency.

</details>


### [125] [LOCUS: Causal Support Windows for Attribute Control in Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22867)
*Zijun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现统一自回归图像生成器中不同属性对图像token序列位置的因果敏感性存在系统性差异，并提出按属性路由到对应因果支撑窗口的测试时潜变量优化方法LOCUS，显著提升多属性约束满足率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一自回归图像生成器难以在单一提示下同时满足多个属性约束。虽然属性信息可从图像token潜变量中解码，但测试时对这些潜变量进行编辑十分困难：向prefix添加固定属性方向只能强化已请求的属性，无法覆盖冲突属性（如提示为蓝色时把车改成红色）。作者因此探究"干预效果如何取决于其在图像token序列中的作用位置"。

Method: 提出因果支撑剖面（causal support profile），针对每个属性度量在给定窗口内patch图像token状态时渲染属性被翻转的频率；据此刻画不同属性的响应窗口（结构属性count/position响应于早期窄窗口，外观属性color/size/material响应于后期更宽窗口），并进行预算匹配的早/中窗口对比与二维支撑图分析。在此基础上提出LOCUS（Latent Optimization in Causal Support windows）：诊断失败的属性，并将测试时潜变量优化路由到其对应的支撑窗口。

Result: 在生成器、优化器、奖励、验证器与token预算均固定的条件下，支撑路由窗口在GenEval++宏平均准确率上比后期窗口高23.6个百分点。生成器冻结时，LOCUS将Janus-Pro的GenEval++准确率整体从24.6%提升至46.8%，多属性标签从19.5%提升至40.5%。在GenEval上，LOCUS单次rollout修复48.1%的失败生成（prefix优化为37.4%），且平均所需轮数减少14%。

Conclusion: 干预位置对自回归图像生成的可控性具有属性依赖的因果效应，基于因果支撑剖面的窗口路由是一种有效且参数高效的测试时控制策略，可在冻结生成器的前提下显著提升多属性组合生成性能。

Abstract: Unified autoregressive (AR) image generators struggle to satisfy multiple attribute constraints in a single prompt. Although these attributes are decodable from image-token latents, editing those latents at test time remains difficult. Adding a fixed attribute direction to the prefix can reinforce a requested attribute but rarely overrides a conflicting one, such as turning a car red when the prompt specifies blue. We investigate how the effect of an intervention depends on where it acts in the image-token sequence. We introduce the causal support profile, which measures, for each attribute, how often patching image-token states within a given window flips the rendered attribute. Structural attributes (count and position) are most responsive in early, narrow windows, while appearance attributes (color, size, and material) respond over later, broader windows. A budget-matched comparison of early and middle windows shows that the effect of window location depends on the target attribute, and two-dimensional support maps show that the responsive regions track spatial content. Based on these profiles, we propose LOCUS (Latent Optimization in Causal Support windows), which diagnoses which attribute failed and routes test-time latent optimization to its corresponding support window. With the generator, optimizer, reward, verifier, and token budget held fixed, support-routed windows outperform late windows by 23.6 percentage points in GenEval++ macro accuracy. With the generator frozen, LOCUS raises Janus-Pro's GenEval++ accuracy from 24.6% to 46.8% overall and from 19.5% to 40.5% on multi-attribute tags. On GenEval, LOCUS repairs 48.1% of failed generations in one rollout, compared with 37.4% for prefix optimization, and needs 14% fewer rounds on average to succeed.

</details>


### [126] [Harnessing Image Diffusion Prior for Photo-Realistic Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22874)
*Fanghua Yu,Hongyu An,Jinfan Hu,Xinqi Lin,Zhiyuan You,Jian Wang,Hongyang Li,Chao Dong,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种生成式视频修复方法，将从图像生成模型迁移的强先验与时间一致性结合：通过噪声能量重平衡、运动对齐噪声扭曲、自监督去噪编码器和解耦时间注意力，实现时间一致且细节丰富的修复结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成模型能生成照片级真实感且细节丰富的图像，但其随机细节合成会带来时间不一致，难以直接迁移到视频修复。现有视频修复方法虽改善时间连贯性，却常牺牲视觉保真度，导致图像级质量与视频级稳定性之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 四个组件：(1) 噪声能量重平衡，抑制破坏结构的低频随机性；(2) 运动对齐噪声扭曲，沿物体运动传播细节；(3) 自监督去噪编码器，缓解编码器引入的时间不确定性；(4) 解耦时间注意力，建模跨帧依赖关系。整体在保持保真度的同时增强细节真实感并维持时间一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在视觉质量、细节丰富度和时间稳定性上显著优于现有视频修复方法。

Conclusion: 借助对噪声、运动与时间注意力的精细设计，可有效将图像生成先验用于视频修复，弥合图像级保真与视频级稳定性之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent image generation models produce photo-realistic results with rich details, but transferring their powerful priors to video restoration remains difficult due to temporal inconsistency from stochastic detail synthesis. Existing video restoration methods improve temporal coherence but often sacrifice visual fidelity, leaving a gap between image-level quality and video-level stability. We propose a generative video restoration method that bridges this gap by enabling temporally consistent detail synthesis from image generation priors. Our method combines noise energy rebalancing to suppress structure-disturbing low-frequency stochasticity, motion-aligned noise warping to propagate details along object motion, a self-supervised denoising encoder to mitigate encoder-induced temporal uncertainty, and decoupled temporal attention to model cross-frame dependencies. Together, these components preserve fidelity, enhance fine-grained realism, and maintain temporal consistency. Extensive experiments show that our method substantially outperforms existing video restoration approaches in visual quality, detail richness, and temporal stability.

</details>


### [127] [SlideJev: Order-Stable and Tissue-Grounded Jev Decisions for Whole-Slide Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22879)
*Lei Wu,Jiashuai Liu,Di Zhang,Chunze Yang,Jiusong Ge,Junbo Lu,Yongqi Liang,Wenjie Zhao,Ni Zhang,Chen Li,Zeyu Gao,Qidong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SlideJev框架，通过独立验证候选答案并学习将判断建立在组织证据上，缓解全切片病理VQA中视觉语言模型对语言先验的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全切片病理VQA的视觉语言模型过度依赖语言先验：答案对候选选项顺序敏感，且可通过绕过组织证据的捷径得到正确答案。

Method: 采用Jev机制，用原始语言模型头输出的支持与拒绝对数似然之差为每个候选打分；各候选以同格式、无选项位置标签的独立提示并行计算后归一化选择答案。为使分数扎根于组织证据，引入候选条件化的阅读与比较，跨解码层门控更新视觉token；主干冻结，仅用问答与配对图像监督训练视觉模块。

Result: 在三个全切片病理VQA基准上取得最高平均总体准确率，并在四种选项顺序下实现100%答案一致性；图像控制实验显示三数据集上正确图像相对不匹配/无图像输入的平均准确率增益最大。

Conclusion: SlideJev有效降低对语言先验与选项顺序的依赖，并将答案判断与组织视觉证据对齐，提升全切片病理VQA的稳健性。

Abstract: Whole-slide visual question answering (VQA) offers a natural-language interface to pathology, allowing diagnostic and phenotypic questions to be answered directly from tissue slides. However, current vision-language models (VLMs) rely heavily on linguistic priors: their answers are sensitive to the order of the candidate answers, and can be reached through shortcuts that bypass the tissue evidence. To address these challenges, we introduce SlideJev, a framework that separately verifies candidate answers against the slide and learns to ground these judgments in tissue evidence. To connect parallel candidate evaluation with visual evidence learning, we adopt Jev and score each candidate by the difference between support and rejection log-likelihoods from the original language-model head. We compute these scores in parallel using separate prompts with the same format and no option-position labels, then normalize them for answer selection. To ground these scores in tissue evidence, candidate-conditioned reading and comparison guide gated updates to visual tokens across decoder layers. With the backbone frozen, we train these visual modules through the same scores using question-answer and paired-image supervision. Across three whole-slide pathology VQA benchmarks, SlideJev achieves the highest mean overall accuracy and 100% answer consistency across four option orders. Image-control experiments further show that SlideJev achieves the largest average accuracy gains from correct images over mismatched and image-free inputs across the three datasets. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/leowu-ai/SlideJev.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Agent-Centric Vision-Language-Action World Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22880)
*Guoqing Wang,Pin Tang,Xiangxuan Ren,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AgentWorld——以智能体为中心的VLA世界模型，用离散智能体token替代像素级未来帧进行推理与轨迹规划，在开环/闭环实验中提升规划性能并给出可解释的世界状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有world VLA模型在像素级生成未来帧，包含大量密集视觉token，其中大部分是与驾驶无关的外观细节，仅少量token编码对规划关键的智能体几何、运动动力学与交互信息，构成性能瓶颈。由此提出核心问题：world VLA应以何种世界状态进行推理。

Method: 主张规划导向的世界建模应聚焦紧凑、结构化的智能体中心潜在状态。AgentWorld将每个离散agent token动态绑定到周围某一智能体，携带语义身份、3D几何、运动与交互上下文等状态；通过三项关键过程学习：显式几何grounding、面向推理的监督微调、面向智能体的强化学习。token同时用于推理与轨迹规划。

Result: 在多样驾驶场景的开环与闭环实验中，AgentWorld提升了规划能力，能够充分利用视觉证据与智能体动力学，并产生可解释的agent-centric世界状态。

Conclusion: 面向规划的世界建模无需像素级未来预测，以结构化、智能体中心的紧凑token作为世界状态即可有效支撑VLA推理与规划，兼顾性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Recent progress in integrating vision-language-action (VLA) models with world modeling has significantly advanced end-to-end autonomous driving by allowing systems to reason about imagined futures instead of merely reacting to current observations. However, most world VLA models generate future frames at the pixel level with plenty of dense visual tokens. Although such generated futures can be visually plausible, they preserve many driving-irrelevant appearance details, i.e., only a small portion of the tokens directly encode critical planning-relevant factors, such as agent geometry, motion dynamics, and interactions. This introduces a significant performance bottleneck. An important question thus arises: what world state should a world VLA model reason over to support planning beyond pixel-level prediction? We argue that planning-oriented world modeling should focus on a compact and structured agent-centric latent state that captures the interactions between objects in the driving scene over time. As such, we introduce AgentWorld, an agent-centric VLA world model that uses discrete agent tokens jointly for reasoning and trajectory planning. Each token is dynamically grounded to a surrounding agent associated with its object's state, such as semantic identity, 3D geometry, motion, and interaction context. AgentWorld learns these tokens through three key processes: explicit geometric grounding, reasoning-oriented supervised fine-tuning, and agent-oriented reinforcement learning. Extensive open-loop and closed-loop experiments under diverse driving scenarios show that AgentWorld enhances planning capabilities, fully exploits visual evidence and agent dynamics, and yields interpretable agent-centric world states.

</details>


### [129] [RoadAnnoter: An AI-Assisted Desktop Tool for Semantic Segmentation Annotation of Road-Scene Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22884)
*Nazmul Haque,Arnob Protim Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出开源桌面工具 RoadAnnoter v1.0，集成六种人机协同标注模式，用于道路场景图像的多边形级语义分割数据集构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路场景语义分割对道路安全研究至关重要，但多边形级标注劳动密集，制约了标注数据集的建设与扩展。

Method: 提供六种标注模式：手工多边形勾画、SAM Prompt、Smart Select、Edge Fill（Canny 边缘检测 + CIELAB 颜色相似性漫水填充）、基于 Cityscapes 预训练 SegFormer 的语义分割建议，以及基于 MobileNetV3-Small 的轻量网络 TinySegNet（利用累积标注迭代训练，适配项目特定特征）。SAM 类模式采用 SAM 与 MobileSAM 的可提示分割；六种模式共用统一的 mask-to-polygon 流水线（形态学处理、高斯平滑、轮廓提取、Douglas-Peucker 简化），输出 YOLO 分割格式标注。

Result: 应用于孟加拉国事故地点街景图像的标注，支撑基于视觉语言模型的自由流速度估计研究；定性评估显示各模式对不同特征几何形态具有互补适用性；与 Roboflow 的视觉对比显示主要道路区域边界特征相近，但未进行定量精度评估。

Conclusion: RoadAnnoter 为交通工程与道路安全研究提供了本地运行的道路场景分割数据集构建框架，但尚缺定量精度验证。

Abstract: Accurate semantic segmentation of road-scene images is essential for road safety research; however, polygon-level annotation remains labor-intensive and limits labelled dataset development. This study presents RoadAnnoter v1.0, an open-source desktop application integrating six human-in-the-loop annotation modes: manual polygon delineation, SAM Prompt, Smart Select, Edge Fill, pretrained semantic segmentation using Cityscapes-trained SegFormer models, and task-specific segmentation using TinySegNet, a lightweight MobileNetV3-Small-based network trained with accumulated annotator-generated labels. SAM-based modes use promptable segmentation with SAM and MobileSAM, while Edge Fill combines Canny edge detection with colour-similarity-based flood filling in CIELAB colour space. SegFormer generates suggestions for common road-scene categories, whereas TinySegNet supports adaptation to project-specific features through iterative training. All six modes use a shared mask-to-polygon pipeline comprising morphological processing, Gaussian smoothing, contour extraction, and Douglas-Peucker simplification; annotations are exported in YOLO segmentation format. RoadAnnoter was used to annotate Google Street View images from recorded road accident locations in Bangladesh for a vision-language model-based investigation of free-flow speed estimation. Qualitative assessment across representative scenes demonstrated the complementary applicability of the annotation modes to diverse feature geometries. Visual comparison with Roboflow indicated similar qualitative boundary characteristics for principal road-scene regions, although no quantitative accuracy assessment was conducted. RoadAnnoter provides a locally operated framework for developing road-scene segmentation datasets for transportation engineering and road safety research.

</details>


### [130] [Wear and Go: Calibration Free Sparse Inertial Motion Capture via Online Sensor Setup Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22894)
*Yuanshuo Tan,Licheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOSAIC框架无需校准姿势，仅利用普通运动即可恢复稀疏惯性动捕中传感器的解剖身份与安装朝向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏惯性动捕通常预设每个传感器的解剖部位与安装朝向已知，实际部署中这一假设难以满足，且缺少校准姿势时难以直接标定。

Method: 核心洞察是身体姿态连续变化而传感器配置保持不变，单一时刻模糊的运动可在时间上提供互补证据。采用setup网络处理无序传感器流，结合时间注意力与跨传感器注意力，将分配与旋转证据累积为会话级估计；恢复的配置用于对冻结的预训练姿态估计器做测量规范化。

Result: 在真实惯性记录、随机化传感器配置下，对规定佩戴部位的传感器，有界偏移时达到接近校准后的姿态精度；任意朝向时累积机制带来显著收益；跨姿态网络的迁移实验表明装置恢复可作为可复用接口。

Conclusion: 将传感器配置恢复与运动重建解耦，使MOSAIC成为可互换传感器与预训练姿态模型之间的通用接口，摆脱校准姿势依赖。

Abstract: Sparse inertial motion capture typically assumes that each sensor's anatomical identity and mounting orientation are known. We present MOSAIC (Motion-based Sensor Assignment and Inertial Calibration), a framework that recovers this setup from ordinary motion without a calibration pose. Our central insight is that body pose changes continuously while the sensor setup persists, allowing movements that are individually ambiguous to provide complementary evidence over time. A setup network processes unordered sensor streams with temporal and cross-sensor attention, then accumulates assignment and rotation evidence into a session-level estimate. The recovered setup canonicalizes measurements for frozen pretrained pose estimators, separating sensor configuration from motion reconstruction. For sensors worn at prescribed body sites, evaluation on real inertial recordings with randomized setups shows near-calibrated pose accuracy for bounded offsets and substantial benefits from accumulation under arbitrary orientations. Transfer across pose networks supports setup recovery as a reusable interface between interchangeable sensors and pretrained pose models.

</details>


### [131] [Impact of Pretraining Length, Model Size, and Annotation Budget on Downstream Clinical Tasks of Foundation Models in Radiotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22896)
*Alejandro Cortina Uribe,Sidsel Højklint Poulsen,Ivan Richter Vogelius,Jens Petersen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在约15万例3D放疗CBCT上以掩码图像建模预训练ViT编码器，系统研究预训练时长、模型规模与微调策略同下游临床性能的关系，并发现预训练验证损失是最稳定的性能预测指标，LoRA可媲美全量微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放疗期间采集的CBCT可反映肿瘤缩小、放疗诱导的组织变化，具备构建监测个体治疗反应的基础模型的潜力；但据作者所知尚无大规模放疗CBCT预训练模型，且下游任务事先未知，导致建模选择（预训练时长、模型规模、微调策略）难以评估，预训练过程中的指标与下游性能的关系亦不清楚。

Method: 在约150,000例原始3D放疗CBCT扫描上，用掩码图像建模（masked image modeling）预训练ViT编码器；以验证损失、有效秩（effective rank）和线性探测（linear probing）跟踪表征质量。下游任务为头颈癌中的分类任务：喂养管存在性（n=51,135例CBCT，1,562名患者）与临床特征（T分期、N分期、性别、年龄、肿瘤部位；n=1,278名患者）。比较LoRA与全量微调相对无预训练的差异，并变化标注数据规模。

Result: 预训练验证损失是最一致的下游性能预测指标；有效秩与线性探测也相关，但在不同模型规模与微调方法间不一致。LoRA仅训练0.5–2.5%的参数即可达到与全量微调相当的性能。全量微调模型在所有规模上均稳定优于无预训练模型：喂养管分类AUROC 0.91–0.97 vs. 0.58–0.85，性别分类AUROC 0.99–1.00 vs. 0.71–0.97。模型规模影响较小且不一致，说明在更低算力下较小模型已足够。

Conclusion: 放疗CBCT大规模自监督预训练显著提升下游临床分类性能；预训练验证损失可作为可靠的过程监控指标；LoRA等参数高效微调与较小模型即可在低计算成本下取得优异效果。代码与预训练模型已开源（非商业许可）。

Abstract: Cone-beam CT (CBCT) scans acquired during radiotherapy capture changes such as tumor shrinkage and radiation-induced tissue changes, offering great potential for foundation models that help monitor individual treatment responses. However, to our knowledge, no large-scale pretrained models for radiotherapy CBCT exist, and evaluating modeling choices is difficult since downstream tasks are not known in advance. We examine how pretraining length, model size, and finetuning strategy relate to downstream clinical performance, and how metrics tracked during pretraining correlate with it. We pretrain vision transformer encoders with masked image modeling on about 150,000 raw 3D radiotherapy CBCT scans, tracking representation quality via validation loss, effective rank, and linear probing. Downstream tasks are classification of feeding tube presence ($n=51,135$ CBCTs, $1,562$ patients) and clinical characteristics (T-, N-stage, sex, age, tumor site; $n=1,278$ patients) in head and neck cancer. We compare low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and full finetuning against no pretraining, varying annotated dataset size. Pretext validation loss was the most consistent predictor of downstream performance; effective rank and linear probing were also associated, but not consistently across model sizes and finetuning methods. LoRA performed comparably to full finetuning while training only 0.5-2.5% of the parameters. Fully finetuned models consistently outperformed models without pretraining across all sizes, with AUROC 0.91-0.97 vs. 0.58-0.85 for feeding tube classification and 0.99-1.00 vs. 0.71-0.97 for sex classification. Model size had only a small, inconsistent effect, suggesting smaller models suffice at lower computational cost. Code and pretrained models (non-commercial license): https://github.com/alexCortinaU/RT-CBCT_SSL

</details>


### [132] [You Only Encode Once: Sub-Pixel-Shifted Frames for Arbitrary-Scale Remote Sensing Super-Resolution with 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22898)
*Canjie Liu,Liuqian Wang,Qi Ming,Wei Lu,Xiaohan Hao,Yuefei Wu,Wentao Chao,Lei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPS-GS：一种"一次编码"的任意尺度遥感图像超分框架，用单张高分图合成多相位低分观测进行训练，通过相位相干混叠解调与高斯投影对齐实现多帧信息融合与任意尺度渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧低分遥感图像高频信息有限，任意尺度超分（ASSR）困难；多帧SR虽可利用亚像素互补信息，但现有方法依赖真实多帧训练序列且需重复特征编码，带来数据获取与计算开销。

Method: 提出SPS-GS框架：(1) Phase-Coherent Aliasing Demodulation (PCAD)，借助基于傅里叶的对齐、相位感知残差融合与最小二乘混叠估计，利用与位移相关的混叠，在单次深度编码前完成多帧信息整合；(2) Gaussian Projection Alignment (GPA)，用多观测反投影迭代优化高斯中心与颜色以提升观测一致性；(3) 精化后的高斯支持任意尺度连续渲染。训练数据由单张高分图合成多相位低分观测，无需真实多帧序列。

Result: 在五个遥感数据集上，相较单图与多帧SR基线取得较强重建性能，且在常见尺度下保持有竞争力的推理效率。

Conclusion: SPS-GS以"一次编码"加合成多相位观测的方式规避真实多帧数据依赖与重复编码成本，验证了混叠解调与高斯投影对齐在遥感任意尺度超分中的有效性，代码将公开。

Abstract: Arbitrary-scale super-resolution (ASSR) of remote sensing images remains challenging due to the limited high-frequency information in a single low-resolution (LR) observation. While multi-frame SR can exploit complementary sub-pixel information, existing approaches often require real multi-frame training sequences and repeated feature encoding, increasing data acquisition demands and computational costs. To address these limitations, we propose SPS-GS (Sub-Pixel-Shifted Gaussian Splatting), an encode-once framework that learns from multi-phase LR observations synthesized from individual high-resolution images, without requiring real multi-frame training sequences. Specifically, Phase-Coherent Aliasing Demodulation (PCAD) exploits shift-dependent aliasing through Fourier-based alignment, phase-aware residual fusion, and least-squares aliasing estimation, enabling multi-frame information to be integrated before a single deep encoding pass. Gaussian Projection Alignment (GPA) then iteratively refines Gaussian centers and colors using multi-observation back-projection to promote observation consistency. The refined Gaussians support continuous rendering at arbitrary scales. Extensive experiments on five remote sensing datasets demonstrate strong reconstruction performance against single-image and multi-frame SR baselines, while maintaining competitive inference efficiency at common scales. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [133] [BRepA: Graph-Based B-Rep Pretraining for Unified CAD Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22903)
*Dekai Zhu,Zhaiyu Chen,Stefan Gavranovic,Slobodan Ilic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BRepA——一个基于图建模、同时重建几何与拓扑的统一自监督预训练框架，仅需一次大规模无标注CAD数据预训练即可泛化到分割与生成下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-Rep以图结构编码几何基元及其关系，其学习需专门神经网络设计；且标注B-Rep数据有限，亟需有效预训练以提升性能并加速训练。

Method: 构建图神经网络式B-Rep建模，在自监督目标下显式重建对象的几何与拓扑信息，形成统一预训练骨干；下游通过微调用于分割，或将扩散模型表示与预训练特征对齐用于生成。

Result: 分割任务上数据效率突出：仅用1%标注样本，mIoU相比从零训练提升32.52%；生成任务上，与BRepA特征对齐使扩散模型收敛加速近2倍，并在同等算力预算下提升生成质量，尽管扩散模型规模约为BRepA的20倍。

Conclusion: 几何-拓扑感知的B-Rep预训练为CAD结构的理解与生成提供强有力基础，可作为多任务下游应用的通用表示骨干，代码将开源。

Abstract: Boundary representation (B-Rep) of CAD models encodes geometric primitives and their relations as a graph structure, enabling efficient shape modeling within CAD software. Learning on B-Reps for downstream tasks, such as segmentation and novel shape generation, requires specialized neural network designs. Moreover, performance improvements and accelerated training can be achieved with effective pretraining strategies, which is particularly important for tasks with limited labeled B-Rep data. In this paper, we introduce BRepA, a unified self-supervised pretraining framework that explicitly reconstructs both the geometry and topology of B-Rep objects via graph-based modeling. By learning from large-scale unlabeled CAD data only once, BRepA produces representations that generalize across tasks and significantly enhance both discriminative and generative learning. On segmentation tasks, BRepA demonstrates remarkable data efficiency. For instance, with only 1% labeled samples, BRepA pretraining improves mIoU by 32.52% compared to models trained from scratch. On generative tasks, aligning diffusion model representations with pretrained BRepA features substantially accelerates convergence, achieving nearly 2 times faster training while improving generation quality under the same computational budget, despite the diffusion model being roughly 20 times larger than BRepA. Overall, our results show that geometry- and topology-aware B-Rep pretraining provides a powerful foundation for both understanding and generating CAD structures, serving as a versatile representation backbone for diverse downstream applications. We will release the code to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [134] [SWIR-cGAN: Synthesizing SWIR Bands Using VNIR Imagery from CBERS and AMAZONIA-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22922)
*Alisson Cleiton de Oliveira,Rennan de Freitas Bezerra Marujo,Thales Sehn Körting*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SWIR-cGAN：一种带注意力增强 U-Net 生成器和多损失目标的条件生成对抗网络，用于从 CBERS-4/CBERS-4A/AMAZONIA-1 的 WFI 可见光-近红外影像合成 Sentinel-2/MSI 的 SWIR-1（B11）与 SWIR-2（B12）波段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西卫星 CBERS-4、CBERS-4A 与 AMAZONIA-1 通过 WFI 提供 1–3 天高时间分辨率的 VNIR 影像，但缺失短波红外（SWIR）波段，限制了应用。现有深度学习光谱合成方法在跨卫星泛化与光谱距离较远的波段上仍存在困难。

Method: 采用条件 GAN 框架，生成器为注意力增强的 U-Net，并设计多损失目标函数；以 WFI 的 VNIR 输入为条件合成 SWIR 波段。数据划分为 4,125 个 patch 用于训练与交叉验证，1,026 个未见 patch 用于测试，超参数在训练子集上优化；各基线模型独立调优后在测试集上统一对比，并进行消融实验。

Result: SWIR-1：SSIM 0.900、PSNR 31.44 dB、MAE 0.019（TOA 反射率）；SWIR-2：SSIM 0.917、PSNR 33.35 dB、MAE 0.015。SWIR-cGAN 优于所有基线，在 SWIR-2 上优势更明显。消融实验表明多损失项为主要性能贡献，注意力与残差模块带来额外提升。

Conclusion: 结果证明了从 WFI 影像合成 SWIR 波段的可行性，可为缺乏 SWIR 通道的高重访卫星数据提供光谱增强手段；代码已开源。

Abstract: Spectral band synthesis is an important research topic in remote sensing. The Brazilian satellites CBERS-4, CBERS-4A, and AMAZONIA-1 provide high temporal resolution (1-3 days) visible and near-infrared (VNIR) imagery through the Wide-Field Imager (WFI), but lack short-wave infrared (SWIR) bands. Recent deep learning methods have shown promising results for spectral synthesis, but challenges remain for cross-satellite generalization and spectrally distant bands. This paper presents a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (cGAN) with an attention-enhanced U-net generator and a multi-loss objective to synthesize Sentinel-2/MSI SWIR bands 11 (SWIR-1) and 12 (SWIR-2) from WFI VNIR inputs. Experiments used 4,125 patches for training and cross-validation and 1,026 unseen patches for testing, with hyperparameters optimized using a subset of the training data. On SWIR-1, the model achieved SSIM of 0.900, PSNR of 31.44 dB, and MAE of 0.019 in TOA reflectance. On SWIR-2, it achieved SSIM of 0.917, PSNR of 33.35 dB, and MAE of 0.015. Benchmarking was performed on the test dataset, with each model optimized separately prior to evaluation. SWIR-cGAN outperformed the baselines, particularly for SWIR-2. The ablation study identified the multi-loss as the main performance contributor, while the attention and residual modules provided additional improvements. Our results demonstrate the feasibility of synthesizing SWIR bands from WFI imagery. The SWIR-cGAN is available at https://github.com/marujore/swir-cgan.

</details>
