The refiner is trained once on a multi-organ corpus spanning compact organs, cavities, branching vessels, and tumour lesions. In the held-out dataset-transfer evaluation, three held-out datasets query a fixed knowledge base built from training data only. A separate leave-one-anatomy-out experiment tests parameter-free index extension: verified cases of an unseen solid organ enter the knowledge base, and the refiner stays frozen. A light shape-aware projection head on a frozen multimodal encoder maps embedding similarity to the shape agreement of the masks, so the knowledge base returns cases that the refiner can use as a shape prior. We evaluate GRACE on 18 public datasets spanning CT, MRI and ultrasound, using nine refinement configurations and seven upstream segmentors that span weak, mid, and strong operating regimes. We report each change with the DSC of its input mask. On the weak tier, the input DSC is 0.386 and GRACE adds up to +0.35 DSC. On a strong segmentor, the input DSC is 0.932 and the mean change is small. Transfer to an imaging modality that is absent from training and from the index is partial and depends on the anatomy, and remains an open research direction. Our results suggest retrieval-conditioned refinement is a practical alternative to anatomy-specific correction models. Code: https://github.com/robustml-eurecom/grace

</details>


### [170] [Reading Scene States: Uncovering Safety Failures in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23206)
*Tongxi Wu,Yuyang Cheng,Jiacheng Xian,Menglin Yang,Kai Chen,Jian Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文识别出视觉语言模型（VLM）的“场景—状态安全漏洞”：模型会通过解读视觉事件本身提供有害协助，即使请求中并未显式给出有害目标；为此提出 SceneSafetyBench 基准与 S3TA 攻击方法，最高攻击成功率（ASR）达 71.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 安全防护主要聚焦输入内容筛查（文本过滤器、视觉审核），但模型可经由对场景状态的推理—解读推断出有害目标并给出协助。作者希望揭示这一被忽视的“场景—状态”安全漏洞，并将其转化为系统性攻击，以推动超越“筛查输入内容”的防护范式。

Method: 构建 SceneSafetyBench：375 个图文问答对，覆盖 Initial、Medium、Ultimate 三种场景状态与五种视觉呈现方式。对 8 个 VLM 评估场景状态输入的攻击效果。对 3 个 VLM 做逐层安全相关信号剖面分析，与 QR 相关图像对比。提出 S3TA：将有害目标转译为场景状态，独立查询各图文问答对，再聚合回答。

Result: 在 8 个 VLM 上，场景状态输入的 ASR 达 45.6%–72.3%。层级分析显示场景状态输入的安全信号剖面最接近 QR 相关图像，但后层读出平均值更低、生成过程信号动态不同。在 MM-SafetyBench 的 7 个类别、8 个开源与商用 VLM 上，S3TA 的平均 ASR 达 71.2%，比最强基线高 6.1 个百分点。文本过滤器与视觉审核提供的防护有限。

Conclusion: VLM 的安全性不能仅依赖输入内容筛查；需针对模型经由场景落地推理所“推断出的协助意图”建立防护。S3TA 揭示了该类漏洞的可系统性利用性，文本侧与视觉侧现有防御均难以有效覆盖。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can provide harmful assistance by interpreting visual events, even without an explicit request for the underlying harmful objective. We identify this \textbf{scene-state safety vulnerability} and introduce \textbf{SceneSafetyBench}, comprising 375 image--question pairs spanning Initial, Medium, and Ultimate scene states and five visual presentations. Across eight VLMs, scene-state inputs elicit harmful assistance with attack success rates (ASRs) of 45.6--72.3\%. Comparative analysis across three VLMs shows that scene-state inputs most closely resemble QR-related images in their layer-wise safety-related signal profiles, while exhibiting lower average late-layer readouts and distinct signal dynamics during generation. To turn this vulnerability into a systematic attack, we propose \textbf{S3TA}, which translates harmful objectives into scene states, queries image--question pairs independently, and assembles the responses. On seven MM-SafetyBench categories across eight open-weight and commercial VLMs, S3TA achieves a model-average ASR of \textbf{71.2\%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 6.1 percentage points}. Text-side filters and visual moderation provide limited protection. These findings highlight the need to safeguard the assistance models infer through scene-grounded reasoning, beyond screening input content.

</details>


### [171] [Learning Grounded Reasoning from Human Gaze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23209)
*Gabriel Sarch,Declan Campbell,Nicholas Budny,Kia Ghods,Katerina Fragkiadaki,Jonathan D. Cohen,Brenden M. Lake,Taylor W. Webb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何有效利用人类注视（gaze）数据提升视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉推理能力，发现将gaze作为"先验/引导"优于用完整注视序列做监督，并提出GGR模型，在准确性、人类行为对齐与视觉grounding质量上均优于或匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉推理依赖对任务相关视觉证据的系统性探索，注视数据可为此提供引导；但人类注视包含噪声与冗余，直接迁移到VLM可能效果不佳。因此需要研究如何在保持与人类行为对齐的同时有效利用gaze数据。

Method: 收集7,109条人类注视试次，覆盖多样且具挑战性的视觉推理任务；训练带有不同形式gaze引导的grounded reasoning模型（对比"作为先验/引导"与"完整注视序列监督"）；提出GGR（Gaze-Guided Reasoner），并进一步用强化学习（无gaze监督）优化任务准确率、注视对齐与grounding质量。

Result: 在图表理解、视觉搜索与计数任务上，以人类gaze作为grounded reasoning先验的方案一致优于完整注视序列监督；GGR在更高的行为对齐性与更可靠的视觉grounding下，准确率匹配或超过主流grounded-reasoning基线；强化学习在无gaze监督下仍提升任务准确率、注视对齐和grounding质量；行为分析显示gaze监督模型在给出答案前表现出更多视觉探索。

Conclusion: 人类gaze的价值在于引导空间探索与塑造推理行为，而非要求模型完整模仿人类行为。

Abstract: Human visual reasoning is driven by systematic exploration and extraction of task-relevant visual evidence. Human gaze provides a window into this process, making gaze a useful source of guidance for visually-grounded reasoning in vision-language models (VLMs). However, human gaze also includes noise and redundancies that may transfer poorly to VLMs. In this work, we investigate how human gaze data can be effectively leveraged to improve visual reasoning in VLMs, while maintaining alignment with human behavior. We collect 7,109 human gaze trials from a diverse and challenging set of visual reasoning tasks, and train grounded reasoning models with different forms of gaze guidance. Across evaluations in chart understanding, visual search, and counting, we find that using human gaze as a prior for grounded reasoning consistently outperforms supervision using the full gaze sequence. Based on these findings, we introduce GGR (Gaze-Guided Reasoner), which achieves greater human alignment and more reliable visual grounding while matching or outperforming leading grounded-reasoning baselines in accuracy. Reinforcement learning further improves task accuracy, gaze alignment, and grounding quality despite using no gaze supervision. Behavioral analyses show that gaze supervised models exhibit more visual exploration before arriving at an answer. Our results suggest that the value of human gaze lies in guiding spatial exploration and shaping reasoning behavior without requiring complete imitation of human behavior.

</details>


### [172] [Multimodal Masked Diffusion Autoencoder for Modeling Impaired Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23211)
*John Gideon,Anshul Gupta,Megan Applegate-Kenton,Kayli Battel,Todd J Rowell,Patricio Reyes Gomez,Laporsha Dees,Emily Sumner,Avinash Balachandran,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ID-MAE，一种多模态掩码扩散自编码器，在损伤预测之外加入自监督重建目标（重建被遮蔽的注视与轨迹），并发布 DIVERT 驾驶模拟器数据集（103 小时、n=109、10 种认知/视觉/情绪损伤诱导），在损伤推断与轨迹、注视预测上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知状态（分心、压力）、注视与驾驶行为之间存在细微交互，AI 可用于辅助驾驶；但大规模获取认知状态等级的真实标签代价高昂，因此需要自监督方法降低对标注的依赖。

Method: 提出 ID-MAE：多模态掩码扩散自编码器，将损伤标签视为掩码 token 进行预测，同时联合重建被掩码的注视与轨迹；构建 DIVERT 数据集（103 小时驾驶模拟数据，109 名被试，10 种损伤诱导，采集注视、车辆状态与场景视频）；并对输入模态、掩码策略、位置编码、注意力机制与解码方式进行设计空间探索，分析不同损伤间在训练与推断中的相互作用。

Result: 在位置匹配配对下，DIVERT 上损伤检测的配对 AUC 达 0.706，而无重建损失时为 0.590，优于现有基线；在 DIVERT 与已有数据集上均显著改进损伤推断，并在轨迹预测、注视预测等附加任务上表现良好；视觉需求型与认知型分心可区分。

Conclusion: 将自监督掩码重建与损伤预测联合训练，可有效缓解认知状态标签稀缺问题，提升多模态驾驶场景下的损伤推断与行为预测性能；多模态与损伤类型的消融揭示了不同损伤的可分离性。

Abstract: Human cognitive state (e.g., distraction, stress), gaze, and behavior interact with the driving environment in subtle ways that AI could use to assist drivers, but ground-truth labels for human cognitive state levels are costly to obtain at scale. We propose a multimodal masked-diffusion autoencoder, ID-MAE, that adds a self-supervised objective to impairment prediction: alongside predicting the impairment label as a masked token, it reconstructs masked gaze and trajectory. To probe the effects of different modalities and types of impairments on model inference, we introduce DIVERT, a driving-simulator dataset containing 103 hours of driving data (n = 109) with ten impairment inductions spanning cognitive, visual, and emotional states, recorded with gaze, vehicle state, and scene video. We demonstrate how ID-MAE affords significant improvements in impairment inference results on both the DIVERT dataset and existing datasets, and enables strong results in additional tasks, such as trajectory and gaze prediction. For location-matched pairs, our model achieves a paired AUC of 0.706 for impairment detection on DIVERT, compared to 0.590 without reconstruction loss, improving over existing baselines. We probe the design space in terms of input modalities, masking scheme, positional encoding, attention mechanism, and decoding. We also investigate the interaction between various impairments in training and inference, demonstrating that visually demanding distractions are separable from cognitive distractions. Code and data will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [173] [EviSlide-MIL: Multi-Query Pooling and Shared Prediction for Class-Incremental Whole-Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23212)
*Zhouhan Qian,Sicheng Chen,Chad Wong,Tianyi Zhang,Enhui Chai,Zeyu Liu,Fei Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EviSlide-MIL，一种面向全切片图像（WSI）的类增量学习框架，通过每类多个可学习查询与共享预测模块缓解遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI 分析多采用弱监督下的多示例学习（MIL）。当引入新的肿瘤亚型时，需在仅有有限历史数据、且推理时无任务标识的条件下学习新类并保留旧类知识，即类增量学习问题。特征聚合与预测模块的更新会导致对旧类的遗忘，这是主要障碍。

Method: EviSlide-MIL 为每个类别配备多个可学习查询，并共享一个预测模块；查询将冻结的 patch 特征聚合为类特定表示，共享预测模块抑制增量阶段的参数膨胀。对旧类关联的查询进行冻结，以保证相同输入的注意力模式不变；同时结合经验回放与知识蒸馏，在共享参数更新时进一步缓解遗忘。

Result: 在来自 The Cancer Genome Atlas 的八种肿瘤亚型上，EviSlide-MIL 在多数学习设置下相较对比方法取得更优性能并降低遗忘。

Conclusion: EviSlide-MIL 具备可扩展且知识保持的 WSI 分类潜力；代码与预训练模型将公开。

Abstract: Histopathological analysis of whole-slide images (WSIs) plays a critical role in cancer diagnosis, and multiple instance learning (MIL) is widely adopted for WSI analysis under weak supervision. As new tumor subtypes are introduced, extending existing MIL models requires learning new classes while retaining knowledge of previously learned ones. This presents a class-incremental learning challenge, where models must acquire new classes with limited access to historical training data and recognize all learned classes without task identifiers at inference. A major obstacle is forgetting, as updates to feature aggregation and prediction modules can degrade performance on previously learned classes. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{EviSlide-MIL}, a class-incremental WSI framework that integrates multiple learnable queries per class with a shared prediction module. The queries aggregate frozen patch features into class-specific representations, while the shared prediction module limits parameter growth across incremental stages. By freezing queries associated with previously learned classes, EviSlide-MIL preserves their attention patterns for identical inputs, while experience replay and knowledge distillation further mitigate forgetting as shared parameters are updated. Experiments on eight tumor subtypes from The Cancer Genome Atlas demonstrate that EviSlide-MIL achieves improved performance and reduced forgetting compared with competing methods across most learning settings, demonstrating its potential for scalable and knowledge-preserving WSI classification. Code and pretrained models will be released.

</details>


### [174] [FastFrames: Video Generation at Interactive Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23226)
*Gene Chou,Guandao Yang,Fujun Luan,Lars Mescheder,Wei Dong,Stephan Richter,Vladlen Koltun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastFrames 通过剖析自回归视频扩散模型的推理瓶颈，将 VAE 解码与 KV cache 更新等"被视为固定实现细节"的组件替换为轻量方案，在单卡 B200 上实现 1.3B 模型 36 ms/帧（28 RPS）且质量持平近 8× 慢的 SOTA，14B 模型在 8×B200 节点达 45 ms/帧（22 RPS）并显著提升画质，从而支持实时交互式视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型基于新观测与输入逐帧生成，天然适合交互式视觉体验，但现有模型推理速度比流畅交互所需慢数个数量级；作者希望通过识别推理流水线中被忽视的延迟来源来实现低延迟而不牺牲质量。

Method: 对 SOTA 模型推理流水线进行性能剖析，发现 VAE 解码与 KV cache 更新等常被视为固定实现细节的操作占端到端延迟的一半以上；进而质疑这些组件的不可替代性，用轻量化替代方案替换它们，构建 FastFrames 系列模型（1.3B 与 14B 两个规模）。

Result: 1.3B 变体在单张 B200 上达 36 ms/帧（28 RPS），质量与慢近 8× 的 SOTA 相当；14B 变体在 8×B200 节点上达 45 ms/帧（22 RPS），视觉质量与真实感显著更优；并在相机条件与物理条件视频生成等应用中实现实时交互。

Conclusion: 推理流水线中的"实现细节"级组件是延迟主因，针对其做轻量化替换即可在不牺牲质量的前提下把自回归视频扩散推进到交互级延迟，开启相机/物理条件等实时交互式视频生成应用。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models generate frames based on new observations and inputs, making them a promising medium for interactive visual experiences. However, existing models are orders of magnitude too slow for fluid interaction. This paper introduces FastFrames, a family of fast autoregressive video diffusion models that achieves interactive latency without compromising quality. We profile the inference pipeline of state-of-the-art models and find that operations often treated as fixed implementation details, such as VAE decoding and KV cache updates, in fact account for over half of end-to-end inference latency. We challenge the assumption that these components are indispensable and replace them with lightweight alternatives. Our 1.3B variant runs at 36 ms per frame, or 28 responses per second (RPS), on a single B200 GPU while matching the generation quality of a state-of-the-art model that is nearly 8x slower. Our 14B variant runs at 45 ms per frame, or 22 RPS, on an 8x B200 GPU node and yields significantly better visual quality and realism. We show that FastFrames enables real-time interaction in applications such as camera-conditioned and physics-conditioned video generation.

</details>


### [175] [Spool: Low-Latency Streaming Inference for Hybrid Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23228)
*Rui Ning,Om Chabra,Hari Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spool：无需训练的滑动窗口框架，利用循环层状态更新的仿射性与可结合性，缓存并组合逐帧状态变换，从而精确移除过期帧而无需重放整窗，实现混合VLM流式推理的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉无障碍代理、机器人等应用需在有限历史帧窗口（如最近两分钟）内快速回答视频问题。混合VLM（注意力与Gated DeltaNet等线性循环层交替）适合该场景，但维护滑动窗口代价高：循环层将所有历史压缩为共享状态，淘汰旧帧通常需将整个窗口重新前向计算。

Method: 观察到循环更新对状态是仿射的、系数依赖输入，故每帧影响可表示为可结合复合的变换。Spool缓存逐帧状态变换，随窗口滑动高效复合，从各循环层状态中移除过期帧而不经骨干重放；层内移除精确，但如注意力中的KV逐出，过期帧可能经前层缓存的输入保留残余影响。为此设计专用数据结构与GPU kernel以降低缓存与复合开销。

Result: 在5个开源混合VLM、4个视频推理基准共20组模型-基准测试中，Spool的p95首token时延均最低，相比两个近期vLLM基线中较快者几何平均降低6.6×，同时平均准确率提升2.3个百分点。

Conclusion: Spool以仿射变换复合实现免训练的精确层内窗口淘汰，显著优于现有推理引擎基线，兼顾时延与精度，为混合VLM的流式视频问答提供实用方案。

Abstract: Applications such as visual accessibility agents and robotics require answering questions about recent video content promptly, over a bounded window of past frames (e.g., the last two minutes). Hybrid vision-language models (VLMs), which interleave attention with linear recurrent layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), are attractive for this setting, but maintaining a sliding window is expensive. The reason is that recurrent layers combine all past frames into a shared state, so expiring old frames normally requires re-running the entire window through the model. We observe that the recurrent update is affine in the state with input-dependent coefficients, so the effect of each frame can be captured as a transformation that composes associatively. We present Spool, a training-free framework that caches per-frame state transformations and composes them efficiently as the window advances, removing expired frames from each recurrent layer's state without replaying frames through the backbone. Removal is exact within each layer; as with key-value eviction in attention, expired frames can retain residual influence through the cached inputs computed by earlier layers. We design a dedicated data structure and GPU kernels to achieve low caching and composition overhead. Across five open-source hybrid VLMs and four video reasoning benchmarks, Spool achieves the lowest p95 time-to-first-token in all 20 model-benchmark tests, reducing it by a geometric mean of $6.6\times$ over the faster of two recent vLLM baselines, while improving accuracy by 2.3 percentage points on average.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [CodeReel: Accelerating Code-to-Video Agents with Exact Frame Reuse and Blocking-Aware Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22967)
*Junyi Shen,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: CodeReel：面向编码智能体视频生成的无训练执行层，通过精确帧复用与阻塞感知调度将智能体等待时间降低36.3%（对比Rebuild）和57.9%（对比Sequential）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体通过"编写-检查-编辑"循环制作视频，一部成品影片中位数耗时11分钟、花费$2.85模型调用，其中超过三分之一时间阻塞在构建上。作者发现两类可避免的浪费：(1) 会话后续影片中37.5%的帧此前已绘制过，只是时间位置偏移或位于被编辑的场景内；(2) 一半构建停在检查环节，智能体只需判定结果，但哪些构建会停在检查在检查完成前未知。

Method: 提出CodeReel，作为智能体现有命令背后的免训练执行层，含两个机制：(1) 精确帧复用——以绘制命令作为帧的键，与时间位置无关，键重复即复用；(2) 阻塞感知调度——先运行检查并同时以空闲优先级启动渲染，除非会话近期构建使通过可能性低。精确键允许后续版本复用为失败版本渲染的帧。

Result: 构建EditLoop基准（可重放智能体会话，影片对照从头渲染校验）。在EditLoop上，等待时间较Rebuild（并行渲染的预热流水线）降低36.3%，较Sequential降低57.9%。时间偏移编辑下复用渲染43.9%，接近像素真正更新比例43.0%，而Manim式缓存为99.8%。真实智能体中，Claude Code上每次构建中位等待为Rebuild的0.62倍（未用于开发的主题），Codex CLI为0.67倍；结论在5个模型、2个框架下成立。

Conclusion: 复用与调度互补：调度消耗CPU以更快给出答案，复用则将CPU返还；精确键使跨版本、跨失败的帧复用成为可能。CodeReel在不训练模型的前提下显著缩短编码智能体视频制作中的阻塞等待，且具备跨模型与框架的泛化性。

Abstract: Coding agents make videos by writing and refining programs in a build--inspect--edit loop. A finished film costs a median of $2.85 in model calls and 11 minutes, over a third of that time in builds that block the agent. We find that much of this time is avoidable, for two reasons. First, 37.5% of the frames of a session's later films were drawn before, many shifted in time or inside an edited scene. Second, half of the builds stop at a check, where the agent needs only the verdict, but which builds stop is unknown until the checks finish. We present CodeReel, a training-free execution layer behind agents' existing commands. Exact frame reuse keys each frame by its drawing commands, regardless of its position in time, and reuses each frame whose key recurs. Blocking-aware scheduling runs the checks first and starts the render alongside them, at idle priority, unless the session's recent builds make a pass unlikely. Scheduling spends CPU to answer sooner and reuse returns it; exact keys let later versions use frames rendered for a failed one as the agent thinks. We introduce EditLoop, a benchmark of replayable agent sessions whose films are checked against renders from scratch. On it, CodeReel cuts the agent's wait by 36.3% against Rebuild, a warm pipeline with parallel rendering, and by 57.9% against Sequential, today's step-by-step pipeline. On time-shifting edits its reuse renders 43.9%, close to the 43.0% whose pixels are new, while Manim-style caching renders 99.8%. With real agents, the median wait per build is 0.62x Rebuild's in Claude Code, on topics never used in development, and 0.67x in Codex CLI. The gain holds for five models and two frameworks.

</details>
